# Project Requirements: aTrainingTracker

This document tracks all functional and non-functional requirements of the project. Every code change must be traceable to a requirement defined here.

## 1. Core Connectivity & Hardware Integration

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-CON-001** | Support multiple ANT+ sensor profiles (HR, Speed, Cadence, Power, Env). | Provide comprehensive data for diverse athletic activities. | `BANALService.java`, `ant_plus/` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-002** | Support Bluetooth LE (BLE) sensor profiles (HR, Speed, Cadence, Power). | Ensure compatibility with modern fitness hardware. | `BANALService.java`, `bluetooth_le/` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-003** | Implement "Best Sensor" selection logic using priority lists. | Maximize data quality by preferring hardware over GPS. | `MySensorManager.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-004** | Provide asynchronous background searching for devices. | Allow users to discover sensors without interrupting app use. | `DeviceManager.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-005** | All BLE characteristic reads must be null-safe. | Prevent application crashes when communicating with hardware. | `MyBTLEDevice.java` | `TST-STR-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-006** | Support barometric pressure sensors for altitude. | Provide precise elevation data where GPS is insufficient. | `AltitudeFromPressureDevice.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-007** | Notify user about low battery levels in sensors. | Prevent data loss during workouts due to sensor failure. | `BatteryStatusHelper.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-008** | Detect missing ANT+ services and link to Play Store. | Ensure users can easily set up required hardware drivers. | `BANALService.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-009** | Support per-device calibration (wheel circumference). | Ensure distance and speed accuracy across setups. | `EditSimpleBikeDeviceFragment.kt`| `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |

## 2. Sensor Data Processing & Filtering

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-FIL-001** | Flexible filtering (Moving Avg, Exponential Smoothing). | Allow for smooth visualization of noisy sensor data. | `FilterManager.java` | `TST-UNT-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-FIL-002** | Configurable smoothing factors (α) for Power. | Balance responsiveness vs. stability. | `ExponentialSmoothingFilter.java` | `TST-UNT-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-FIL-003** | Calculate derived metrics (VAM, slope, pace). | Provide advanced metrics for climbing and analysis. | `VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDevice.java` | `TST-UNT-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-FIL-004** | **Atomic Altitude Correction.** | The system SHALL apply altitude corrections from known locations atomically across all data structures. This MUST include shifting previous sample points, updating summary extrema (Min/Max), and recalculating total ascent/descent for the active workout to ensure 100% data consistency. | `TrackerService.java`, `KnownLocationsDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-FIL-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-FIL-005** | Implement "Proxy Sensors" and "Accumulator Sensors". | Extensible architecture for complex multi-sensor data. | `ProxySensor.java` | `TST-STR-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-FIL-006** | Geographical rolling averages for start altitude. | Filter out momentary GPS or barometric jitter. | `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java`| `TST-STR-001` | Verified |

## 3. Tracking Logic & Session Management

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-TRK-001** | Support Start, Stop, Pause with Service persistence. | Ensure data integrity in the background. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-REG-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-002** | **Workout Lap Recording Lifecycle, Manual Splitting & Pause/Resume Neutrality.** | The system SHALL manage the lifecycle of workout laps deterministically during recording, tracking termination, and database retrieval, preventing the creation and presentation of zero-duration phantom laps:<br>1. *Pause and Resume Neutrality*: Transitioning active tracking into a paused state (`pauseTracking()`) or resuming tracking from paused (`resumeFromPaused()`) SHALL NOT broadcast `REQUEST_NEW_LAP` and SHALL NOT create, increment, or close workout laps. Active lap duration and distance accumulation SHALL pause and resume transparently with the session.<br>2. *Manual Lap Splitting Integrity*: Explicit lap requests initiated by the user (via `TrackingViewsRepository.requestNewLap()` in the tracking UI or `BUTTON_LAP` from a connected smartwatch) SHALL finalize the active lap, persist its split metrics to `Laps.db` (`saveLap(...)`), broadcast `BANALService.LAP_SUMMARY`, and initialize the subsequent sequential lap index ($N+1$).<br>3. *Workout Finalization & Termination Guards*: Upon tracking completion (`TrackerService.endWorkout()`), `createNewLap()` SHALL finalize and persist the active in-progress lap if and only if `lapTime_s > 0` or `lapDistance > 0.0`. If the active lap has zero duration and zero distance, the system SHALL discard it and MUST NOT persist it to `Laps.db`.<br>4. *Defensive Database & Receiver Validation*: `TrackerService.mLapSummaryReceiver` and `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap(...)` SHALL reject any lap record where `timeTotal <= 0 && distanceTotal <= 0.0`, ensuring invalid or empty splits are never written to SQLite.<br>5. *Historical & Vectorized Query Sanitization*: In `LapsDatabaseManager.getLaps(workoutId)` and `getLapsForWorkouts(workoutIds)`, queries SHALL filter out legacy zero-duration records (`TIME_TOTAL_s > 0 OR DISTANCE_TOTAL_m > 0`), ensuring corrupted historical splits do not render in workout summaries (`WorkoutLaps.kt`) or export files (`TCXFileWriter.java`, `GPXFileWriter.java`).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an active live tracking session with no manual lap presses,<br>• *When* the user pauses tracking, resumes tracking, pauses again, and ends the workout,<br>• *Then* `Laps.db` SHALL contain exactly 1 lap record whose duration and distance match the total active workout metrics, with zero 00:00:00 phantom laps.<br>• *Given* an active live tracking session where the user explicitly taps "New Lap" once at minute 10 and ends the workout at minute 25,<br>• *Then* `Laps.db` SHALL contain exactly 2 lap records (Lap 1 = 10 min, Lap 2 = 15 min), and zero zero-duration laps.<br>• *Given* an attempt to invoke `LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap()` with `lapTime = 0` and `lapDistance = 0.0`,<br>• *Then* the method SHALL discard the write and log a warning without inserting into `Laps.TABLE`.<br>• *Given* a database containing a historical zero-duration lap from prior app versions,<br>• *When* `LapsDatabaseManager.getLaps(workoutId)` is queried,<br>• *Then* the zero-duration lap SHALL be excluded from the returned list.<br><br>**Invariants**: Manual lap splitting (`TrackingViewsRepository`, `PebbleService`), total active time/distance calculation (`REQ-TRK-001`), live pause duration updates (`REQ-UI-038`), and TCX/GPX export schema compliance (`REQ-DAT-012`) MUST NOT be broken. | Eliminate confusing phantom zero-duration laps from workout summaries, preserve Rabbit/Hedgehog performance badge integrity, and ensure clean TCX exports by aligning pause/resume behavior with standard athletic tracking. | `TrainingApplication.java`, `TrackerService.java`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-TRK-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-003** | Use WakeLocks during active tracking. | Prevent CPU sleep and data gaps. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-REG-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-004** | Automatically guess Sport Type and Equipment. | Minimize user effort via automated categorization. | `EquipmentDiscoveryManager.kt` | `TST-UNT-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-005** | Implement session recovery ("Resume from Crash"). | Ensure data integrity against system pressure. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-STR-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-006** | Support sport-specific search triggers. | Sensors ready when user changes activity. | `TrainingApplication.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-007** | Deterministic 1Hz sampling rate during recording. | Consistent time-base for analysis and export. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-REG-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-008** | Align session averages with core summary metrics. | Ensure background tracking focus matches optimized UI summary for consistency and performance. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-STR-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-009** | Total Time Persistence. | The `TrackerService` SHALL continue to update the `TIME_TOTAL_s` field in the `WorkoutSummaries` database table even when the recording is in a PAUSED state. | `TrackerService.java` | `TST-REG-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-TRK-010** | **Live Tracking Finalization Event Dispatch.** | Upon tracking completion (`endWorkout`), the system SHALL broadcast `WORKOUT_UPDATED_INTENT` and `TRACKING_FINISHED_INTENT` with the workout ID parameter (`WORKOUT_ID`) via `LocalBroadcastManager`, while preserving the package-targeted system broadcast of `TRACKING_FINISHED_INTENT`. The system SHALL ensure downstream subscribers (`WorkoutRepository`, `PeriodsRepository`, `WorkoutClusterEngine`) consume the finalized session to reactively create and update period summaries (Day, Week, Month, Year).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* an active live tracking session,<br>• *When* the workout is finished,<br>• *Then* `LocalBroadcastManager` SHALL dispatch `WORKOUT_UPDATED_INTENT` with extra `WORKOUT_ID` = `mWorkoutID`, triggering atomic rollup in `PeriodsRepository`.<br>**Invariants**: Existing intent action names and extras MUST NOT be changed; system-level broadcast receivers (`TrainingApplication`, `MainActivityWithNavigation`) MUST NOT be disrupted. | Ensure live-tracked workouts immediately generate and update period summaries, matching the reactive behavior of TCX imports. | `TrackerService.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `PeriodsRepository.kt` | `TST-PER-016` | Verified |

## 4. Advanced Metrics & User Profiles

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-SET-001** | HR and Power training zones (5-zone model). | Allow athletes to train within specific intensities. | `ZonesSettingsActivity.kt` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-002** | Equipment management (Bikes/Shoes) with odometer. | Help users monitor gear wear and tear. | `EquipmentRepository.kt` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-003** | Configurable speed thresholds for classification. | Tailor automation to user's specific pace profile. | `TrainingApplication.java` | `TST-UNT-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-004** | "Fancy Name" schemes using location and detours. | Provide descriptive workout names automatically. | `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`| `TST-STR-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-050** | **Navigation Drawer Structure.** | The navigation drawer SHALL be organized into logical hubs: Training, Spatial, Equipment, Ecosystem, and System. Key management features (Sport Types, Training Zones, Strava, Dropbox, Export) SHALL be individual first-class items in the drawer. Technical application settings (Units, Search, Display, Tracking Tabs) SHALL also be accessible as individual drawer items. | `main_navigation_drawer.xml`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.java`, `ui/settings/` | `TST-NAV-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-055** | **Modular Settings Architecture.** | The system's settings configuration SHALL follow a modular architectural pattern. All settings-related UI components SHALL reside within dedicated functional sub-packages under `ui.settings` (e.g., `display`, `units`, `export`). The system SHALL NOT contain redundant preference fragments or legacy XML configurations that are no longer active in the primary navigation flow. | `ui/settings/`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.java` | `TST-INT-008` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-051** | **Direct Tracking Tab Selection.** | The system SHALL provide a modal interface for Activity Type selection accessible directly from the navigation drawer. Upon selection, the system SHALL launch the dedicated configuration cockpit for the chosen Activity Type. | `MainActivityWithNavigation.java`, `ActivityTypeSelectionHelper.kt` | `TST-NAV-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-052** | **Modal Display Settings & Synchronized Preference State.** | The system SHALL provide direct access to display-related preferences (`forcePortrait`, `keepScreenOn`, `noUnlocking`) via a modal dialog (`DisplaySettingsDialog`) triggered from the navigation drawer (`DisplaySettingsDialogFragment`):<br>1. *Default State Synchronization*: The display settings dialog MUST initialize switch states using `TrainingApplication.DEFAULT_DISPLAY_OPTIONS` (with `forcePortrait`, `keepScreenOn`, and `noUnlocking` all enabled) whenever the preference key `pref_display_options` is not explicitly persisted in `SharedPreferences`. The visual switch state in the dialog MUST match the runtime evaluation of `TrainingApplication.forcePortrait()`, `TrainingApplication.keepScreenOn()`, and `TrainingApplication.NoUnlocking()`.<br>2. *Atomic State Hoisting*: The dialog state MUST be hoisted at the dialog root to ensure toggling any display switch atomically updates the option set and persists it to `SharedPreferences` without dropping or reverting toggles made to sibling switches.<br>3. *Bidirectional Dynamic Window & Orientation Application*: When display options are modified or the dialog is dismissed, the host activity (`MainActivityWithNavigation`) SHALL immediately and dynamically apply the settings:<br>• `keepScreenOn`: Sets `window.decorView.keepScreenOn` to the matching boolean value.<br>• `noUnlocking`: When enabled, adds `WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SHOW_WHEN_LOCKED`; when disabled, explicitly clears `FLAG_SHOW_WHEN_LOCKED`.<br>• `forcePortrait`: When enabled, sets `requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT`; when disabled, explicitly resets `requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED`.<br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a fresh install or an environment where `pref_display_options` has not been modified,<br>• *When* the user opens the Display Settings dialog from the navigation drawer,<br>• *Then* all three switches ("force portrait", "keep screen on", "keep screen unlocked") SHALL be displayed in the ON (checked) state.<br>• *Given* the Display Settings dialog,<br>• *When* the user toggles "keep screen on" to OFF and then toggles "keep screen unlocked" to OFF,<br>• *Then* both settings SHALL remain OFF, and SharedPreferences SHALL contain only `["forcePortrait"]`.<br>• *When* the user disables "force portrait",<br>• *Then* the activity orientation lock SHALL be released (`SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED`) dynamically without requiring an activity restart.<br>**Invariants**: The SharedPreferences key `pref_display_options`, option string literals (`forcePortrait`, `keepScreenOn`, `noUnlocking`), and backward-compatible reading of existing user preferences MUST NOT change. | `DisplaySettingsDialog.kt`, `DisplaySettingsDialogFragment.kt`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java` | `TST-NAV-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-053** | **Modal Units Selection.** | The system SHALL provide direct access to global unit system settings (Metric/Imperial) via a modal dialog triggered from the navigation drawer. | `MainActivityWithNavigation.java`, `UnitsSettingsDialogFragment.kt` | `TST-NAV-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-054** | **Modal Export Configuration.** | The system SHALL provide direct access to file export preferences (TCX, GPX, etc.) via a modal dialog triggered from the navigation drawer. | `MainActivityWithNavigation.java`, `ExportSettingsDialogFragment.kt` | `TST-NAV-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-005** | **Global Unit Support (Metric/Imperial).** | The system SHALL support both Metric and Imperial unit systems globally across all modules (Tracking, Aftermath, Periods, WorkoutClusters, and Settings). All user-facing values, entry fields, and visualizations SHALL adapt dynamically to the user's unit preference. | `MyUnits.java`, `MyHelper.java` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-006** | **Agnostic Workout Clustering.** | The system SHALL automatically group similar tracks into \"Workout Clusters\" based on spatial fingerprints (Start, End, Apex, Distance) regardless of the initial sport type. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-007** | **Majority-Based Sport Inference.** | The system SHALL determine the most likely Name and Sport for a Workout Cluster based on the majority of associated workouts. Manual confirms/overrides SHALL take precedence in the learning knowledge base. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-SET-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-008** | **Learning Feedback Loop.** | The system SHALL refine the cluster centroids and update probable sport associations whenever a user overrides or confirms a suggestion. The cluster's name SHALL ONLY be updated if it currently has a generic/default name (e.g., 'Cluster at...'). | `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-009** | **Favorite Tracks Heatmap.** | The system SHALL provide a visualization for Workout Clusters that overlays all associated workout tracks as a heatmap, along with the spatial fingerprint markers. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `MapContentScope.kt` | `TST-SET-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-010** | **Tuning Parameters UI.** | The system SHALL provide a UI to edit the tuning parameters for the Workout Cluster Engine, featuring a primary \"Strict-to-Relaxed\" master slider, optional detailed controls for individual tolerances (Endpoint, Apex, Distance), and a toggle to optionally include Sport Type in similarity calculations. | `ClusterTuningScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-003`, `TST-SET-034` | Proposed |
| **REQ-SET-011** | **Clustering Recalculation.** | Upon modification of tuning parameters, the system SHALL allow the user to trigger a full recalculation of Workout Clusters across the entire workout history. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-012** | **Workout Cluster List Visualization.** | The system SHALL display a Workout Cluster list using a layout harmonized with the Workout and Route lists. Each item SHALL feature a full-width header (BSportType icon and Name) positioned at the top. Secondary metadata and metrics SHALL be displayed below the header, alongside a right-aligned map preview of the spatial signature. The Cluster Detail Heatmap SHALL also utilize a similar header layout to maximize visual consistency across the analytical suite. | `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-013** | **Name-Aware Clustering.** | The system SHALL prioritize existing identical workout names when matching routes to clusters to improve grouping accuracy for user-named paths. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-014** | **Auto-Dismiss Tuning UI.** | The system SHALL automatically return to the cluster list view once a recalculation process is successfully completed. | `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-015** | **Unique Cluster Naming.** | The system SHALL ensure that every workout cluster has a unique name by appending a variant counter (e.g., var 2) if a name collision occurs for spatially distinct routes. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt` | `TST-SET-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-016** | **Edit Cluster Identity.** | The system SHALL allow the user to manually rename a workout cluster and change its primary sport type via a dedicated edit dialog in the cluster's detail view. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-008` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-017** | **Manual Workout Cluster Reassignment.** | The system SHALL allow the user to manually reassign a workout to a different workout cluster via the heatmap view. Tapping a workout track SHALL show a list of candidate clusters sorted by similarity score. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-009` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-018** | **Cluster Suggestions in Edit Workout.** | The system SHALL provide a list of spatially similar cluster candidates when editing a workout's name. Selecting a candidate SHALL automatically update the workout's Name, Sport, and Cluster association. | `EditWorkoutScreen.kt`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-010`, `TST-SET-046` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-019** | **Manual Cluster Creation.** | The system SHALL allow the user to manually create a new workout cluster by specifying its spatial fingerprint (Start, End, Apex) and distance metrics via a dedicated UI. | `ManualClusterScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-011` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-020** | **Workout Peek in Heatmap.** | The system SHALL display a detailed peek (Bottom Sheet) when a workout track is tapped in the cluster heatmap, similar to the Periods view. This peek SHALL include an option to reassign the workout to a different cluster. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-012` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-021** | **Manual Fingerprint Editing.** | The system SHALL allow the user to manually adjust the signature points (Start, End, Apex) of an existing workout cluster via the heatmap detail view. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-013` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-022** | **Workout Markers in Cluster Detail.** | When viewing a workout cluster heatmap, the system SHALL display the spatial signature markers (Start, Stop, Apex) for EVERY member workout of the cluster to visualize spatial distribution. Additionally, it SHALL display all individual markers (Start, Stop, Extrema) for a specific workout when peeked. Clicking on any marker SHALL trigger the workout peek functionality. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-014` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-023** | **Global Workout Unit Support.** | The system SHALL support both Metric and Imperial units in the WorkoutCluster management UI. Distance entry and tuning visualization MUST use high-level units (km/mile) with appropriate precision to ensure professional-grade spatial definition. | `ManualClusterScreen.kt`, `ClusterTuningScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-015` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-024** | **Cluster Equipment Determination.** | The system SHALL dynamically determine and display the equipment associated with a Workout Cluster's majority sport. This SHALL use the technical mapping notation (→) consistent with the sensor management UI. | `WorkoutClustersListScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-016` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-031** | **Route-to-Cluster Synchronization.** | Every explicit Route in the system (imported or created) SHALL have a corresponding Workout Cluster. Renaming a route SHALL automatically update the corresponding Workout Cluster name to ensure naming consistency across the spatial knowledge base. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `RoutesRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-022` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-033** | **Workout Cluster Deletion.** | The system SHALL allow the user to delete a Workout Cluster via a long-click context menu on its detail header. Deleting a cluster SHALL nullify all associations with workouts and routes to maintain data integrity, while preserving the actual workout recordings. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-024` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-032** | **Reference Route Visualization.** | When a Workout Cluster is linked to an explicit Route, the system SHALL display that route's path as a prominent reference layer on the detailed heatmap view to provide an authoritative spatial baseline. | `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-023` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-061** | **Idempotent Route Cluster Creation.** | When learning a cluster from a workout with a specified route name, the system SHALL first search for an existing cluster with the exact normalized name before creating a new cluster. If a cluster with the same name exists, the system SHALL refine the existing cluster rather than creating a duplicate cluster appended with "var 2". **Invariants**: Spatial anchors (start/end/apex/bounds) MUST be updated when matching by name. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt` | `TST-SET-047` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-062** | **Automatic Zero-Workout Cluster Purging.** | The system SHALL automatically delete orphan route clusters that have zero actual workouts assigned in the database during repository refresh and post-import self-healing. **Invariants**: Clusters linked to an explicit Route (imported or created) MUST NOT be deleted even when they contain zero workouts. Clusters with 1 or more assigned workouts MUST NOT be deleted. | `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt`, `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt` | `TST-SET-048` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-025** | **Workout Cluster Collapsing Header.** | The Workout Cluster list SHALL feature a collapsing top app bar that integrates with the list's scroll behavior, providing a modern and consistent navigation experience similar to the Workouts and Routes screens. | `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-017` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-027** | **Persistent Scroll State.** | The system SHALL preserve the scroll position and active tab of the Workout Cluster list when navigating back from a detail heatmap view. | `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt`, `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-019` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-026** | **Workout Cluster Tabbed Layout.** | The Workout Cluster list SHALL feature a tabbed layout based on `BSportType` (All, Bike, Run, Other), matching the design pattern of Workouts, Segments, and Routes. | `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt` | `TST-SET-018` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-028** | **Unified Action Button Layout.** | The "Add Cluster" Floating Action Button SHALL use a standardized layout (manual Box alignment and navigationBarsPadding) consistent with the Sport Type and Equipment management screens. | `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-020` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-029** | **Storage-Layer Identity Determination.** | To ensure data consistency and prevent UI race conditions, the system SHALL determine a workout's Workout Cluster association and Auto-Name at the storage layer (immediately after tracking ends) rather than during UI mapping. | `TrackerService.java`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-021` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-030** | **Sensor-First Identity Arbitration.** | The system SHALL implement a confidence-based arbitration model to resolve conflicts between active hardware and route history. High-confidence sensor signals (e.g., uniquely linked hardware) SHALL veto route-based sport/gear overrides, while low-confidence sessions (GPS-only) SHALL be corrected by the Workout Cluster majority. | `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-021` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-034** | **Stable Cluster Naming.** | The system SHALL maintain the base name of a Workout Cluster when assigning it to a workout. The auto-generated workout name (including a hit counter) MUST NOT be propagated back to the cluster's permanent name. Manual session renames in the Edit Workout screen SHALL NOT rename the linked cluster. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-025`, `TST-SET-042` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-037** | **Atomic Cluster Linking.** | When a workout is assigned to a Workout Cluster, the system SHALL atomically update the workout's cluster association and increment the cluster's hit count. If reassigned from another cluster, the source cluster's count SHALL be decremented but the cluster MUST NOT be deleted automatically. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-028` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-038** | **Self-Healing Hit Counts.** | The system SHALL ensure that Workout Cluster hit counts are always accurate by either calculating them on-the-fly from the workout summary database or by performing a periodic integrity check that synchronizes the redundant `hit_count` column with the actual number of associated sessions. The UI selection dialog MUST display these accurate counts. | `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt`, `WorkoutClusterSelectionDialog` | `TST-SET-044` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-035** | **Preview Heatmap.** | The Workout Cluster list item SHALL display a representative spatial preview on its mini-map, consisting of the linked Route path (if available) or the 5 most recent workout tracks rendered as a low-opacity heatmap. | `WorkoutClusterComponents.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-026` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-036** | **Persistent DB Connection.** | The Workout Cluster database SHALL remain open during the application lifecycle to facilitate real-time debugging and inspection via external tools. | `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt` | `TST-SET-027` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-039** | **Locale-Aware Distance Input.** | The manual cluster creation interface SHALL support locale-specific decimal separators (e.g., comma in DE, dot in EN) when entering workout distances. | `ManualClusterScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-030` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-040** | **Unclustered Workout Access.** | The system SHALL provide a dedicated view for workouts that are not associated with any Workout Cluster to facilitate manual organization and re-clustering. | `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-SET-029` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-056** | **Unclustered Workout Visualization.** | Unclustered workouts SHALL be displayed in the cluster management UI using a layout harmonized with `ClusterItem`, including a spatial mini-map preview. Clicking an unclustered workout SHALL navigate the user to a detailed map view of that workout, featuring a direct option to manually assign it to a cluster. | `WorkoutClusterComponents.kt`, `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt`, `TrackOnMapScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-040` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-057** | **Cluster Member Listing.** | The system SHALL allow the user to view all workouts associated with a specific Workout Cluster. | **REQ-SET-063** | **Workout Cluster Apex Arithmetic Mean & Live-Session Tracking Synchronization.** | The system SHALL ensure that a Workout Cluster's Max Line Distance (Apex) signature marker (`maxDispLat, maxDispLng`) strictly represents the running arithmetic mean of the maximum displacement points (`maxDisplacementLatLng`) across all member workouts in the cluster, preserving the centroid across member additions, deletions, and reassignments. The cluster's apex SHALL NOT be snapped or overwritten to arbitrary individual track coordinates during UI inspection or linked route resolution. Furthermore, when a live workout finishes, `WorkoutClusterEngine.onWorkoutFinished()` SHALL pass the workout's actual `maxDisplacementLatLng` (with fallback to `endLatLng`) as the apex rather than `startLatLng`. Additionally, `SpeedAndLocationDevice` SHALL reset `mStartLocation` upon accumulator reset so that live displacement tracking is anchored to the workout's actual start location. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt`, `SpeedAndLocationDevice.java` | `TST-SET-049` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-064** | **Speed-Based Multi-Sport Route Clustering & Ambiguity Disambiguation in Tracker and Workout Editor.** | The system SHALL evaluate cluster similarity against all candidate sport types supported by a workout session's speed profile both during live tracking completion (`TrackerService`) AND in the workout editor (`EditWorkoutViewModel.kt` / `EditWorkoutScreen.kt`), ensuring cost minimization without artificial mismatch penalties:<br>1. *Pre-Selection & Hardware Sovereignty*:<br>• If the user explicitly pre-selected a sport type prior to tracking and it is not `BSportType.UNKNOWN`, candidate sports SHALL be restricted strictly to `{userSelectedSport}`.<br>• If high-confidence hardware sensors (e.g., bike cadence, power meter, smart trainer) are active (`identity.isHighConfidence`), candidate sports SHALL be restricted strictly to `{hardwareSport}` (`REQ-SET-030`).<br>2. *Speed-Based Multi-Sport Candidate Set & Overlapping Sport Types*:<br>• In the absence of high-confidence hardware and explicit user pre-selection, the system SHALL query `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager` to retrieve all candidate `BSportType`s matching the session's average speed.<br>• When custom sport types have overlapping speed ranges with built-in sports (e.g., custom sport `Einkaufen` configured at 7–15 km/h with base sport `BIKE` overlapping `Running` at 7.2–14.4 km/h with base sport `RUN`), ALL matching base sports SHALL be included in the candidate sports set (`{RUN, BIKE}`).<br>• In `SportTypeDatabaseManager`, queries with `BSportType.UNKNOWN` SHALL retrieve all speed-matching sports regardless of `BASE_SPORT_TYPE`, ensuring that walking speeds or low-speed commute stops do not block candidate retrieval.<br>3. *Candidate-Aware Similarity Evaluation & Cost Minimization*:<br>• `WorkoutClusterEngine.suggestCluster()` and `calculateSimilarity()` SHALL accept the candidate sport set. If an existing cluster's `bSportType` matches ANY sport in the candidate set, the sport similarity penalty SHALL be 0.0, allowing geometrically matching clusters to pass the similarity threshold (< 1.0).<br>• In `EditWorkoutViewModel.fetchClusterSuggestions()`, the system SHALL derive candidate sports from `workout.avgSpeedMps` and invoke `getClusterScores(..., candidateSportTypes = candidateSports)`. Both matching sport clusters SHALL receive 0.0 sport penalty, and the cluster with the lower geometric similarity score (cost) SHALL be ranked first.<br>• In `EditWorkoutViewModel.updateSuggestedSportTypeNames()`, when the workout's base sport is `UNKNOWN` or unconstrained, the suggested sports dropdown SHALL include all sports matching the speed profile across all candidate base sports (e.g., including `Einkaufen`).<br>• If a cluster's `bSportType` is outside the candidate set, standard sport penalties (+2.0 for UNKNOWN, +5.0 for mismatched sport) SHALL be applied.<br>4. *Disambiguation & Identity Adoption*:<br>• When an ambiguous workout matches an existing cluster, the workout SHALL adopt the cluster's `bSportType`, route name, and associated equipment.<br>• If multiple existing clusters across different candidate sports match geometrically, the candidate cluster with the lowest overall similarity score (minimal cost) SHALL win tie-breaking.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* an existing Cycling cluster on a route and a newly recorded workout with average speed ~10 km/h without sensors or pre-selection where both `Running` and custom sport `Einkaufen` match the speed,<br>• *When* tracking ends or when viewing `EditWorkoutScreen`,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutClusterEngine` SHALL evaluate both Running and Cycling as candidate sports, apply 0.0 sport penalty for both clusters, and select/rank the cluster with the lowest geometric similarity cost.<br>• *Given* an explicit user pre-selection of Running on the same route,<br>• *When* evaluated,<br>• *Then* the Cycling cluster SHALL receive a +5.0 penalty and SHALL NOT be matched.<br>• *Given* an active cycling cadence sensor,<br>• *When* evaluated,<br>• *Then* candidate sports SHALL be strictly `{BIKE}`.<br>**Invariants**: Dedicated hardware sensor sovereignty (`REQ-SET-030`) MUST be preserved; existing clusters with single-sport matching MUST NOT be degraded; manual cluster reassignment workflows MUST remain unchanged. | Eliminate false duplicate cluster creation and inaccurate sport guessing after tracking and inside the workout editor on recurring routes when intermediate speeds or overlapping custom sports allow multiple plausible sport types. | `TrackerService.java`, `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`, `SportTypeDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-SET-051`, `TST-SET-056` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-065** | **Altitude Extrema-Aware 3D Route Clustering & Database Migration.** | The system SHALL take the geographic coordinates of the minimum altitude point (`minAltitudePos`) and maximum altitude point (`maxAltitudePos`) into account when evaluating route cluster similarity and managing cluster fingerprints:<br>1. *Default State & Preference Toggle*: The system SHALL provide a user preference `use_altitude_pos_for_clustering` (`TrainingApplication.useAltitudePosForClustering()`) enabled by default (`true`).<br>2. *Dedicated Tolerance Control*: The system SHALL provide a dedicated `altitudePositionTolerance` slider in `ClusterTuningScreen.kt` under Detailed Controls (range: 10m–500m, default: 400m).<br>3. *Balanced 6-Term Similarity Formulation*: When enabled and altitude coordinates are present on both the workout and the cluster candidate, `WorkoutClusterEngine.calculateSimilarity()` SHALL calculate similarity using a balanced 6-term formula:<br>• Start Point: weight 0.20<br>• End Point: weight 0.20<br>• Apex (Max Line Distance): weight 0.20<br>• Total Distance: weight 0.20<br>• Min Altitude Position: weight 0.10<br>• Max Altitude Position: weight 0.10<br>(Sum of weights = 1.0).<br>4. *Graceful Fallback & Invariants*: If altitude position coordinates are absent on either the workout or the cluster candidate (e.g., flat routes, indoor sessions, or sensorless recordings), or if the preference is disabled, the system SHALL gracefully fall back to the standard 4-term spatial scoring (Start, End, Apex, Distance with weight 0.25 each) with zero penalty.<br>5. *TCX Import Synchronization*: During TCX import (`LegacyImportEngine.kt` and `ImportEngine.kt`), the parser SHALL track the trackpoint coordinates of lowest and highest elevation, persist them to `TABLE_EXTREMA_VALUES` under `SensorType.ALTITUDE` for `MIN` and `MAX`, and pass them to `suggestCluster()`.<br>6. *Database Schema & Historical Backfill Migration*:<br>• The `RouteClusters.db` schema SHALL be upgraded from Version 9 to 10 by adding nullable columns: `min_alt_lat REAL`, `min_alt_lng REAL`, `max_alt_lat REAL`, `max_alt_lng REAL`.<br>• During migration, the system SHALL backfill existing clusters by computing the mean minimum and maximum altitude coordinates across their associated member workouts from `WorkoutSummaries` and `TABLE_EXTREMA_VALUES`.<br>7. *Detail Map Visualization & Interactive Fingerprint Relocation*:<br>• `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` SHALL display Min Altitude and Max Altitude markers on the map, with independent visibility toggles in the marker options dropdown.<br>• In fingerprint editing mode (`isEditingFingerprint = true`), both Min Altitude and Max Altitude markers SHALL be draggable, allowing the user to manually reposition the altitude extrema anchors and persist the changes (`REQ-SET-021`).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* two routes sharing identical Start, End, Apex, and Distance, but having distinct Min/Max altitude locations (> 400m apart),<br>• *When* cluster similarity is evaluated with altitude position clustering enabled,<br>• *Then* the altitude displacement terms SHALL push the similarity score $\ge 1.0$, preventing false cluster merging.<br>• *Given* a workout or cluster lacking altitude data,<br>• *When* evaluated,<br>• *Then* the system SHALL fall back to 4-term scoring without penalty.<br>• *Given* an existing database at version 9,<br>• *When* the app upgrades to version 10,<br>• *Then* existing clusters SHALL be backfilled with member workout altitude centroids.<br>**Invariants**: Existing 2D cluster geometries and non-elevation routes MUST NOT fail matching; user-selected sport and equipment rules MUST remain untouched. | Prevent false cluster merging in hilly and alpine terrain where 2D horizontal fingerprints cannot distinguish ridge from valley routes, and provide complete 3D cluster fingerprint lifecycle management (editing, tuning, visualization, import). | `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `ClusterTuningScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `ImportEngine.kt` | `TST-SET-052`, `TST-UNT-015` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-066** | **Workout Cluster Sequential Auto-Naming Counter Ergonomics.** | The system SHALL format automated workout names derived from Workout Clusters (`WorkoutCluster`) such that the initial workout session of a cluster receives only the cluster's base name without any sequential number suffix, while subsequent sessions receive an incrementing numerical suffix (`#<count>`):<br>1. *First Workout Session Naming (`hitCount <= 1`)*:<br>When a workout is assigned to a cluster where the resulting hit count is 1 (the initial session in that cluster), the system SHALL set the workout's name to the exact cluster name (`cluster.name`) without appending `"#1"`.<br>2. *Subsequent Workout Sessions Naming (`hitCount >= 2`)*:<br>When a subsequent workout matches or is assigned to a cluster resulting in a hit count of 2 or greater, the system SHALL format the workout name using `@string/cluster_autoname_format` (`"%1$s #%2$d"`), appending `"#<count>"` (e.g., `"Morning Run #2"`, `"Morning Run #3"`).<br>3. *Unified Call Sites*:<br>This ergonomic naming policy SHALL apply uniformly across all workout naming avenues:<br>• Live tracking completion with cluster suggestion (`TrackerService`).<br>• Historical and TCX workout import assignment (`WorkoutClusterEngine.assignClusterToWorkout`).<br>• Manual cluster assignment in workout editor (`EditWorkoutViewModel`).<br>• Cluster creation from existing workout (`WorkoutClusterEngine.createNewClusterFromWorkout`).<br>4. *Matching & Deduplication Invariants*:<br>Normalization (`WorkoutClusterEngine.normalizeName`) and hit count stripping (`WorkoutClusterEngine.stripHitCount`) SHALL continue to cleanly strip both numbered suffixes (`#\d+`) and base cluster names without numerical suffixes, ensuring cluster clustering, suggestion scoring, and deduplication remain consistent.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>1. *Given* a cluster named `"Morning Run"` with no prior workouts (`hitCount = 0`),<br>2. *When* a workout is assigned to this cluster,<br>3. *Then* the workout name SHALL be formatted as `"Morning Run"`.<br>4. *Given* a cluster named `"Morning Run"` with 1 existing workout (`hitCount = 1`),<br>5. *When* a second workout is assigned to this cluster,<br>6. *Then* the workout name SHALL be formatted as `"Morning Run #2"`.<br>7. *Given* a cluster with 5 existing workouts (`hitCount = 5`),<br>8. *When* a sixth workout is assigned to this cluster,<br>9. *Then* the workout name SHALL be formatted as `"Morning Run #6"`.<br><br>**Invariants**: The underlying mathematical clustering engine, 3D topological similarity scoring, and spatial tolerances MUST remain unaltered. Numbering for workouts with count $\ge 2$ MUST strictly preserve the `"%1$s #%2$d"` pattern. Room database entities and schemas MUST NOT change. | Avoid unnatural and cluttered names like "Morning Run #1" for solitary or initial route activities while preserving numbered series tracking for recurring routes. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-053` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-067** | **Optional Route Cluster Activity Counter / Auto-Numbering.** | The system SHALL provide a per-cluster configuration option (`hasCounter: Boolean`) determining whether workouts belonging to a Workout Cluster receive sequential numbered suffixes (`#<count>`):<br>1. *Database Schema & Persistence*:<br>• The `RouteClusters.db` SQLite schema SHALL be upgraded from Version 10 to Version 11 by adding column `has_counter INTEGER DEFAULT 1`.<br>• `WorkoutCluster` SHALL include `hasCounter: Boolean = true` (default `true` for 100% backward compatibility with all existing clusters).<br>2. *Formatting Rule Execution*:<br>• When `hasCounter == false`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.formatClusterWorkoutName(...)` SHALL always return the clean base cluster name (`clusterName`) without any numerical suffix (`#<count>`), regardless of hit count.<br>• When `hasCounter == true`, `formatClusterWorkoutName(...)` SHALL follow `REQ-SET-066` (no suffix for `hitCount <= 1`, appending `"#<count>"` for `hitCount >= 2`).<br>3. *Interactive UI Configuration*:<br>• `EditWorkoutClusterIdentityDialog` SHALL provide an interactive Switch/Toggle allowing users to enable or disable the activity counter for the cluster.<br>• `EditWorkoutClusterDialog` ("Create New Route...") SHALL provide a toggle for the counter on newly created routes (default: enabled).<br>4. *Unified Call Sites & Invariants*:<br>• All workout naming avenues (`assignClusterToWorkout`, `TrackerService.java`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`) SHALL respect `cluster.hasCounter`.<br>• `cluster.hitCount` tracking in the database, 3D similarity calculations, and cluster deduplication MUST remain unaltered. | Allow users to suppress numerical suffixes (#2, #3, ...) for routes where static unnumbered naming is preferred (e.g., commutes, races, named trail loops) while preserving sequential numbering for routes where desired. | `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClusterComponents.kt`, `TrackerService.java`, `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt` | `TST-SET-054` | Verified |

recency statistics for each Workout Cluster (`WorkoutCluster`):<br>1. *Database Aggregation & Model*:<br>• The system SHALL execute a single-pass aggregate query in `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager` against `WorkoutSummaries.TABLE` grouped by `clusterId`:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`COUNT(*)` as total workouts,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`MAX(timeStart)` as latest workout date,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`AVG(speedAverage_mps)` as average speed,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`AVG(timeActive_s)` as average active duration,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`MIN(CASE WHEN finished = 1 AND timeActive_s > 0 THEN timeActive_s ELSE NULL END)` as personal record (Best Time),<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`SUM(distanceTotal_m)` as total cumulative distance,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`SUM(ascending)` as total cumulative elevation gain,<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;`SUM(timeActive_s)` as total cumulative active moving time.<br>• The system SHALL encapsulate these values in `data class WorkoutClusterStats` and expose them via `WorkoutClusterRepository.clusterStats: StateFlow<Map<Long, WorkoutClusterStats>>` refreshed atomically alongside clusters.<br>2. *Semantic Information Architecture & Layout*:<br>• *Detail Screen Dashboard (`WorkoutClusterSummaryHeader`)*: The system SHALL Speed for cycling/other).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;b) *Cumulative Totals*: Total Distance, Total Climbing, and Total Moving Time.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;c) *Activity & Recency*: Interactive Recordings Count button (tapping navigates to filtered workout list) and Last Activity date with relative recency (e.g., `"06.09.2026 (3 days ago)"`).<br>• *List Card View (`ClusterItem` / `WorkoutClusterMetadataBlock`)*: unaffected.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>1. *Given* a Workout Cluster with 3 completed workouts (times: 40m, 35m, 45m; distances: 10km, 10km, 10km; ascents: 100m, 120m, 110m),<br>2. *When* statistics are calculated and displayed,<br>3. *Then* the system SHALL report Best Time = 35m, Average Time = 40m, Total Distance = 30km, and Total Climbing = 330m.<br>4. *Given* a Running cluster,<br>5. *When* velocity is displayed,<br>6. *Then* the system SHALL format the metric as average pace (e.g. `4:30 /km`).<br>7. *Given* a newly created cluster with no sessions,<br>8. *When* viewed in list or detail view,<br>9. *Then* the system SHALL display empty/placeholder stats safely without errors.<br><br>**Invariants**: Cluster fingerprinting, apex anchoring, database schema, and existing cluster filtering/sorting MUST NOT be modified. | Provide athletes with comprehensive historical insights, personal benchmarks, and cumulative volume tracking for recurring routes | **REQ-SET-069** | **Workout, Route & Segment Detail Bottom Sheet Scaffold Status Bar Boundary Constraint.** | The system SHALL constrain the maximum upward expansion of bottom sheet scaffolds hosting Workout, Route, and Segment details to the bottom edge of the Android system status bar (`WindowInsets.statusBars`):<br>1. *Scaffold Top Boundary Alignment*: In `PeriodMapScreen`, `MapFragmentWithTrack`, and `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen`, the `BottomSheetScaffold` composable SHALL apply `Modifier.statusBarsPadding()` to align the top edge of the scaffold container with the bottom boundary of the system status bar (`Y = statusBarHeight`).<br>2. *Status Bar Overlap Prevention*: When the bottom sheet is dragged or animated to its fully expanded state (`SheetValue.Expanded`), the top of the sheet SHALL stop at `0f` relative to the scaffold (matching the status bar bottom edge), preventing the sheet title, drag handle, or workout metadata from colliding with or drawing behind system status bar icons (clock, battery, notifications).<br>3. *Peek Height & Drag Handle Integrity*: The bottom sheet's peek height (`sheetPeekHeight`) SHALL continue to be measured from the bottom edge of the screen / navigation bars without offset distortion. The drag handle (`MinimumDragHandle`) SHALL retain its standard compact dimensions (~24 dp) and minimal padding, ensuring all peeked content remains fully visible in the initial popup without excessive whitespace or truncation.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout detail peek in `PeriodMapScreen`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen`, or a segment/route detail peek in `MapFragmentWithTrack`,<br>• *When* the bottom sheet is in its initial peeked state,<br>• *Then* the sheet SHALL render with compact drag handle dimensions and the initial header content SHALL be fully visible without truncation.<br>• *When* the user drags or expands the bottom sheet to its maximum height (`SheetValue.Expanded`),<br>• *Then* the top edge of the sheet SHALL stop cleanly at the bottom boundary of the system status bar, and zero header content or drag handle graphics SHALL draw underneath the status bar icons.<br><br>**Invariants**: `sheetPeekHeight` calculations, `MinimumDragHandle` layout dimensions, and underlying map rendering logic MUST NOT be broken. | Prevent status bar icons and clocks from colliding with bottom sheet titles across all map detail sheets while preserving initial peek height and a compact drag handle. | `PeriodMapScreen.kt`, `MapFragmentWithTrack.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` | `TST-SET-058` | Verified |
| **REQ-SET-070** | **Direct Navigation to Workout Editor from Workout Detail Popup & Unified Action Button Layout.** | The system SHALL allow users to directly open the Workout Editor (`EditWorkoutScreen`) from the Workout Detail Popup (`TrackOnMapScreen` / `WorkoutHeader`) in analytical map views (Period Map and Favorite Tracks / Cluster Heatmap), and SHALL enforce a unified action button layout where the edit action is positioned on the very right:<br>1. *Workout Popup Edit Trigger*:<br>• `TrackOnMapScreen` and `WorkoutHeader` SHALL accept an optional callback `onEditWorkout: ((Long) -> Unit)? = null`.<br>• When `onEditWorkout != null`, `WorkoutHeader`'s top-end action row SHALL render a compact `32.dp` `IconButton` with `R.drawable.ic_table_edit`, tinted with `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary` and localized content description `@string/edit_workout`. Tapping this button SHALL invoke `onEditWorkout(workoutData.id)`.<br>• Tapping the workout header surface itself (`onClicked`) SHALL also invoke `onEditWorkout(workoutData.id)`.<br>2. *Unified Action Button Ordering*:<br>• *Workout Popup (`WorkoutHeader`)*: The Edit Workout button SHALL be positioned on the **very right** of the action row. Contextual actions (e.g., `SwapHoriz` for cluster transfer in the cluster heatmap) SHALL be positioned to the left of the edit button.<br>• *Favorite Tracks Header (`ClusterSummaryHeader`)*: The | **REQ-EXP-008** | **Smart Asymmetric Workout Naming Strategy & Pre-Upload Duplicate Discovery for Strava.** | The system SHALL implement a smart asymmetric naming strategy and pre-upload duplicate discovery when synchronizing workouts to Strava (`StravaUploader.kt`), distinguishing fresh uploads from existing/duplicate activities:<br>1. *Pre-Upload Duplicate Discovery (Existing Activity Check)*:<br>• Before submitting a TCX file to Strava's upload endpoint (`POST /api/v3/uploads`), the system SHALL query Strava's athlete activities API (`GET /api/v3/athlete/activities?after={start - 300}&before={start + 300}`) using the workout's start timestamp (`TIME_START`).<br>• If an activity already exists on Strava within this temporal window (or if already recorded in `StravaUploadDbHelper`), the system SHALL bypass the TCX file upload entirely, preventing Strava from creating an unwanted duplicate activity in the athlete's feed. The system SHALL link the discovered Strava `activity_id` in `StravaUploadDbHelper` and immediately proceed with the duplicate reconciliation flow.<br>2. *Duplicate Reconciliation Flow (`isDuplicate == true` or existing Strava activity)*:<br>• *Strava Authority & Overwrite Protection*: When a workout already exists on Strava (via pre-upload query, local DB record, or upload duplicate error), the system SHALL NOT submit the workout name parameter (`NAME` / `"name"`) in the metadata update request (`doUpdate`). The activity title existing on Strava SHALL be preserved as authoritative and MUST NOT be overwritten by the app.<br>• *Local Database Enrichment & Strava Default Name Evaluation*: The system SHALL inspect the Strava activity name (`"name"`):<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;- *Default Strava Name*: If the Strava name matches an auto-generated localized time-of-day default template (e.g., `"Radfahrt am Morgen"`, `"Lauf am Abend ⛅"`, `"Morning Ride"`, `"Afternoon Run"`), the app SHALL preserve the local name generated by the app (e.g. from route clustering or TCX tags) and SHALL NOT overwrite the local name in SQLite.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;- *Custom Strava Title*: If the Strava name is different from Strava default templates (e.g., `"Sunday Club Ride"`, `"Schwarzwald Tour"`, `"Berlin Marathon"`), the system SHALL update `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` in SQLite with the custom title from Strava.<br>• *Resilient Duplicate Fallback*: In `checkAndUpdateDuplicate()`, the system SHALL inspect both `error` and `status` response properties using regex pattern matching (`"duplicate of.*?(\\d+)"`) as a fallback if pre-upload check was bypassed.<br>3. *Fresh Upload Flow (`isDuplicate == false`)*:<br>• *Meaningful Name Serialization*: For newly created Strava activities, the system SHALL include the `name` parameter in the metadata update payload if and only if `workoutName` is non-blank and does NOT equal `fileBaseName`.<br>• *Strava Auto-Naming Preservation*: If the local workout has no custom name (`workoutName.isNullOrBlank() || workoutName == fileBaseName`), the system SHALL omit the `name` parameter from the update payload, allowing Strava to assign its natural localized time-of-day title.<br>4. *System Invariants*:<br>• Sport type mapping (`TYPE`, `SPORT_TYPE`), equipment/gear ID (`GEAR_ID`), workout description (`DESCRIPTION`), trainer flag (`TRAINER`), and commute flag (`COMMUTE`) SHALL continue to be synchronized to Strava during both fresh and duplicate updates.<br>• TCX file export serialization (`REQ-DAT-013`) and spatial route clustering (`REQ-ROU-001`) MUST NOT be altered.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an old workout with `TIME_START = \"2024-05-12 10:15:30\"` and local name `\"Hausrunde #3\"` where Strava already contains an activity at that timestamp with default title `\"Radfahrt am Morgen\"`,<br>• *When* `doExport` executes,<br>• *Then* `findExistingStravaActivity` SHALL discover the existing Strava activity, bypass the TCX file upload (preventing duplicate activity creation on Strava), and preserve local SQLite `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` as `\"Hausrunde #3\"`.<br>• *Given* an old workout with `TIME_START = \"2024-05-12 10:15:30\"` and local name `\"Cycling\"` where Strava already contains an activity with custom title `\"Sunday Club Ride\"`,<br>• *When* `doExport` executes,<br>• *Then* `findExistingStravaActivity` SHALL discover the existing Strava activity, bypass the TCX file upload, and enrich local SQLite `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` to `\"Sunday Club Ride\"`.<br>• *Given* a workout not existing on Strava,<br>• *When* `doExport` executes,<br>• *Then* `findExistingStravaActivity` SHALL return null, and the system SHALL proceed to upload the TCX file via `POST /api/v3/uploads`.<br>• *Given* a workout with `WORKOUT_NAME = \"2024-05-12_10-15-30\"` (`fileBaseName = \"2024-05-12_10-15-30\"`) uploaded to Strava as a fresh activity,<br>• *When* `doUpdate` executes for the fresh upload,<br>• *Then* the update request sent to Strava SHALL NOT contain parameter `name`, allowing Strava to auto-name the activity.<br>• *Given* a workout with custom name `WORKOUT_NAME = \"Mountain Stage\"` (`fileBaseName = \"2024-05-12_10-15-30\"`) uploaded to Strava as a fresh activity,<br>• *When* `doUpdate` executes for the fresh upload,<br>• *Then* the update request sent to Strava SHALL contain parameter `name = \"Mountain Stage\"`. | `StravaUploader.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-EXP-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-009** | **Strava Duplicate Reconciliation Equipment, Retired Gear & Detailed Sport Type Enrichment.** | The system SHALL enrich imported workouts matching existing Strava activities (`isDuplicate == true`) with equipment and detailed sport classification without overwriting Strava cloud data:<br>1. *Strava Cloud Authority on Duplicate*: When reconciling an existing Strava activity, the system SHALL NOT submit `type`/`sport_type` or `gear_id` to Strava's update API (`doUpdate`), preserving Strava's cloud metadata as authoritative.<br>2. *Equipment Schema & Reverse Lookup*: `Equipment.db` SHALL maintain a `Retired` column (`INTEGER DEFAULT 0`, version 2 migration) indicating retirement status. `EquipmentDbHelper` SHALL provide reverse lookup `getIdFromStravaId(String stravaId)`.<br>3. *On-Demand Lazy Fetch for Missing / Retired Gear*: If a duplicate activity has `gear_id != null` that does not exist in local `Equipment.db`, the system SHALL query Strava's `GET /api/v3/gear/{gear_id}`. The returned equipment SHALL be inserted into `Equipment.db` using its original Strava `name`, `strava_id`, `frame_type`, derived `sport_type` (BIKE vs RUN), and `retired` status (`Retired = (retired ? 1 : 0)`).<br>4. *Local Workout Equipment Enrichment*: If `gear_id` resolves to a local equipment record (either pre-existing or freshly fetched), `WorkoutSummaries.EQUIPMENT_ID` in SQLite SHALL be updated with the resolved local `_ID`.<br>5. *Detailed Sport Type Enrichment*: If the Strava activity contains `sport_type` or `type`, `SportTypeDatabaseManager` SHALL resolve the local `sport_id` via `getSportTypeIdFromStravaName(stravaName)` (e.g. `MountainBikeRide` -> MTB), falling back to `BSportType` mapping if no direct sub-sport match exists. `WorkoutSummaries.SPORT_ID` in SQLite SHALL be updated with the resolved `sport_id`.<br>6. *In-Memory Cache Synchronization*: `WorkoutRepository.reloadExportStatusesFor(fileName)` SHALL reload the full workout session from `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager`, updating `sportId`, `sportName`, `bSportType`, and `equipmentId` in the in-memory cache so UI views update reactively.<br>7. *System Invariants*: Fresh uploads (`isDuplicate == false`) SHALL continue to serialize local equipment and sport types. Default title preservation rules from `REQ-EXP-008` SHALL remain intact.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a duplicated workout where Strava has `gear_id = \"b123\"` corresponding to local bike ID 5,<br>• *When* duplicate reconciliation executes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutSummaries.EQUIPMENT_ID` SHALL be updated to 5, and Strava's cloud gear SHALL NOT be overwritten.<br>• *Given* a duplicated workout where Strava has `gear_id = \"b999\"` not present in `Equipment.db`,<br>• *When* duplicate reconciliation executes,<br>• *Then* `GET /api/v3/gear/b999` is queried, the equipment is saved in `Equipment.db` with original Strava name and `Retired = 1` if retired on Strava, and its new local `_ID` is assigned to `WorkoutSummaries.EQUIPMENT_ID`.<br>• *Given* a duplicated workout where Strava has `sport_type = \"MountainBikeRide\"` and local workout was imported generically as `BIKE`,<br>• *When* duplicate reconciliation executes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutSummaries.SPORT_ID` SHALL be updated to MTB.<br>• *Given* a successful reconciliation with enriched equipment and sport type,<br>• *When* `reloadExportStatusesFor` completes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutRepository` in-memory cache reflects the updated `sportName` and `equipmentId`. | `StravaUploader.kt`, `EquipmentDbHelper.java`, `SportTypeDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-EXP-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-010** | **Strava Segment Personal Best Update & Achievement Presentation.** | The system SHALL update segment Personal Best (PB) records in the local database based on activity feedback received from Strava after workout upload, refresh in-memory segment state reactively, and prominently celebrate new achievements:<br>1. *Strava Activity Effort Parsing & PB Detection*:<br>• In `StravaUploader.kt`, upon retrieving detailed activity feedback (`getStravaActivity`) during `doUpdate()`, the system SHALL parse the `segment_efforts` array.<br>• For each effort having a valid Strava segment ID (`segment.id` or `segment_id`), duration (`elapsed_time > 0`), and representing a personal record (`pr_rank == 1`, `kom_rank == 1`, or achievements array containing `type == "pr"` or `"kom"` with `rank == 1`), the system SHALL trigger a local database update.<br>2. *Database Persistence (`SegmentsDatabaseManager`)*:<br>• The system SHALL provide `updateSegmentPrTime(long stravaSegmentId, int newPrTimeSeconds): boolean` in `SegmentsDatabaseManager.java`.<br>• If `stravaSegmentId` exists in `StarredSegmentsTable` and the new duration is faster than the existing record (`newPrTimeSeconds < currentPrTime || currentPrTime <= 0`), the system SHALL update `pr_time = newPrTimeSeconds` in SQLite and return `true`.<br>• If `stravaSegmentId` does not exist in `StarredSegmentsTable` or the existing PR time is already faster or equal, the database record SHALL NOT be altered and the method SHALL return `false`.<br>3. *Reactive In-Memory State Propagation (`SegmentsRepository`)*:<br>• Upon a successful database update (`updateSegmentPrTime == true`), `StravaUploader` SHALL notify `SegmentsRepository.getInstance(context).updateSegmentPr(segmentId, elapsedTimeSec)`.<br>• `SegmentsRepository` SHALL update the matching segment's `SegmentSummary` (`prTime_raw` and formatted `prTime`) within its internal `_allSegmentsWithPath` flow, immediately propagating the new PR to observers (`SegmentListViewModel`, `LiveSegmentsRepository`) without requiring an application restart or manual re-sync.<br>4. *Prominent Achievement Presentation in Workout Summary*:<br>• When a workout contains $\ge 1$ segment efforts qualifying as a new personal best (`prRank == 1 || komRank == 1`), `StravaActivitySection.kt` SHALL render a prominent celebration highlight banner at the top of the Strava activity results card.<br>• The celebration banner SHALL feature a themed container (`TTColor.Gold` / gold container styling), an achievement icon (`ic_trophy` or `ic_crown`), celebratory headline, and segment achievement details formatted with 100% localization parity across all 9 supported languages (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>• The banner SHALL NOT display if the workout contains zero rank-1 PR or KOM segment efforts.<br>5. *System Invariants*:<br>• Strava upload flow, smart asymmetric workout naming (`REQ-EXP-008`), and equipment duplicate reconciliation (`REQ-EXP-009`) MUST NOT be altered.<br>• Segment catalog filtering (`REQ-UI-134`), segment sorting, and live tracking gate geometry (`REQ-LIV-001` - `REQ-LIV-004`) MUST NOT be regressed.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an uploaded workout returning a Strava segment effort on a starred segment with `elapsed_time = 120` and `pr_rank = 1` where local DB has `pr_time = 135`,<br>• *When* `doUpdate` processes the activity JSON,<br>• *Then* `SegmentsDatabaseManager.updateSegmentPrTime` SHALL update `pr_time` to 120, `SegmentsRepository.allSegmentsWithPath` SHALL emit the updated PR time, and `StravaActivitySection` SHALL render the celebration highlight banner.<br>• *Given* an uploaded workout returning a segment effort with `pr_rank = 2` (or null),<br>• *When* `doUpdate` processes the activity JSON,<br>• *Then* local `pr_time` SHALL NOT be updated and the celebration banner SHALL NOT be displayed.<br>• *Given* an uploaded workout returning a segment effort with `pr_rank = 1` and `elapsed_time = 150` on a segment where local DB already has `pr_time = 140`,<br>• *When* `doUpdate` processes the activity JSON,<br>• *Then* local `pr_time` SHALL remain 140 and `updateSegmentPrTime` SHALL return `false`.<br>• *Given* an uploaded workout returning a segment effort with `pr_rank = 1` for a segment not present in `StarredSegmentsTable`,<br>• *When* `doUpdate` processes the activity JSON,<br>• *Then* no SQLite rows SHALL be inserted or modified, and `updateSegmentPrTime` SHALL return `false`. | `StravaUploader.kt`, `SegmentsDatabaseManager.java`, `SegmentsRepository.kt`, `StravaActivitySection.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-EXP-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-011** | **Strava Segments Mandatory Daily Automated Synchronization.** | The system SHALL support mandatory automated daily background synchronization of starred Strava segments via Android `WorkManager` (ATT-913, ATT-1178):<br>1. *Mandatory Daily Scheduling*: When Strava is authenticated (`TrainingApplication.getStravaAccessToken() != null`), `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker.schedule(context)` SHALL enqueue unique periodic work `automated_strava_segments_sync_work` with a fixed interval of 1 day (`1, TimeUnit.DAYS`), `NetworkType.CONNECTED`, and `requiresBatteryNotLow(true)`.<br>2. *No User-Configurable Intervals ("No need to ask the user")*: The system SHALL NOT prompt the user or allow configuring intervals or disabling automated sync; `StravaSettingsDialog.kt` SHALL omit automated sync toggles and interval dropdowns.<br>3. *Cancellation upon Disconnect*: When Strava is disconnected, `schedule(context)` SHALL cancel the periodic work via `WorkManager.cancelUniqueWork("automated_strava_segments_sync_work")`.<br>4. *Execution & TTL Pruning*: In `doWork()`, the worker SHALL execute 7-day TTL cache pruning, then invoke `SegmentsRepository.getInstance(applicationContext).syncStarredSegments(BSportType.UNKNOWN)` and update `last_update_time_of_strava_segments` in SharedPreferences.<br>5. *Manual Synchronization Cards*: `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL maintain the `OutlinedCard` for manual segment synchronization (`@string/updateStravaSegments`), displaying the formatted timestamp of the last synchronization.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete authenticated with Strava,<br>• *When* `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker.schedule(context)` is called,<br>• *Then* `WorkManager` SHALL have unique periodic work `automated_strava_segments_sync_work` enqueued with fixed 1-day interval and connected network constraints.<br>• *Given* an athlete disconnected from Strava,<br>• *When* `schedule(context)` is called,<br>• *Then* `WorkManager.cancelUniqueWork(\"automated_strava_segments_sync_work\")` SHALL be invoked.<br>• *Given* an athlete viewing `StravaSettingsDialog`,<br>• *When* the dialog renders,<br>• *Then* automated sync toggles and interval dropdowns SHALL NOT be displayed, and manual update cards with timestamps SHALL be displayed. | `StravaSettingsDialog.kt`, `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `SegmentsRepository.kt` | `TST-EXP-008` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-012** | **Strava Routes Mandatory Daily Automated Synchronization.** | The system SHALL support mandatory automated daily background synchronization of Strava routes via Android `WorkManager` (ATT-914, ATT-1178):<br>1. *Mandatory Daily Scheduling*: When Strava is authenticated (`TrainingApplication.getStravaAccessToken() != null`), `StravaRoutesSyncWorker.schedule(context)` SHALL enqueue unique periodic work `automated_strava_routes_sync_work` with a fixed interval of 1 day (`1, TimeUnit.DAYS`), `NetworkType.CONNECTED`, and `requiresBatteryNotLow(true)`.<br>2. *No User-Configurable Intervals ("No need to ask the user")*: The system SHALL NOT prompt the user or allow configuring intervals or disabling automated sync; `StravaSettingsDialog.kt` SHALL omit automated sync toggles and interval dropdowns.<br>3. *Cancellation upon Disconnect*: When Strava is disconnected, `schedule(context)` SHALL cancel the periodic work via `WorkManager.cancelUniqueWork("automated_strava_routes_sync_work")`.<br>4. *Execution & TTL Pruning*: In `doWork()`, the worker SHALL execute 7-day TTL cache pruning, then invoke `RoutesRepository.getInstance(applicationContext).syncRoutesFromStrava()` and update `lastUpdateTimeOfStravaRoutes` in SharedPreferences.<br>5. *Manual Synchronization Cards*: `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL maintain the `OutlinedCard` for manual route synchronization (`@string/updateStravaRoutes`), displaying the formatted timestamp of the last synchronization.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete authenticated with Strava,<br>• *When* `StravaRoutesSyncWorker.schedule(context)` is called,<br>• *Then* `WorkManager` SHALL have unique periodic work `automated_strava_routes_sync_work` enqueued with fixed 1-day interval and connected network constraints.<br>• *Given* an athlete disconnected from Strava,<br>• *When* `schedule(context)` is called,<br>• *Then* `WorkManager.cancelUniqueWork(\"automated_strava_routes_sync_work\")` SHALL be invoked.<br>• *Given* an athlete viewing `StravaSettingsDialog`,<br>• *When* the dialog renders,<br>• *Then* automated sync toggles and interval dropdowns SHALL NOT be displayed, and manual update cards with timestamps SHALL be displayed. | `StravaSettingsDialog.kt`, `StravaRoutesSyncWorker.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `RoutesRepository.kt` | `TST-EXP-009` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-013** | **Strava Activity Feedback Data Minimization to Athlete Achievements.** | The system SHALL minimize persisted Strava activity response payloads to athlete-specific achievements and preserve strict Command-Query Separation across database interactions (ATT-1190):<br>1. *Data Minimization to Achievements*: Upon receiving activity feedback (`GET /api/v3/activities/{id}`), `StravaUploader` SHALL NOT persist raw third-party JSON blobs containing transient social, profile, map, or partner fields to SQLite. Instead, the system SHALL extract only the athlete's own achievements and serialize a compact JSON payload (`"v": 2`) containing strictly `id`, `segment_efforts` (`name`, `elapsed_time`, `pr_rank`, `kom_rank`, `starred`, `segment_id`), and `best_efforts` (`name`, `elapsed_time`, `pr_rank`, `distance`). All extraneous fields (kudos, comments, athlete profiles, photos, external URLs, polyline vectors) SHALL be discarded before storage.<br>2. *Deterministic Omission of Null/Absent Fields*: In the minimized schema (`"v": 2`), optional fields that are null, empty, non-positive, or false (e.g. unranked efforts, unstarred segments, zero distances) SHALL be omitted from serialization rather than written as explicit JSON nulls.<br>3. *Strict Command-Query Separation (Zero Write-on-Read)*: `StravaUploadDbHelper.getStravaActivityData()` and related query methods SHALL remain strictly read-only and MUST NOT perform implicit write operations, row updates, or database mutations during cursor reads.<br>4. *Robustness & Truncation Resilience*: `StravaActivityParser.parse()` SHALL handle both legacy uncompacted JSON (v1) and minimized JSON (v2) transparently. If a payload is truncated, corrupted, or malformed, the parser SHALL catch `JSONException`, log a warning, and safely return `null` without throwing unhandled runtime exceptions.<br>5. *System Invariants*:<br>• In-memory duplicate reconciliation and segment PR extraction (`processSegmentEffortsForPrs`) in `StravaUploader` SHALL continue to execute on the full in-memory response prior to storage.<br>• Offline rendering in `StravaActivitySection` (celebration banners, best efforts, segment accordions) SHALL retain 100% visual and functional parity.<br>• `StravaDataPurgeManager.purgeAllStravaData()` SHALL continue to wipe all local records on deauthorization.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a raw Strava activity response containing kudos, athlete profile, photos, comments, and segment efforts,<br>• *When* `StravaActivityParser.minimize(...)` or `StravaUploader.doUpdate` processes the payload,<br>• *Then* the persisted JSON SHALL contain `"v": 2`, athlete achievements (`segment_efforts`, `best_efforts`), and SHALL NOT contain athlete profile or social engagement fields.<br>• *Given* a segment effort with no PR (`pr_rank == null`),<br>• *When* serialized to minimized JSON,<br>• *Then* `"pr_rank"` SHALL be omitted from the effort JSON object.<br>• *Given* a truncated or malformed JSON string stored in SQLite,<br>• *When* `StravaActivityParser.parse()` executes,<br>• *Then* it SHALL return `null` safely without crashing the UI thread.<br>• *Given* any read invocation of `StravaUploadDbHelper.getStravaActivityData()`,<br>• *When* executed,<br>• *Then* zero database write transactions or row updates SHALL be executed. | Comply with Strava API Agreement Section 6.2 (Cache & Retention), eliminate database bloat, and protect UI thread stability via strict CQS. | `StravaActivityData.kt`, `StravaUploader.kt`, `StravaUploadDbHelper.java`, `StravaActivitySection.kt` | `TST-EXP-010` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXP-014** | **Reactive Strava Segment Synchronization Progress Indicator & Transient State Decoupling.** | The system SHALL provide reactive in-memory synchronization progress tracking for starred Strava segments and decouple transient UI state from persistent storage (ATT-1219):<br>1. *Reactive In-Memory State Flow*: `SegmentsRepository` SHALL expose an overarching `val isSyncing: StateFlow<Boolean>` property that transitions to `true` upon the initiation of starred segment synchronization (`syncStarredSegments`) and reliably resets to `false` via a `finally` block upon completion or failure.<br>2. *Defensive Worker Cleanup*: In `syncStarredSegmentsWorker(bSportType)`, the system SHALL wrap the worker execution in `try ... finally` to ensure `_refreshingSports.update { it - bSportType }` is guaranteed even when network or JSON parsing exceptions occur.<br>3. *Transient State UI Decoupling (Storage Invariant)*: The system SHALL NOT persist transient progress states or localized temporal indicators (such as `"now"` / `"jetzt"`) to `SharedPreferences` (`SP_LAST_UPDATE_TIME_OF_STRAVA_SEGMENTS`). SharedPreferences SHALL strictly store formatted historical completion timestamps (`DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date())`) or remain uninitialized ("never").<br>4. *Reactive Composable Rendering in Strava Popup*: In `StravaSettingsDialog`, the secondary text under the "Update Strava Segments" card (`@string/updateStravaSegments`) SHALL collect `isSyncing` from `SegmentsRepository`. While `isSyncing` is `true`, the Composable SHALL render `@string/lastUpdateOfSegmentsNow` directly via Compose `stringResource()`. While `isSyncing` is `false`, the Composable SHALL render the formatted completion timestamp (`segmentsLastUpdate`).<br>5. *Manual Sync Debounce Guard*: `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL debounce manual sync requests by ignoring card click events while `isSyncing` is `true`, preventing redundant background coroutine dispatches.<br>6. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*: String resource `lastUpdateOfSegmentsNow` SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN: `"now"`, DE: `"jetzt"`, ES: `"ahora"`, FR: `"maintenant"`, IT: `"adesso"`, JA: `"今"`, NL: `"nu"`, PL: `"teraz"`, PT: `"agora"`).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete viewing `StravaSettingsDialog` with an idle segment repository (`isSyncing == false`),<br>• *When* the athlete taps the "Update Strava Segments" card,<br>• *Then* `SegmentsRepository.isSyncing` SHALL emit `true`, the card secondary text SHALL immediately display the localized `"now"` string (`@string/lastUpdateOfSegmentsNow`), and `SharedPreferences` SHALL NOT be mutated with `"now"`.<br>• *Given* active segment synchronization (`isSyncing == true`),<br>• *When* the athlete taps the "Update Strava Segments" card again,<br>• *Then* the click handler SHALL be ignored and no additional background sync tasks SHALL be dispatched.<br>• *Given* active segment synchronization (`isSyncing == true`),<br>• *When* the synchronization completes successfully,<br>• *Then* `SharedPreferences` SHALL be updated with the current formatted date-time string, `isSyncing` SHALL emit `false`, and the card secondary text SHALL display the formatted completion timestamp.<br>• *Given* active segment synchronization (`isSyncing == true`),<br>• *When* the network call fails or throws an exception,<br>• *Then* `isSyncing` SHALL reset to `false` via `finally`, `SharedPreferences` SHALL retain the prior uncorrupted timestamp, and the card secondary text SHALL revert to the prior timestamp.<br>• *Given* any supported device locale (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT),<br>• *When* segment synchronization is active,<br>• *Then* the card secondary text SHALL display the correct translation of `"now"`. | `SegmentsRepository.kt`, `StravaSettingsDialog.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-EXP-011` | Verified |

Tapping this icon SHALL display the `SensorSourceDialog` focused on technical accuracy (in meters) to provide actionable data quality insights. | `SensorStatus.kt`, `SensorType.java` | `TST-UI-054` | Verified |

| **REQ-UI-055** | Primary Metric Highlighting. | Primary performance data (Total Distance, Active Time) SHALL use the `primary` color to establish a clear visual hierarchy. | `MetricItem.kt`, `WorkoutDetails.kt` | `TST-UI-060` | **Deactivated** |

| **REQ-UI-059** | **Robust & Asynchronous Workout Sharing.** | The sharing system SHALL handle optional UI components (Header, Elevation Profile) gracefully. If a component is hidden or has zero size, it SHALL be omitted from the combined snapshot without causing a crash. The `DropdownMenu` SHALL be positioned consistently at the **Top-Left corner** of the card (covering the header) to ensure a predictable and professional user interface. | `MappableListItem.kt` (and callers) | `TST-UI-070` | Verified |
| **REQ-MAP-016** | **Heatmap DSL Support.** | The Map DSL SHALL support the declarative definition of Heatmap layers using standardized sequential gradients (Cyan -> Blue -> Indigo).
