Member marker pins SHALL be culled at city-scale to prevent visual clutter and OOM crashes. | `MapContentScope.kt`, `MapUtils.kt` | `TST-SET-002`, `TST-MAP-010` | Verified |

## 9. External API Compliance

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-EXT-001** | "Powered by Strava" branding on all Strava data. | Comply with mandatory API guidelines. | `PoweredByStrava.kt` | `TST-REG-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-002** | **Official Strava Assets.** | The system SHALL use official Strava \"Connect\" authentication assets to comply with partner brand requirements. | `StravaUploadFragment.kt` | `TST-REG-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-003** | Display logo contextually within lists/headers. | Maintain compliance without UI clutter. | `SegmentList.kt` | `TST-REG-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-004** | Integrated Strava Authorization. | The system SHALL use Chrome Custom Tabs for Strava authorization to provide a seamless user experience within the application context. | `StravaHelper.kt`, `StravaUploadFragment.kt` | `TST-EXT-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-005** | Reactive OAuth Feedback. | The system SHALL provide immediate visual feedback (loading/error) during the Strava token exchange process using modern UI components. | `StravaOAuthCallbackActivity.kt`, `StravaAuthViewModel.kt` | `TST-EXT-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-006** | **Strava Profile Scope & Equipment Synchronization Integrity.** | The system SHALL request the `profile:read_all` OAuth permission scope during Strava authentication (alongside `read`, `read_all`, `activity:read_all`, and `activity:write`) to ensure athlete gear details (bikes and shoes) are returned by Strava API `/api/v3/athlete`. `StravaEquipmentSynchronizeThread` SHALL validate that an active access token is present before executing requests, accurately extract both `bikes` and `shoes` arrays into `EquipmentDbHelper`, avoid creating duplicate local entries for existing unlinked equipment with identical names, and report a descriptive warning if gear arrays are absent due to missing permissions. Furthermore, `EquipmentFragment` SHALL listen for `SYNCHRONIZE_EQUIPMENT_STRAVA_FINISHED` to automatically refresh its equipment view upon synchronization completion. | `StravaHelper.kt`, `StravaEquipmentSynchronizeThread.java`, `EquipmentFragment.kt` | `TST-EXT-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-007** | **Automated & On-Demand Strava Route Synchronization.** | The system SHALL automatically initiate synchronization of athlete routes from Strava API (`/api/v3/athletes/{id}/routes`) into `RoutesDatabaseManager` when Strava authorization succeeds (`StravaAuthState.Success`), alongside equipment and segments. The system SHALL provide an on-demand synchronization control `updateStravaRoutes` in Strava settings (`prefs_strava.xml`), displaying the last route synchronization timestamp and triggering route synchronization when tapped. The route synchronization process SHALL extract high-resolution route streams (`/api/v3/routes/{id}/streams`), decode polyline fallback coordinates if streams are unavailable, persist routes with `RouteSource.STRAVA` and bounds in `RoutesDatabaseManager`, update the last route synchronization timestamp, and seed imported routes into `WorkoutClusterEngine` for recurring route pattern discovery. Existing non-Strava routes, user-selected route states, and database schemas MUST remain preserved. | `StravaUploadFragment.kt`, `RoutesRepository.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `prefs_strava.xml` | `TST-EXT-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-008** | **Automated Community Upload for Imported Workouts.** | Upon successful completion of a TCX workout import and statistical recalculation (`LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx`), the system SHALL evaluate whether community upload to Strava is enabled (`TrainingApplication.uploadToCommunity(FileFormat.STRAVA)`). If enabled, the system SHALL schedule an asynchronous community upload task for the imported workout using `ExportManager`, persisting tracking export status to `ExportStatusDatabaseManager` and queuing the background upload via `WorkManager`. The imported workout record in `WorkoutSummaries` SHALL have `UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA` initialized to reflect the upload request (`1` if requested, or `-1` default). **Invariants**: Import file parsing, local database insertion, and spatial recalculation MUST NOT block on network execution. If network upload fails, the local workout record and stats MUST remain completely intact and available. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `ExportManager.java`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-EXT-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-009** | **Strava Data Deletion on Token Invalidation, Deauthorization & Reauthorization Integrity.** | The system SHALL ensure strict compliance with the Strava API Policy (Section 7.4 Deletion Obligation & Section 6.2 Cache and Retention) regarding authorization termination, while protecting native athlete data and hardware sensor configurations:<br>1. *Mandatory Purge on Deauthorization & Revocation*: Upon explicit user disconnection in `StravaSettingsDialog` or automated server-side revocation detection (unrecoverable token refresh failure with `invalid_grant` / HTTP 400 in `StravaHelper.getRefreshedAccessToken`), the system SHALL execute a comprehensive data purge:<br>• *Credentials & State*: Remove all Strava tokens (`SP_STRAVA_TOKEN`, `SP_STRAVA_REFRESH_TOKEN`, `SP_STRAVA_TOKEN_EXPIRES_AT`, `SP_STRAVA_ATHLETE_ID`) and reset Strava upload toggles (`SP_UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA`) and demo mode to `false` in `SharedPreferences`.<br>• *Activity Details*: Purge all Strava activity JSON data, segment efforts, best efforts, PR/KOM rankings, and Strava activity IDs (`STRAVA_ACTIVITY_DATA` and `STRAVA_ID`) from `StravaUploadDbHelper` (`StravaUpload.db`).<br>• *Starred Segments*: Delete all Strava starred segments from `SegmentsDatabaseManager` (`Segments.db`) and clear in-memory cache in `SegmentsRepository`.<br>• *Strava Routes*: Delete all routes originating from Strava (`ORIGIN = 'STRAVA'` / Strava route IDs) from `RoutesDatabaseManager` (`Routes.db`) and refresh in-memory cache in `RoutesRepository`.<br>• *Equipment Unlinking*: In `EquipmentDbHelper` (`Equipment.db`), clear `StravaId = NULL` and `StravaName = NULL` across all equipment items, preserving local equipment entities, hardware sensor pairings (ANT+/BLE in `Links` table), and sport type links.<br>• *Worker Cancellation*: Cancel automated periodic synchronization workers (`StravaSegmentsSyncWorker` and `StravaRoutesSyncWorker`) via Android `WorkManager`.<br>• *UI State Notification*: Update the UI state across the application to reflect the disconnected state (`isConnected = false`).<br>2. *Remote Deauthorization*: When initiated by the user, the deauthorization request to Strava (`/oauth/deauthorize`) SHALL be dispatched using modern `OkHttpClient` before local credentials are wiped. If network fails, local data purging MUST still proceed.<br>3. *Automated Revocation Detection*: When `StravaHelper.getRefreshedAccessToken()` encounters an unrecoverable OAuth error (`invalid_grant` / HTTP 400), it SHALL automatically trigger the local data purge.<br>4. *Reauthorization Integrity & Deduplication*: Upon subsequent reauthorization (`StravaAuthState.Success`), `StravaEquipmentSynchronizeThread` SHALL match unlinked local equipment by `Name` where `StravaId IS NULL`, restoring `StravaId` and `StravaName` without creating duplicate equipment rows. Starred segments and routes SHALL be freshly synchronized without creating duplicate route or segment rows across concurrent sync tasks or repeated reconnects: `RoutesRepository.syncRoutesFromStrava` and `SegmentsRepository.syncStarredSegments` SHALL serialize concurrent sync executions with a `Mutex`, `RoutesDatabaseManager.insertRoute` and `SegmentsDatabaseManager.addOrUpdateSegment` SHALL idempotently update existing records and purge legacy duplicate rows when matching external/Strava IDs, `SegmentsDatabaseManager.insertSegmentStreams` SHALL delete existing stream records before re-inserting points, `SegmentsDatabaseManager.getAllSegmentSummaries` SHALL filter duplicates in-memory, `SegmentsRepository._allSegmentsWithPath` SHALL update existing segments in-place, and `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL execute segment synchronization sequentially rather than dispatching concurrent `BIKE` and `RUN` sync jobs.<br>5. *Interactive Disconnect Confirmation*: When an athlete initiates disconnection in `StravaSettingsDialog`, the system SHALL NOT immediately purge data or deauthorize. It SHALL present a modal confirmation dialog clearly detailing the consequences (deletion of Strava routes, starred segments and times, unlinking of equipment from Strava, and removal of Strava upload status for past workouts) and reassuring the user that native recorded workouts remain untouched. Disconnection and data purging SHALL ONLY proceed upon explicit confirmation, and all dialog strings SHALL be localized across all 9 supported languages.<br>6. *Invariants*: Native workout sessions, GPS trackpoint streams in `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager`, calculated metrics and durations in `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager`, and interval records in `LapsDatabaseManager` recorded natively by aTrainingTracker MUST NOT be deleted. Non-Strava local routes, local equipment hardware bindings, and sport types MUST remain completely intact. | Comply with Strava API Policy Section 7.4 mandating permanent deletion of Strava data upon token revocation, while protecting native athlete workout recordings and hardware sensor links. | `StravaAuthManager.kt`, `StravaSettingsDialog.kt`, `StravaDeauthorizationThread.java`, `StravaDataPurgeManager.kt`, `StravaHelper.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `StravaUploadDbHelper.java`, `SegmentsDatabaseManager.java`, `SegmentsRepository.kt`, `RoutesDatabaseManager.kt`, `RoutesRepository.kt`, `EquipmentDbHelper.java` | `TST-EXT-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-EXT-010** | **Strava Route & Segment 7-Day TTL Cache Retention, Orphan Pruning & Route Preservation.** | The system SHALL enforce strict compliance with Section 6.2 ("Cache and Retention") of the Strava API Agreement and Developer Terms across all Strava-originated routes and starred segments:<br>1. *7-Day Retention Limit & Persistent Timestamping*: The system SHALL NOT retain cached Strava routes (`RouteSource.STRAVA` in `Routes.db`) or starred segments (in `Segments.db`) for longer than 7 days (604,800,000 ms) without fresh synchronization. Both `Routes.db` (`TABLE_ROUTES`) and `Segments.db` (`TABLE_STARRED_SEGMENTS`) SHALL maintain a `synced_at` timestamp column (`INTEGER DEFAULT 0`, Schema v8). Whenever a route or segment is synchronized or updated from the Strava API, its `synced_at` timestamp SHALL be updated to current epoch milliseconds (`System.currentTimeMillis()`).<br>2. *Automated TTL Expiration & Cascade Eviction*: The system SHALL automatically evict records where `currentTimeMillis() - synced_at > 7 days`. TTL eviction SHALL execute upon repository instantiation (`init`), during background sync worker execution (`StravaRoutesSyncWorker`, `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker`), and on demand. For expired segments, the eviction SHALL cascade-delete associated polyline stream coordinates from `SegmentStreamsTable`.<br>3. *Orphan & Tombstone Pruning*: During remote synchronization, the system SHALL compare local Strava records against the remote API response. Any local route with `source == RouteSource.STRAVA` whose external Strava ID is absent from the active remote route set SHALL be promptly pruned from `Routes.db`. Similarly, any local starred segment whose Strava ID is absent from the active remote starred segments set SHALL be pruned from `Segments.db` alongside its stream coordinates.<br>4. *Athlete Route Preservation (Local GPX Duplication)*: To allow athletes to retain desired navigation tracks permanently offline without violating Strava caching limits, the system SHALL provide a "Save as Local Route" / "Als lokale Route speichern" feature. Duplicating a Strava route SHALL create an independent route record in `Routes.db` with `source = RouteSource.LOCAL_GPX`, clear external Strava identifiers, and copy all spatial coordinate points, bounds, and elevation profiles. Converted local routes SHALL be athlete-owned data and SHALL NOT be subject to Strava TTL eviction or orphan pruning.<br>5. *System Invariants*: Native recorded workouts (`WorkoutSummaries.db`, `WorkoutSamples.db`, `Laps.db`), athlete-owned routes (`RouteSource.LOCAL_GPX` and `RouteSource.WORKOUT`), recurring route clusters in `WorkoutCluster.db`, and hardware sensor bindings in `Equipment.db` MUST NOT be touched, altered, or deleted during TTL eviction or orphan pruning. Eviction operations MUST execute safely in background transactions without blocking UI threads or failing on offline devices. | Comply with Strava API Agreement Section 6.2 mandating a 7-day maximum cache retention and prompt orphan deletion, while safeguarding athlete-owned routes, native workout records, and sensor configurations. | `RoutesDatabaseManager.kt`, `RoutesRepository.kt`, `SegmentsDatabaseManager.java`, `SegmentsRepository.kt`, `StravaRoutesSyncWorker.kt`, `StravaSegmentsSyncWorker.kt` | `TST-EXT-007` | Verified |

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-UI-101** | Neutral Backgrounds. The system SHALL use neutral white backgrounds (Light) or Material Surface (Dark) for all content areas, including progress notifications and migration status cards. Branding colors MUST be isolated strictly to navigation elements (e.g., TabRow backgrounds). | Maintain a clean, professional aesthetic and emphasize content over branding. | `Theme.kt`, `PeriodsTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MAN-001`, `TST-UI-072` | Verified |

| **REQ-UI-104** | **Volume Bar Graph.** | The system SHALL feature a scrollable bar graph for period volume to enhance historical navigation and trend analysis. The graph SHALL remain synchronized with the current scroll position of the period list and MUST be consistently visible below the primary header tabs. During initial data loading or migration, the graph SHALL prioritize the display of the most recent periods to match the user's focus. | `PeriodsTabsScreen.kt`, `PeriodBarGraph.kt` | `TST-MAN-001`, `TST-UI-073`, `TST-UI-076` | Verified |

| **REQ-UI-106** | **Global Localization (Languages).** | The system SHALL support multiple languages (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, PT, NL, PL, JA). All user-facing strings MUST be externalized in `strings.xml`. | `strings.xml`, `values-xx/` | `TST-MAN-001`, `TST-STR-013`, `TST-STR-014`, `TST-STR-016` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-122** | **Strict Format String Compliance.** | All localized string resources with placeholders MUST strictly follow the `java.util.Formatter` syntax. Positional specifiers MUST include both the index and the type suffix (e.g., `%1$s`, `%2$d`). Incomplete specifiers like `%1` are strictly prohibited to prevent runtime crashes. | `strings.xml` in all locales | `TST-STR-016` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-113** | **String Resource Integrity.** | All string resources that are translated in a specific locale MUST also be present in the default `values/strings.xml` to ensure fallback consistency and prevent build warnings/runtime issues. | `strings.xml`, `values-*/strings.xml` | `TST-STR-013` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-107** | **Ubiquitous Unit Support.** | The system SHALL support both Metric and Imperial unit systems everywhere in the application. All user-facing interfaces (Tracking, Analysis, Management, Settings) MUST dynamically adapt to the user's unit preference for distance, speed, pace, and altitude. | `MyUnits.java`, `MyHelper.java`, `MetricFormatterContext.kt` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-108** | **Flexible sorting for lists.** | Allow users to organize and find data easily based on Date, Distance, or Elevation. | `WorkoutSummariesViewModel.kt` | `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-109** | **Communication Clarification.** | Agents SHALL ask for clarification if instructions are unclear to protect project quality and precision. | `project_protocol.md` | `TST-STR-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-110** | Optionally display the current track on a map and its elevation profile. | Provide real-time spatial and vertical situational awareness of the active session. | `BANALServiceRepository.kt`, `SensorGridScreen.kt` | `TST-UI-029` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-111** | Adaptive layout for tab configuration settings. | Ensure all configuration toggles remain accessible and legible across different screen sizes using FlowRow, in the prioritized order: Map, Elevation Profile, Live Segments, Lap Button. | `TrackingTabConfigHeader.kt` | `TST-INT-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-112** | **Navigation Drawer Terminology.** | The system SHALL use professional and standardized terminology for all navigation drawer labels across all supported languages. Specifically, the management hub SHALL be labeled \"Equipment\" (English) / \"Ausrüstung\" (German), the cloud integration section SHALL be labeled \"Synchronization\" (English) / \"Synchronisation\" (German), and the file export item SHALL be labeled \"Export\" (English / German). | `main_navigation_drawer.xml`, `strings.xml` | `TST-NAV-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-114** | **Original Sport Icons.** | The system SHALL display sport type icons in their original colors to ensure quick identification and preserve visual identity. These icons SHALL NOT be subject to theme-based tinting unless they are explicitly in a \"muted\" or \"disabled\" state (e.g. background layers). | `ActivityTypeSelectionDialog.kt`, `SportTypeListFragment.kt` | `TST-INT-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-115** | **Concise Settings Terminology.** | The system SHALL use concise and direct terminology for settings-related navigation items by omitting redundant qualifiers such as \"Settings\", \"Configuration\", \"Einstellungen\", or \"Konfiguration\". | `strings.xml`, `main_navigation_drawer.xml` | `TST-UI-075` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-116** | **Weekly Period Date Range.** | The system SHALL display the explicit start and end dates (e.g., \"May 11 - May 17\") below the title for weekly period summaries in the Periods screen to provide precise temporal context. | `PeriodsRepository.kt`, `PeriodSummaryCard.kt` | `TST-UI-074` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-117** | **Adaptive Header Height.** | The system's collapsing headers (Workouts, Periods, Clusters, etc.) SHALL dynamically calculate their maximum height based on the device's actual status bar insets and the standard content height (Title + Tabs) to ensure consistent layout across all Android hardware (e.g., Samsung, Pixel). | `WorkoutTabsScreen.kt`, `PeriodsTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, etc. | `TST-BUG-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-118** | **Internal Sensor Transparency.** | The `SensorSourceDialog` SHALL correctly display all active internal sensor sources (e.g., Pressure Sensor for Altitude) by ensuring they are included in the active device telemetry list and assigned unique negative identifiers. | `DeviceManager.java`, `AltitudeFromPressureDevice.java` | `TST-BUG-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-DAT-006** | **Eager Internal Sensor Registration.** | The `DeviceManager` SHALL ensure that all detectable internal sensors (e.g., Pressure Sensor) are registered in the database *prior* to any pairing checks or device instantiations within its constructor. This guarantees that internal hardware always has a valid database identity and can be correctly auditable in the UI. | `DeviceManager.java`, `DevicesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-BUG-011` | Verified |
| **REQ-CON-010** | **Internal Sensor Pairing.** | The system SHALL allow the user to manually activate or deactivate internal smartphone sensors (e.g., Barometric Pressure Sensor) via the "My Sensors" interface. | **REQ-DAT-009** | **Authoritative Altitude Extrema Reconciliation & Self-Healing.** | When loading or mapping workout records (`WorkoutDataMapper.kt`) that contain a non-empty `ALTITUDE_STREAM`, the system SHALL verify that persisted `minAltitude` and `maxAltitude` in `extrema_values` are consistent with the decoded stream points. If stored extrema deviate from the actual stream envelope (specifically, if stored min > stream min [persisted minimum higher than actual lowest stream point] or stored min < stream min - 15.0m [historical pre-ATT-499 GPS startup spike], or stored max < stream max or stored max > stream max + 15.0m), the system SHALL reconcile the authoritative extrema to the decoded stream bounds, update `WorkoutData`, and idempotently persist the corrected extrema to `extrema_values` in SQLite.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a workout recorded with corrupted summary extrema (e.g. Min = -63 m, Max = 60 m, or Min = 368 m with stream min = 360 m),<br>• *When* the workout is loaded and mapped by `WorkoutDataMapper`,<br>• *Then* the system SHALL derive the true extrema from the altitude stream, update `extrema_values` in SQLite, and return reconciled values in `WorkoutData`.<br>**Invariants**: Legitimate workouts where extrema match their stream MUST NOT be re-written; workouts without altitude streams MUST retain existing extrema. | Eliminate historical multi-sensor pollution and uncalibrated cold-start extrema from legacy workout records, ensuring summary table matches actual physical stream. | `WorkoutDataMapper.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-DAT-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-DAT-010** | **Bulk Workout Deletion & Relational Storage Purge.** | The system SHALL provide functionality to bulk-delete workout sessions and all associated relational telemetry older than a user-specified retention threshold ($D \ge 0$ days).<br>1. *Query Selection*: The system SHALL select all workout records from `WorkoutSummaries` where `TIME_START <= datetime('now', '-' || ? || ' days')` using parameterized SQL binding.<br>2. *Authoritative Purge Sequencing*: For each identified session, the system SHALL query its unique `FILE_BASE_NAME` *prior* to deleting the summary record, and MUST completely purge all related data across SQLite databases:<br>• `WorkoutSummaries` (`workout_summaries`),<br>• `TABLE_ACCUMULATED_SENSORS` (`accumulated_sensors`),<br>• `TABLE_EXTREMA_VALUES` (`extrema_values`),<br>• `LapsDatabaseManager` (`laps`),<br>• `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager` (dropping the corresponding table `workout_samples_<fileBaseName>`),<br>• `ExportStatusDatabaseManager` (`export_status`).<br>3. *Downstream Cache Resynchronization & Cluster Integrity*: Upon completing the relational deletion of old workouts, the system MUST invoke `PeriodsRepository.resyncAllPeriods()` (`REQ-DAT-011`) and `WorkoutClusterRepository.refreshClusters(forceCheckIntegrity = true)` to ensure downstream analytical caches are rebuilt and zero-workout unlinked orphan clusters are purged.<br>4. *Surviving Cluster Counter Preservation*: For all surviving workout clusters (clusters retaining $\ge 1$ session or linked to an explicit Route), their lifetime hit counter (`hitCount`) SHALL remain untouched.<br>5. *Reactive State Invalidation*: Upon completion, the system SHALL reload `allWorkouts` so UI observers immediately reflect the updated session list.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* recorded workouts older than $D$ days and workouts recorded within $D$ days,<br>• *When* bulk deletion is executed with retention threshold $D$ (default: 365 days),<br>• *Then* all sessions older than $D$ days SHALL be fully purged across all databases without leaving orphaned sample tables, sessions recorded within $D$ days MUST remain intact, and surviving workout clusters SHALL preserve their historical `hitCount` untouched.<br>**Invariants**: Sessions newer than the retention threshold MUST NOT be deleted; single-workout deletion behavior MUST remain unaltered; surviving workout cluster counters (`hitCount`) MUST NOT be decremented or reset upon bulk deletion. | Prevent unbounded database growth and enable user data sovereignty by allowing users to reclaim device storage while preserving lifetime route cluster achievements. | `WorkoutDeletionHelper.java`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt` | `TST-DAT-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-DAT-011** | **Period Database Cache Invalidation & Resynchronization upon Bulk Deletion.** | Following bulk deletion of workouts older than $D$ days, the system SHALL invalidate and rebuild the persistent period cache (`PeriodSummaries.db`) to ensure that aggregated analytical summaries match the remaining workouts in the database:<br>1. *Historical Purge*: All fully purged historical periods (Days, Weeks, Months, Years with zero surviving workouts) MUST be completely removed from `PeriodSummaries.db`.<br>2. *Boundary Recalculation*: Boundary periods spanning across the cutoff retention date MUST be recalculated based strictly on surviving workouts (updating `total_workouts`, `total_duration`, `total_distance`, geographic bounds, and sport breakdowns).<br>3. *Foreign Reference Integrity*: All foreign references to workout IDs (`longest_workout_id`, bounding anchor IDs `north_workout_id`, `south_workout_id`, `east_workout_id`, `west_workout_id`) MUST point exclusively to active, surviving workout sessions or evaluate to `-1`.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a workout history with periods older and newer than 365 days cached in `PeriodSummaries.db`,<br>• *When* bulk deletion is completed,<br>• *Then* `PeriodSummaries.db` SHALL contain zero records for periods whose workouts were 100% purged, and boundary periods SHALL accurately reflect only the surviving workouts.<br>**Invariants**: Single-workout surgical deletion via `onWorkoutDeleted` MUST remain operational for single-session deletions. | Ensure analytical period summaries, heatmaps, and metrics do not display ghost data or dangling workout references after bulk deletion. | `PeriodsRepository.kt`, `PeriodSummariesDatabaseManager.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-DAT-005` | Verified |
| **REQ-DAT-012** | **TCX Lap Information (Name and Description) Bidirectional Export & Import.** | The system SHALL support bidirectional serialization and deserialization of lap names and descriptions within Garmin TCX v2 files (`TrainingCenterDatabasev2.xsd`), preserving custom lap labels, quick tags, coaching notes, and activity descriptions across export and import cycles:<br>1. *TCX Export Schema Placement & Formatting (`TCXFileWriter.java`)*:<br>• In each exported `<Lap>` element, the system SHALL serialize the lap's custom `name` and/or `description` if present.<br>• *Schema Sequence Compliance*: In accordance with `TrainingCenterDatabasev2.xsd` (`ActivityLap_t`), lap notes and extensions MUST be placed immediately after the closing `</Track>` tag and before `</Lap>`.<br>• *Universal Human-Readable `<Notes>`*: The system SHALL write a `<Notes>` element formatted as: `[Name] Description` (if both exist), `[Name]` (if only name exists), or `Description` (if only description exists).<br>• *Lossless Structured `<Extensions>`*: To guarantee 100% round-trip fidelity between aTrainingTracker instances, the system SHALL write a structured extension: `<Extensions><att:LapExtension xmlns:att="http://atrainingtracker.com/xmlschemas/TrainingCenterDatabaseExtensions/v1"><att:Name>...</att:Name><att:Description>...</att:Description></att:LapExtension></Extensions>`.<br>• *Activity-Level Notes*: If the workout has an overall description (`WorkoutSummaries.DESCRIPTION`), the system SHALL serialize it under `<Activity>` in `<Notes>` immediately following all `<Lap>` elements and before `<Creator>`.<br>• *XML Character Escaping*: All text content within `<Notes>`, `<att:Name>`, and `<att:Description>` MUST be strictly sanitized for XML special characters (`&amp;`, `&lt;`, `&gt;`, `&quot;`, `&apos;`).<br>• *Query Optimization*: The system SHALL pre-load all lap records for the exported workout in a single batch query (`LapsDatabaseManager.getLaps(workoutID)`) to eliminate N+1 SQLite queries during trackpoint processing.<br>2. *TCX Import Engine Parsing (`LegacyImportEngine.kt`)*:<br>• The system SHALL extend `ParsedLap` with `name: String?` and `description: String?`.<br>• *Parser Context Isolation*: The XML parser SHALL isolate lap-level `<Notes>` from activity-level `<Notes>`: a `<Notes>` element parsed while inside a `<Lap>` context MUST be assigned to the active `ParsedLap`, and a `<Notes>` element parsed outside a `<Lap>` context MUST be assigned to the workout's overall `workoutNotes`.<br>• *Format Parsing*: If `<att:Name>` / `<Name>` or `<att:Description>` / `<Description>` tags are encountered inside `<Lap>`, they SHALL directly populate `ParsedLap.name` and `ParsedLap.description`. Otherwise, when `<Notes>` is parsed, if it matches the bracket format `^\[(.*?)\](?:\s*(.*))?$`, group 1 SHALL be extracted as `name` and group 2 as `description`. If no brackets are present: if the text contains a newline, line 1 (if $\le 40$ chars) SHALL be treated as `name` and the remainder as `description`; if a single line $\le 40$ chars, it SHALL be treated as `name`; otherwise as `description`.<br>• *Database Persistence*: When creating lap records in `Laps.db` (`LapsDatabaseManager.saveLap(...)`), the system SHALL persist the parsed `name` and `description` alongside split metrics.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout with annotated laps (e.g. Lap 1: Name="Warm-up", Desc="Zone 1 spin"; Lap 2: Name="Interval", Desc="Hard effort") and an overall workout description,<br>• *When* the workout is exported via `TCXFileWriter`,<br>• *Then* the generated TCX file SHALL contain valid `<Notes>` and `<Extensions>` within each `<Lap>` element strictly after `</Track>`, and `<Notes>` under `<Activity>`, conforming to TCX schema.<br>• *Given* the generated TCX file,<br>• *When* it is imported via `LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx`,<br>• *Then* all laps in `Laps.db` SHALL be restored with their exact `name` and `description`, and the workout summary SHALL retain the overall description.<br>• *Given* a third-party TCX file containing standard `<Notes>[Tempo] Cadence 95</Notes>` or `<Notes>Sprint</Notes>`,<br>• *When* it is imported,<br>• *Then* the lap name and description SHALL be correctly extracted and stored.<br>• *Given* lap names or descriptions containing special characters (e.g. `Hill & Climb <Sprint> "Test"`),<br>• *When* exported and imported,<br>• *Then* XML escaping SHALL prevent parse errors and restore the exact original text string.<br><br>**Invariants**: Quantitative lap metrics (`time_total_s`, `distance_total_m`, `speed_average_mps`) MUST NOT be altered; files lacking lap notes MUST import cleanly with null name/desc; backup database archiving (`.attbackup`) MUST remain unaffected. | Guarantee complete data preservation and interoperability of customized lap labels, quick tags, and coaching notes across exports, cloud synchronizations, and imports. | `TCXFileWriter.java`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-DAT-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-DAT-013** | **TCX Workout Name & Description Bidirectional Export & Import.** | The system SHALL support bidirectional serialization and deserialization of the workout name (`WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` / `exportName`) and workout description (`WorkoutSummaries.DESCRIPTION`) in Garmin TCX v2 files (`TrainingCenterDatabasev2.xsd`), ensuring that custom workout names and descriptions are preserved across export and import cycles and protected against cluster-name overwriting:<br>1. *TCX Export Serialization (`BaseFileWriter.java` & `TCXFileWriter.java`)*:<br>• `BaseFileWriter.getHeaderData()` SHALL query and populate `workoutName` from `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` (`"exportName"`).<br>• *Universal Human-Readable `<Activity><Notes>`*: Under `<Activity Sport="...">`, the system SHALL serialize a `<Notes>` element immediately following all `<Lap>` elements (and before `<Creator>` / `<Extensions>`). If both workout name and description exist, it SHALL write `[Workout Name] Description`; if only name exists, `[Workout Name]`; if only description exists, `Description`.<br>• *Lossless Structured `<Activity><Extensions>`*: In accordance with TCX schema sequence rules (immediately following `<Creator>`, or after `<Lap>` if `<Creator>` omitted), the system SHALL write a structured extension: `<Extensions><att:ActivityExtension xmlns:att="http://atrainingtracker.com/xmlschemas/TrainingCenterDatabaseExtensions/v1"><att:Name>Workout Name</att:Name><att:Description>Description</att:Description></att:ActivityExtension></Extensions>`.<br>• *XML Character Escaping*: All text content within `<Notes>`, `<att:Name>`, and `<att:Description>` MUST be strictly sanitized for XML special characters (`&amp;`, `&lt;`, `&gt;`, `&quot;`, `&apos;`).<br>2. *TCX Import Parsing & Persistence (`LegacyImportEngine.kt`)*:<br>• The system SHALL track both `workoutName` and `workoutNotes` during TCX parsing.<br>• *Format Parsing*: If `<att:ActivityExtension>` with `<att:Name>` / `<att:Description>` or Garmin `<Training><Plan><Name>` is encountered, it SHALL populate `workoutName` and `workoutNotes` directly. Otherwise, when activity-level `<Notes>` is parsed, if it matches bracket format `^\[(.*?)\](?:\s*(.*))?$`, group 1 SHALL be extracted as `workoutName` and group 2 as `workoutNotes`. If no brackets are present and a single line $\le 60$ chars or line 1 $\le 60$ chars is encountered, line 1 SHALL be treated as `workoutName` and remainder as `workoutNotes`.<br>• *Database Persistence*: When creating or updating the workout record in `WorkoutSummaries` (`recalculateStats`), the system SHALL persist the extracted `workoutName` into `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` (falling back to `baseFileName` only if no name was provided).<br>• *Cluster Overwrite Immunity*: Because `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` is restored to the explicit custom name ($\ne `fileBaseName`), `WorkoutClusterEngine.assignClusterToWorkout()` line 627 (`if (forceIdentity || currentName.isNullOrEmpty() || currentName == fileBaseName)`) SHALL evaluate to false, thereby protecting the user's custom workout name from being overwritten by a cluster name.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout with a custom name (e.g., "Morning Intervals") and description (e.g., "Felt strong today"),<br>• *When* exported to TCX via `TCXFileWriter`,<br>• *Then* the generated XML SHALL contain `<Notes>[Morning Intervals] Felt strong today</Notes>` and `<att:ActivityExtension>` with `<att:Name>` and `<att:Description>`.<br>• *Given* the generated TCX file,<br>• *When* deleted and re-imported via `LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx`,<br>• *Then* the workout SHALL be stored with `WORKOUT_NAME = "Morning Intervals"` and `DESCRIPTION = "Felt strong today"`.<br>• *Given* a cluster assignment run on the newly imported workout,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutClusterEngine` SHALL NOT overwrite the custom workout name with the cluster name.<br>• *Given* a 3rd party TCX file with `<Training><Plan><Name>Tempo Run</Name></Training>` or `<Notes>[Long Ride] Sunday morning</Notes>`,<br>• *When* imported,<br>• *Then* `WORKOUT_NAME` SHALL be set to the extracted name.<br><br>**Invariants**: Lap-level notes parsing (`REQ-DAT-012`), sensor extrema and time/distance metrics (`REQ-DAT-001`), and automatic cluster creation (`REQ-ROU-001`) MUST NOT be altered. | Prevent data loss of custom workout titles across TCX export/import cycles and eliminate unwanted cluster name overwrites. | `BaseFileWriter.java`, `TCXFileWriter.java`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt` | `TST-DAT-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-123** | **Declarative Compose Navigation Drawer & High-Density Presentation.** | The navigation drawer content SHALL be implemented in Jetpack Compose (`AppNavigationDrawer.kt`) hosted inside the existing Activity `DrawerLayout` via `ComposeView`. The dynamic tracking status ("Start Tracking", "Tracking", "Pause") SHALL be driven by reactive Compose state (`State<Int>`) rather than legacy menu mutations. The legacy XML `NavigationView` inflation SHALL be eliminated to prevent reflection-based `InflateException` crashes (resolving ATT-516). | Eliminate XML inflation crash risk, modernize UI architecture to Jetpack Compose, and provide ergonomic high-density drawer navigation. | `main_activity_with_navigation.xml`, `AppNavigationDrawer.kt`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.java` | `TST-NAV-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-124** | **Kotlin Modernization of Navigation Activity (`MainActivityWithNavigation`).** | The application's primary navigation host activity SHALL be migrated from Java to idiomatic Kotlin (`MainActivityWithNavigation.kt`). The Kotlin implementation MUST maintain 100% functional and binary interoperability:<br>1. *Interface Compliance*: The activity MUST implement `BANALService.GetBanalServiceInterface`, `PreferenceFragmentCompat.OnPreferenceStartScreenCallback`, and `StartOrResumeInterface`.<br>2. *Companion Object & Java Interop*: Constants `SELECTED_FRAGMENT_ID`, `SELECTED_FRAGMENT`, and `EXTRA_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_WORKOUT` MUST be exposed as `@JvmField` in a companion object to ensure zero disruption to Java callers (`TrackerService`, dialogs).<br>3. *Navigation & Routing Integrity*: The activity MUST retain identical routing in `navigateToDrawerItem(itemId)` across all 21 items, including modal settings dialog dispatch (`ActivityTypeSelectionHelper`, `DisplaySettingsDialogFragment`, `UnitsSettingsDialogFragment`, `ExportSettingsDialogFragment`) and fragment transactions.<br>4. *Lifecycle & Service Binding*: The activity MUST manage broadcast receivers (`mStartTrackingReceiver`, `mPauseTrackingReceiver`, `mStopTrackingReceiver`, `mTrackingStoppedReceiver`, `mAntDependencyReceiver`) in `onResume()` / `onPause()`, BANALService binding with delayed disconnect, and window insets dispatch to `compose_nav_view`.<br>5. *Back Gesture Invariant*: Back gesture when the drawer is open MUST dismiss the drawer without closing the activity.<br>**Invariants**: Manifest declaration, launch mode (`singleTask`), theme (`Theme.aTrainingTracker`), and fragment state restoration contracts MUST remain identical. | Modernize core activity codebase to Kotlin, improve null safety, and streamline Compose interop while preserving all existing behavioral and lifecycle contracts. | `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml` | `TST-NAV-008` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-125** | **Period Summaries Empty State Presentation.** | The period summaries screen (`PeriodList.kt` / `PeriodsTabsScreen.kt`) SHALL display an informative empty state presentation whenever no period summary records exist (`periods.isEmpty()`):<br>1. *Visual Hierarchy & Component Reuse*: The system SHALL present an `EmptyStatePlaceholder` component (standardized with `WorkoutList`, `RouteList`, `SegmentList`, and `WorkoutClustersList`) featuring a calendar/history icon (`Icons.Default.DateRange`), a localized headline message (`no_periods_available`), and a supportive description guiding the user.<br>2. *Layout Insets & Alignment*: The empty state placeholder SHALL be vertically centered within the content viewport below the collapsible header, properly accounting for top collapsing header offset padding and bottom system navigation bar insets.<br>3. *Localization Parity*: All user-facing strings SHALL be externalized and translated across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* an empty period list (`periods.isEmpty()`) for any temporal tab (Week, Month, Year) or sport filter,<br>• *When* the user views the Periods screen,<br>• *Then* the system SHALL render the `EmptyStatePlaceholder` with the localized message and icon instead of an uninformative blank screen.<br>• *Given* a period list containing one or more periods,<br>• *When* the user views the Periods screen,<br>• *Then* the system SHALL render the period summary items and bar graph normally.<br>**Invariants**: Period bar graph, temporal tab switching, collapsing header behavior, sport filtering, and period card layout for non-empty lists MUST NOT be altered. | Provide clear, consistent user feedback when no training data is available for the selected period or filter, eliminating confusion caused by a blank screen. | `PeriodList.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-PER-017` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-126** | **Elevation Profile Chart Bounds Sanitization, Containment & Plausibility Protection.** | The `ElevationProfile` component SHALL sanitize effective vertical bounds (`min` and `max`) against recorded stream points (`pathPointsDownsampled`) such that the rendered vertical scale strictly envelopes all rendered and smoothed points without vertical clipping or cropping.<br>1. *Strict Containment Invariant*: The effective minimum bound `min` SHALL NEVER be greater than `streamMin` (even if an override is provided), and the effective maximum bound `max` SHALL NEVER be less than `streamMax`. If `minAltitudeOverride > streamMin`, it SHALL be clamped down to `streamMin`. If `maxAltitudeOverride < streamMax`, it SHALL be clamped up to `streamMax`.<br>2. *Outlier Clamping*: If `minAltitudeOverride < streamMin - outlierToleranceMeters`, it SHALL be clamped to `streamMin`. If `maxAltitudeOverride > streamMax + outlierToleranceMeters`, it SHALL be clamped to `streamMax`.<br>3. *Aesthetic Minimum Span Preservation*: When expanding a narrow elevation profile to `minSpanMeters` (20.0m) centered around the midpoint, `expandedMin` SHALL NOT exceed `streamMin` and `expandedMax` SHALL NOT be less than `streamMax`.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* an elevation profile with points dipping to 360 m and an override of 364 m or 368 m,<br>• *When* `ElevationProfile` renders the chart,<br>• *Then* `bounds.min` SHALL be <= 360 m, and NO point on the profile line SHALL extend below the bottom axis or bound.<br>• *Given* a flat sea-level route (0 m to 3 m) with corrupted overrides (-63 m, +60 m),<br>• *Then* bounds SHALL clamp to [-8.5 m, 11.5 m] (20 m span) enveloping all points.<br>**Invariants**: Visual grade legend (`REQ-MAP-010`) and distance step calculation MUST NOT be altered. | Guarantee that elevation profile curves never drop below the chart baseline or clip above the chart ceiling. | `ElevationProfile.kt` | `TST-UI-079` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-127** | **Delete Old Workouts Compose Dialog & Action Trigger.** | The `WorkoutTabsScreen` and `WorkoutListActions` components SHALL provide a dedicated action button and dialog for triggering bulk deletion of old workouts:<br>1. *Action Trigger*: An action button featuring icon `ic_baseline_delete_sweep_24` and localized content description (`@string/deleteOldWorkouts`) SHALL be displayed in `WorkoutListActions` in the workouts header.<br>2. *Dialog Interface*: Tapping the action button SHALL display `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog` containing:<br>• Localized headline: `@string/deleteOldWorkouts` ("Delete old workouts").<br>• Localized explanatory prompt: `@string/deleteWorkoutsThatAreOlderThanDays` ("delete all workouts that are older than … days").<br>• Numeric input field (`OutlinedTextField`) defaulting to 365 days (driven by `@string/defaultDaysToKeep`), configured with `KeyboardType.Number`.<br>• *Input Validation*: The confirm button SHALL be disabled unless the input is a valid non-negative integer ($D \ge 0$).<br>• *Dismissal & Confirmation*: Tapping dismiss ("Cancel") SHALL close the dialog without action; tapping confirm ("Delete" / "OK") SHALL close the dialog and dispatch `executeDeleteOldWorkouts(daysToKeep)` to the ViewModel.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the user is on the Workouts tabbed screen,<br>• *When* the user taps the delete sweep action button,<br>• *Then* the dialog SHALL appear with default value 365.<br>• *Given* an invalid input (e.g. negative number, empty text, or non-numeric),<br>• *Then* the confirm button SHALL be disabled.<br>• *Given* a valid number (e.g. 180) and tapping confirm,<br>• *Then* `executeDeleteOldWorkouts(180)` SHALL be invoked and the dialog dismissed.<br>**Invariants**: Header title row height (`LayoutConstants.HEADER_TITLE_ROW_HEIGHT`) and existing actions (compact view toggle, sort menu) MUST remain responsive and unobstructed. | Re-enable user-friendly bulk cleanup of old workouts in the modernized Jetpack Compose interface with clear confirmation and input validation. | `WorkoutListActions.kt`, `WorkoutTabsScreen.kt`, `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesViewModel.kt`, `strings.xml` | `TST-UI-080` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-128** | **Bulk Workout Deletion Progress Feedback & Notification.** | While bulk-deleting workouts older than $D$ days, the system SHALL provide continuous, observable progress feedback both within the user interface and via system notification:<br>1. *In-App Progress Dialog*: The system SHALL present a non-dismissible modal Compose dialog (`WorkoutDeletionProgressDialog`) upon initiating bulk deletion, preventing conflicting user interactions during database mutations and cache resynchronization.<br>2. *Granular Progress Reporting*: During deletion (Phase 1), the dialog and notification SHALL display the localized deletion title (`@string/deleteOldWorkouts`), the specific workout session currently being deleted (`@string/deleting_workout`), a numeric counter ($X / Y$), and a linear progress bar indicating fractional completion ($X / Y$).<br>3. *Downstream Finalization Indicator*: During analytical cache resynchronization and cluster integrity verification (Phase 2), the dialog SHALL display a progress indicator (circular spinner) and status message (`@string/please_wait`) until all background database operations conclude.<br>4. *Foreground / System Notification*: The system SHALL post an ongoing notification (`NotificationCompat.Builder`) with `CATEGORY_PROGRESS` and `setOngoing(true)` to keep the user informed if the application is backgrounded during deletion. The notification SHALL be dismissed immediately upon completion.<br>5. *Lifecycle & State Safety*: In both normal completion and error scenarios, the system MUST guarantee reset of deletion state to `Idle`, ensuring the user interface is never locked in an unrecoverable modal state.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the user confirms deletion of old workouts in `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog`,<br>• *When* deletion is executing,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutTabsScreen` SHALL display the modal progress dialog showing the current workout name, progress $X / Y$, and a progress bar.<br>• *Given* deletion of records finishes and cache resync begins,<br>• *Then* the progress dialog SHALL transition to the finalization phase showing the spinner and wait message.<br>• *Given* all operations complete,<br>• *Then* the progress dialog and system notification SHALL automatically dismiss and the list SHALL refresh.<br>**Invariants**: Deletion order and period/cluster cache invariants (`REQ-DAT-010`, `REQ-DAT-011`) MUST NOT be altered; non-bulk deletion MUST NOT display bulk progress dialog. | Prevent perceived application freezes during lengthy bulk database operations and keep users clearly informed of ongoing data purges and cache resynchronizations. | `WorkoutDeletionProgressDialog.kt`, `WorkoutTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesTabbedFragment.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesViewModel.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `WorkoutDeletionHelper.java` | `TST-UI-081` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-129** | **Bulk Workout Deletion Confirmation Dialog.** | Prior to executing bulk deletion of old workouts, the system SHALL require explicit user confirmation to prevent accidental and irreversible data loss:<br>1. *Two-Step Deletion Flow*: Upon the user specifying a valid retention threshold ($D \ge 0$ days) in `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog` and clicking the proceed action, the system SHALL NOT initiate deletion immediately. Instead, the system SHALL display an explicit confirmation dialog (`DeleteConfirmationDialog`).<br>2. *Confirmation Dialog Content*: The confirmation dialog SHALL present:<br>• Localized title: `@string/deleteOldWorkouts` ("delete old workouts").<br>• Localized warning prompt explicitly stating the retention threshold: `@string/really_delete_old_workouts_format` ("Do you really want to delete all workouts older than %1$d days?").<br>• Confirm action button: `@string/delete` ("delete").<br>• Dismiss action button: `@string/Cancel` ("Cancel").<br>3. *Cancellation Safety*: If the user dismisses the confirmation dialog (tapping "Cancel", tapping outside, or pressing the system back gesture), the deletion flow MUST terminate without modifying the database, and zero workouts SHALL be deleted.<br>4. *Confirmed Execution*: Only when the user explicitly clicks the confirmation button SHALL `onDeleteOldWorkouts(daysToKeep)` be dispatched, triggering the progress notification and deletion process (`REQ-UI-128`, `REQ-DAT-010`).<br>5. *Localization Parity*: All user-facing strings for the confirmation dialog SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the user is on the Workouts tabbed screen and opens `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog`,<br>• *When* the user specifies a retention threshold (e.g. 180 days) and taps the proceed button,<br>• *Then* the retention input dialog SHALL close and the confirmation dialog SHALL appear displaying the specified threshold (180 days).<br>• *Given* the confirmation dialog is visible,<br>• *When* the user taps "Cancel",<br>• *Then* the dialog SHALL dismiss, and zero database deletion operations SHALL be executed.<br>• *Given* the confirmation dialog is visible,<br>• *When* the user taps "Delete",<br>• *Then* `executeDeleteOldWorkouts(180)` SHALL be dispatched and the progress dialog (`REQ-UI-128`) SHALL appear.<br>**Invariants**: Single workout deletion (`WorkoutDeleteDialog`) MUST remain unaltered; retention threshold validation ($D \ge 0$) from `REQ-UI-127` MUST remain enforced; no background deletion SHALL start before explicit confirmation. | Prevent accidental, irreversible loss of historical workout data and telemetry through explicit user confirmation of bulk purge parameters. | `WorkoutTabsScreen.kt`, `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog.kt`, `DeleteConfirmationDialog.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-082` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-130** | **Sport-Specific Equipment Selection & In-Place Expansion in Edit Workout Dialog.** | Within the workout editing dialog (`EditWorkoutScreen` / `EditWorkoutViewModel`), the system SHALL strictly partition available equipment options by the active workout's base sport type (`currentBSportType`):<br>1. *Base Sport Type Partitioning (`BSportType` Firewall)*:<br>• For `BSportType.BIKE`: Available equipment SHALL be constrained strictly to bicycles (`frameType` $\in 1..4$, `sportType == BIKE`). Running shoes and other incompatible equipment MUST NOT be included.<br>• For `BSportType.RUN`: Available equipment SHALL be constrained strictly to running shoes (`frameType == 0`, `sportType == RUN`). Bicycles and other incompatible equipment MUST NOT be included.<br>• For `BSportType.UNKNOWN` / `OTHER`: The equipment dropdown SHALL display only `- none -` (plus any explicitly linked equipment configured for that specific sport).<br>2. *Three-Case Hierarchy Based on Linked Equipment Count ($N$)*:<br>• **Case 1 ($N > 1$: Multiple equipment items linked to the sport)**: The dropdown options list SHALL present: `- none -`, the linked equipment items ($L$), and the expansion item (`+ all bikes +` for cycling, `+ all shoes +` for running). Selecting the expansion item SHALL expand the dropdown options list in-place (via `stayOpenOn`) to display `- none -` and all equipment belonging to that base sport ($A$).<br>• **Case 2 ($N == 1$: Exactly one equipment item linked to the sport)**: The dropdown options list SHALL present: `- none -` and all equipment of that base sport ($A$). The uniquely linked equipment item SHALL be automatically preselected.<br>• **Case 3 ($N == 0$: No equipment items linked to the sport)**: The dropdown options list SHALL present: `- none -` and all equipment of that base sport ($A$). `- none -` SHALL be preselected (unless the workout already has an assigned equipment item that is valid for this sport).<br>3. *Cross-Sport Switching Invariants*:<br>• When the user changes the sport type to a different `BSportType` (e.g. Cycling $\rightarrow$ Running): Any previously selected equipment item SHALL be cleared (since bicycles cannot be assigned to running workouts). If the new sport has $N == 1$ linked equipment, that linked item SHALL be preselected; if $N == 0$, `- none -` SHALL be preselected.<br>• When the user changes the sport type within the same `BSportType` (e.g. Road Cycling $\rightarrow$ Mountain Biking): If the new sport has $N == 1$ linked equipment, that linked equipment SHALL be preselected; otherwise, if the current equipment is valid for the base sport, it SHALL be retained.<br>4. *Cross-Sport Backdoor Elimination*:<br>• The generic token `+ all equipment +` and unfiltered fallback to `equipmentRepository.equipmentList` SHALL be eliminated from `EditWorkoutViewModel`, preventing cross-sport gear pollution.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a cycling workout with 2 linked bikes (Bike A, Bike B) and 1 unlinked bike (Bike C),<br>• *When* the equipment dropdown is opened,<br>• *Then* options SHALL be `[- none -, Bike A, Bike B, + all bikes +]`.<br>• *When* `+ all bikes +` is tapped,<br>• *Then* options SHALL expand in-place to `[- none -, Bike A, Bike B, Bike C]`, with zero running shoes displayed.<br>• *Given* a running workout with 1 linked shoe (Shoe 1) and 2 unlinked shoes (Shoe 2, Shoe 3),<br>• *When* the dialog opens,<br>• *Then* options SHALL be `[- none -, Shoe 1, Shoe 2, Shoe 3]` and `Shoe 1` SHALL be preselected.<br>• *Given* a cycling workout with 0 linked bikes,<br>• *When* the dialog opens for a new/unassigned workout,<br>• *Then* options SHALL be `[- none -, <all bikes>]` and `- none -` SHALL be preselected.<br>• *Given* an active workout with Bike A selected,<br>• *When* the user changes the sport to Running,<br>• *Then* the selected equipment SHALL NOT remain Bike A, but SHALL switch to the linked shoe ($N == 1$) or `- none -` ($N == 0$).<br>**Invariants**: `SportTypeEquipmentLinkManager` and `EquipmentDbHelper` data persistence MUST NOT be altered; existing valid equipment assignments on workouts matching their sport MUST NOT be overwritten without user action. | Prevent cross-sport equipment contamination and restore intuitive sport-specific equipment preselection in the edit workout screen. | `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`, `EditWorkoutScreen.kt` | `TST-UI-083` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-131** | **Compact Strava Segment Efforts Presentation with Expandable Accordion in Workout Summary.** | Within the Strava activity results section of the workout summary card (`StravaActivitySection.kt`), the system SHALL provide a smart expandable presentation for segment efforts to prevent summary card bloat while preserving full access to all segment details:<br>1. *Segment Effort Classification*:<br>A segment effort is classified as *Highlight* (important) if:<br>• It represents a Personal Record (`prRank != null`, where `prRank` $\in \{1, 2, 3\}$, parsed directly or from `achievements`), OR<br>• It represents a Course Record / Overall Rank (`komRank != null`, parsed directly or from `achievements`), OR<br>• The segment is starred by the user on Strava (`isStarred == true`, `starred_date` present, or locally synchronized in `SegmentsDatabaseManager` by ID or name).<br>All other segment efforts are classified as *Standard*.<br>2. *Collapse Eligibility Threshold ($K = 4$)*:<br>The expandable accordion SHALL be activated for a workout if and only if:<br>• Total segment efforts count exceeds threshold $K$ ($N > 4$), AND<br>• Total segment efforts count exceeds the count of highlighted segments ($N > N_{\text{highlight}}$).<br>If $N \le 4$ OR if all segments are highlighted ($N == N_{\text{highlight}}$), all segment efforts SHALL be displayed directly without expansion controls.<br>3. *Default Compact View*:<br>When eligible for collapse, the section SHALL default to collapsed state (`isExpanded == false`), displaying:<br>• If $N_{\text{highlight}} > 0$: Strictly the highlighted segment efforts (PRs, KOMs, Starred).<br>• If $N_{\text{highlight}} == 0$: A localized note indicating no starred segments or PRs (`@string/strava_no_highlights`) with zero segment rows.<br>• Directly below the displayed segments, an expansion action button SHALL be rendered: e.g. `"▼ Show all %1$d segments (+%2$d more)"` (`@string/strava_show_all_segments_format`), indicating total count ($N$) and hidden count ($N - N_{\text{highlight}}$).<br>4. *In-Place Expansion & Collapse Toggle*:<br>• Tapping the expansion action SHALL immediately expand the list in-place to display all $N$ segment efforts without navigating away or closing the card.<br>• While expanded (`isExpanded == true`), the control button SHALL toggle to a collapse action: e.g. `"▲ Show only starred & PRs (%1$d)"` (`@string/strava_show_less_segments_format`), displaying $N_{\text{highlight}}$.<br>• Tapping the collapse action SHALL collapse the list back to the highlighted segments.<br>• Expansion state SHALL be preserved during lazy scrolling via `rememberSaveable`.<br>5. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*:<br>All user-facing action labels and formatting strings SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a workout with 15 segment efforts where 3 are PRs and 1 is starred (4 highlights, 11 standard),<br>• *When* the workout summary is rendered,<br>• *Then* exactly 4 highlighted segment efforts SHALL be visible, followed by an expansion button `"▼ Show all 15 segments (+11 more)"`.<br>• *When* the user taps the expansion button,<br>• *Then* all 15 segment efforts SHALL be displayed, and the button label SHALL become `"▲ Show only starred & PRs (4)"`.<br>• *When* the user taps the collapse button,<br>• *Then* the list SHALL collapse back to 4 segments.<br>• *Given* a workout with 7 segment efforts where 0 are PRs or starred,<br>• *When* the workout summary is rendered in collapsed view,<br>• *Then* zero segment rows and `@string/strava_no_highlights` SHALL be visible, followed by `"▼ Show all 7 segments (+7 more)"`.<br>• *Given* a workout with 3 segment efforts,<br>• *When* the summary is rendered,<br>• *Then* all 3 segments SHALL be displayed directly with NO expansion button.<br>• *Given* a workout with 8 segment efforts where all 8 are PRs or starred,<br>• *When* the summary is rendered,<br>• *Then* all 8 segments SHALL be displayed directly with NO expansion button.<br>**Invariants**: Best Efforts section, external Strava activity click launch (`StravaHelper.openActivity`), and Strava branding requirements (`PoweredByStrava`) MUST remain fully intact. | Prevent summary card bloat on multi-segment activities while highlighting personal achievements and starred segments by default. | `StravaActivitySection.kt`, `StravaActivityData.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-084` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-132** | **Multi-Dimensional Workout Filtering, Modal Filter Sheet & Persistent Filter State.** | The system SHALL provide multi-dimensional filtering for workout sessions on the tabbed workout list (`WorkoutTabsScreen`):<br>1. *Action Trigger & Active Indicator*: The header action row (`WorkoutListActions`) SHALL display a dedicated filter action button with the standard Material funnel icon (`Icons.Default.FilterAlt`) and localized content description. When one or more filter criteria are active (`isFilterActive == true`), the action icon SHALL display a visual badge indicating the active filter count.<br>2. *Filter Dimension Set*: The system SHALL support filtering across orthogonal dimensions encapsulated in immutable `WorkoutFilterCriteria`:<br>• *Text Search*: Case-insensitive substring matching against workout title, description, and notes.<br>• *Date & Time Period*: Year selection, Month, and/or Custom Date Range (`startDateS` to `endDateS`).<br>• *Sport Sub-Type*: Specific sport ID (`sportTypeId`).<br>• *Equipment / Gear*: Specific equipment ID (`equipmentId`).<br>• *Workout Flags & Attributes*: Toggle filters for Commute (`commute == true`), Trainer / Indoor (`trainer == true`), Has GPS Track (`mapPolyline.isNotEmpty()`), and Cluster ID (`clusterId`).<br>• *Distance & Duration Thresholds*: Minimum distance threshold ($D \ge D_{\text{min}}$ in meters) and/or minimum active duration threshold ($T \ge T_{\text{min}}$ in seconds).<br>3. *Modal Filter Bottom Sheet (`WorkoutFilterBottomSheet`)*: Tapping the filter action button SHALL present a modal bottom sheet containing organized controls for each filter dimension. The bottom sheet SHALL be properly anchored to the bottom edge without bottom gap artifacts when dragged. The sheet SHALL feature:<br>• A dedicated top header row with localized title and close dismiss button (`Icons.Default.Close`).<br>• A dedicated bottom action row providing distinct "Reset all" (clear) and "Apply" / "OK" action buttons with prominent touch targets.<br>4. *Removable Active Filter Chip Strip*: When one or more filter criteria are active, the system SHALL render a horizontal scrolling chip strip directly above the workout list (below the sport tabs). Each active dimension SHALL render a distinct chip with localized label and a trailing remove icon (`Icons.Default.Close`). Tapping the remove icon SHALL clear that specific filter dimension reactively without requiring the bottom sheet to be opened.<br>5. *Filter Lifecycle & Main View Reset*: Filter criteria SHALL remain active while navigating within the workouts section (switching tabs, editing workouts, inspecting track on map), but SHALL be automatically cleared when navigating back to the main view (or exiting the workouts section), ensuring subsequent visits start with the full unfiltered workout list.<br>6. *Reactive List Pipeline & Tab Interoperability*: `WorkoutSummariesViewModel` SHALL combine `allWorkouts`, `sortOrder`, and `filterCriteria` into the reactive `workouts` state flow. The top-level tabs (*All*, *Bike*, *Run*, *Other*) SHALL continue to partition workouts by `BSportType` while strictly respecting all active sub-filters.<br>7. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*: All filter category titles, action buttons, chip descriptors, and empty-state filter prompts SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the workout list header,<br>• *Then* the filter action SHALL display the funnel icon (`Icons.Default.FilterAlt`).<br>• *When* the user opens `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet`,<br>• *Then* the sheet SHALL anchor flush to the bottom edge without gaps, the top header SHALL display the title and a close button, and a separate bottom row SHALL display "Reset all" and "Apply".<br>• *Given* active filters applied to the workout list,<br>• *When* the user navigates back to the main view (e.g. Start Tracking),<br>• *Then* all active filters SHALL be cleared, and re-entering the workouts section SHALL show all workouts.<br>**Invariants**: Sorting orders (`WorkoutSortOrder.DATE`, `WORKOUT_DURATION`, `WORKOUT_DISTANCE`, `TOTAL_ELEVATION_GAIN`), compact/detailed view mode toggle, individual and bulk workout deletion workflows, and SQLite database schema MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Enable athletes with extensive workout histories to quickly search, slice, and inspect specific activities across time, gear, activity type, and physical attributes. | `WorkoutFilterCriteria.kt`, `WorkoutListActions.kt`, `WorkoutTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet.kt`, `ActiveFilterChipsRow.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesViewModel.kt`, `MyPreferenceManager.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-085` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-133** | **Multi-Dimensional Route Filtering, Shared Modal Filter Sheet & Persistent Filter State.** | The system SHALL provide multi-dimensional filtering for routes on the tabbed route list (`RouteTabbedScreen`):<br>1. *Action Trigger & Active Indicator*: The header action row (`RouteTabbedScreen`) SHALL display a dedicated filter action button positioned consistently at the end of the action row (`Select All` $\rightarrow$ `Import` $\rightarrow$ `Sync` $\rightarrow$ `Sort` $\rightarrow$ `Filter`) featuring the Material funnel icon (`Icons.Default.FilterAlt`) and localized content description. When one or more filter criteria are active (`isFilterActive == true`), the filter action button SHALL display a visual badge indicating the active filter count.<br>2. *Filter Dimension Set*: The system SHALL support filtering across orthogonal route dimensions encapsulated in immutable `RouteFilterCriteria`:<br>• *Text Search*: Case-insensitive substring matching against route `name` and `description`.<br>• *Route Source*: Specific route origin (`RouteSource.STRAVA`, `LOCAL_GPX`, or `WORKOUT`).<br>• *Map Selection*: Toggle filter for routes currently marked selected / visible on map (`isSelected == true`).<br>• *Distance Thresholds*: Minimum distance threshold ($D \ge D_{\text{min}}$ in meters: 10 km, 25 km, 50 km, 100 km).<br>• *Elevation Gain Thresholds*: Minimum elevation gain threshold ($E \ge E_{\text{min}}$ in meters: 100 m, 250 m, 500 m, 1000 m).<br>3. *Shared Modal Filter Bottom Sheet Architecture*: The system SHALL implement a shared layout shell (`FilterBottomSheetScaffold`) eliminating boilerplate and UI divergence between workout and route filters. The modal bottom sheet SHALL feature:<br>• A dedicated top header row with localized title and close dismiss button (`Icons.Default.Close`).<br>• Zero bottom gap artifacts when expanded or dragged, anchoring flush to the device navigation bar.<br>• A dedicated bottom action bar providing distinct "Reset all" (`OutlinedButton`) and "OK" (`Button`) action buttons with prominent touch targets.<br>4. *Removable Active Filter Chip Strip*: When one or more filter criteria are active, the system SHALL render a horizontal scrolling chip strip directly below the sport tabs. Tapping the remove icon SHALL clear that specific filter dimension reactively without opening the sheet.<br>5. *Filter Lifecycle & Main View Reset*: Filter criteria SHALL remain active while navigating within the routes section (switching tabs, editing routes, inspecting route on map), but SHALL be automatically cleared when navigating back to the main view (`drawer_start_tracking`) or switching drawer items in `MainActivityWithNavigation`, ensuring subsequent visits start with the full unfiltered route list.<br>6. *Reactive List Pipeline & Tab Interoperability*: `RoutesViewModel` SHALL combine `routesRepository.allRoutes`, `_sortOrder`, `currentLocation`, and `filterCriteria` into the reactive `routes` state flow. The top-level tabs (*All*, *Bike*, *Run*, *Other*) SHALL continue to partition routes by `BSportType` while strictly respecting all active sub-filters.<br>7. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*: All route filter category titles, action buttons, chip descriptors, and empty-state filter prompts SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the route list header,<br>• *Then* the filter action SHALL display the funnel icon (`Icons.Default.FilterAlt`) with badge counter when active.<br>• *When* the user opens `RouteFilterBottomSheet`,<br>• *Then* the sheet SHALL anchor flush to the bottom edge without gaps, displaying title, close button, filter controls, and separate bottom "Reset all" and "OK" buttons using `FilterBottomSheetScaffold`.<br>• *Given* active filters applied to the route list,<br>• *When* the user navigates back to the main view (e.g. Start Tracking),<br>• *Then* all active route filters SHALL be cleared, and re-entering the routes section SHALL show all routes.<br>**Invariants**: Sorting orders (`RouteSortOrder.DISTANCE_TO_USER`, `TOTAL_ELEVATION_GAIN`, `ROUTE_DISTANCE`, `NAME`), sport tabs, GPX import, Strava route sync, route deletion, route editing, and workout filtering (`REQ-UI-132`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Enable athletes with extensive route libraries to quickly find, slice, and inspect specific routes across origin, selection, distance, and elevation gain. | `RouteFilterCriteria.kt`, `FilterBottomSheetScaffold.kt`, `FilterActionButton.kt`, `RouteTabbedScreen.kt`, `RouteFilterBottomSheet.kt`, `ActiveRouteFilterChipsRow.kt`, `RoutesViewModel.kt`, `MyPreferenceManager.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-086` | Verified |
