| **REQ-UI-136** | **Favorite Tracks (Lieblingsstrecken) Sorting by Recordings, Distance to User, Length, and Name.** | The system SHALL provide flexible sorting capabilities for Favorite Tracks / Workout Clusters on the tabbed clusters list (`WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`):<br>1. *Action Trigger & Sort Menu*: The header action row (`WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`) SHALL display a dedicated sort action button (`Icons.Default.Sort`) with localized content description (`@string/sort`), positioned consistently between the tuning settings icon and the filter action button. Tapping the sort button SHALL open a dropdown menu (`DropdownMenu`) presenting all available sort options defined by `ClusterSortOrder`.<br>2. *Sort Dimensions (`ClusterSortOrder`)*:<br>• **Recordings / Frequency (`RECORDINGS`)** (Default): Sorts clusters by `hitCount` descending (most frequented favorite tracks first), with secondary tie-breaking by `name.lowercase()` ascending. Labeled via `@string/filter_section_recordings` ("Recordings" / "Aufzeichnungen").<br>• **Distance to User (`DISTANCE_TO_USER`)**: Sorts clusters by geodetic distance from current user GPS coordinates (`currentLocation`) to cluster start coordinates (`startLat`, `startLng`) ascending (closest first). If GPS location is unavailable or denied, this menu item SHALL be disabled and visually muted (`alpha = 0.38f`), with fallback sorting by `name.lowercase()`. Labeled via `@string/sort_closest` ("Closest" / "Entfernung").<br>• **Track Length / Reference Distance (`DISTANCE`)**: Sorts clusters by `refDistance` descending (longest tracks first), with secondary tie-breaking by `name.lowercase()` ascending. Labeled via `@string/sort_length` ("Length" / "Länge").<br>• **Name (`NAME`)**: Sorts clusters alphabetically by `name.lowercase()` ascending. Labeled via `@string/sort_name` ("Name").<br>3. *Selection State & Interaction*: The currently active sort order SHALL be indicated by a leading checkmark icon (`Icons.Default.Check`) in the dropdown menu. Selecting an option SHALL update the ViewModel's sort order state, re-sort the cluster list reactively, and dismiss the dropdown menu.<br>4. *Tab Interoperability & Scope*: Sorting SHALL apply reactively to the filtered cluster list across all sport tabs (*All*, *Bike*, *Run*, *Other*). The *Unclustered* tab SHALL remain ordered by workout date.<br>5. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*: All sort labels and content descriptions SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the favorite tracks header,<br>• *Then* the sort action button SHALL be visible between the tuning icon and filter icon.<br>• *When* the sort menu is opened,<br>• *Then* options `RECORDINGS`, `DISTANCE_TO_USER`, `DISTANCE`, and `NAME` SHALL appear, with a checkmark next to the active selection.<br>• *Given* GPS location is unavailable,<br>• *Then* the `DISTANCE_TO_USER` option SHALL be disabled and grayed out.<br>• *When* the user selects a sort order (e.g. `NAME`),<br>• *Then* the cluster list SHALL immediately reorder alphabetically.<br>**Invariants**: Existing filtering (`REQ-UI-135`), cluster tuning, manual cluster creation, deletion, and route/segment sorting (`RouteSortOrder`, `SegmentSortOrder`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Harmonize favorite track list usability with routes and segments, allowing athletes to quickly find recurring routes by frequency, proximity, length, or name. | `ClusterSortOrder.kt`, `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt` | `TST-UI-089` | Proposed |
| **REQ-UI-137** | **Cluster Algorithm & Lieblingsstrecken Educational Info Modal.** | The system SHALL provide athletes with structured, comprehensive in-app educational documentation explaining the workout clustering algorithm (detection of "Lieblingsstrecken" / Favorite Tracks):<br>1. *Action Triggers & Entry Points*:<br>• **Favorite Tracks Header (`WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`)**: The header action row SHALL display a dedicated info action button (`Icons.Outlined.Info`) with localized content description (`@string/cluster_info_title`), positioned in the action row alongside Tuning, Sort, and Filter. Tapping this button SHALL display `ClusterInfoDialog`.<br>• **Cluster Parameters / Tuning TopAppBar (`ClusterTuningScreen`)**: The top app bar SHALL display an info action button (`IconButton` with `Icons.Outlined.Info`) with localized content description (`@string/cluster_info_title`). Tapping this button SHALL display `ClusterInfoDialog`.<br>2. *Educational Modal Dialog Architecture (`ClusterInfoDialog`)*:<br>The modal dialog SHALL be implemented in Jetpack Compose (`AlertDialog`), featuring:<br>• **Title**: Prominently displays the localized headline `@string/cluster_info_title` ("Workout Clustering / Lieblingsstrecken").<br>• **Scrollable Content (`Column` with `verticalScroll`)**:<br>  - *Section 1: What are Lieblingsstrecken?* (`@string/cluster_info_what_title`, `@string/cluster_info_what_desc`): Explains automatic route grouping, smart naming using iconic country-specific lakes/courses (e.g. "Lake Tahoe Loop #5", „Chiemsee-Runde #5“), equipment tracking, and multi-session performance comparison across identical paths.<br>  - *Section 2: The 3D Topological Fingerprint* (`@string/cluster_info_fingerprint_title`, `@string/cluster_info_fingerprint_desc`): Details the simple but surprisingly good algorithm based on a 6-dimensional spatial metric: (a) Start point (200m), (b) End point (200m), (c) Apex / Max Line Distance point (furthest geographical deviation from start, 400m), (d) Track Length (relative tolerance, 20%), (e) Min Altitude Position (optional spatial 3D anchor, 400m), (f) Max Altitude Position (optional spatial 3D anchor, 400m).<br>  - *Section 3: Sport Awareness & Learning Centroid* (`@string/cluster_info_sports_title`, `@string/cluster_info_sports_desc`): Explains sport-type matching firewall (e.g. distinguishing Road Bike vs. Running) and dynamic running centroid coordinate adaptation as new workouts join the cluster.<br>  - *Section 4: Tuning & Sensitivity* (`@string/cluster_info_tuning_title`, `@string/cluster_info_tuning_desc`): Explains how to use the Master Sensitivity Slider (from Strict to Relaxed / Locker) and fine-tune individual tolerances for varied terrain.<br>• **Action Button**: A prominent "Close" button (`TextButton`) with localized label `@string/close` to dismiss the dialog.<br>3. *Responsive & Theme Integration*:<br>• Dialog SHALL adapt gracefully to dark and light Material 3 themes.<br>• Content MUST be vertically scrollable to guarantee full readability on compact screen heights and large font scales without truncation.<br>4. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*:<br>All educational text keys, section titles, descriptions, and action labels SHALL be fully translated and localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* the Favorite Tracks screen (`WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`),<br>• *When* the user taps the info icon in the header,<br>• *Then* `ClusterInfoDialog` SHALL appear displaying all 4 educational sections.<br>• *Given* the Cluster Tuning screen (`ClusterTuningScreen`),<br>• *When* the user taps the info action icon in the top app bar,<br>• *Then* `ClusterInfoDialog` SHALL appear displaying all 4 educational sections.<br>• *When* the user taps "Close" or outside the dialog,<br>• *Then* the dialog SHALL dismiss cleanly without state loss.<br>**Invariants**: Opening or dismissing the dialog MUST NOT alter cluster tolerance preferences, cluster database records, sorting orders, or filtering criteria. Existing cluster tuning sliders and switches MUST continue to operate without interference. | Educate athletes on the 3D topological algorithm behind favorite track discovery, empowering them to interpret cluster assignments and effectively tune tolerances. | `ClusterInfoDialog.kt`, `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen.kt`, `ClusterTuningScreen.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-090` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-138** | **Detailed Workout Inspection from Favorite Tracks with Full-Fidelity Tracks, Extrema, and Elevation Profiles.** | The system SHALL ensure that navigating from the Favorite Tracks / Workout Clusters view (`WorkoutClustersFragment` / `WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`) to inspect a specific workout session displays the complete, high-fidelity workout track with authentic elevation profiles, sensor extrema markers, and technical track layers:<br>1. *Workout Track & Telemetry Loading*: When a workout within a cluster or unclustered list is selected for detailed inspection, `WorkoutClustersViewModel` SHALL ensure `_clusterWorkouts` state contains the cluster's workouts so that `selectWorkoutForPeek(workout.id)` successfully resolves the workout, loads its complete track telemetry via `WorkoutClusterRepository.getWorkoutWithTrack(workout.id)`, and updates `_peekedWorkoutDataWithTrack`.<br>2. *Elevation Profile Fidelity*: The elevation profile (`ElevationProfile`) SHALL render the authentic altitude samples (`encodedAltitudes`) and distances (`encodedDistances`) of the inspected workout. The altitude axes, minimum/maximum bounds, and elevation gain SHALL accurately reflect recorded trackpoint elevation rather than default zero or flatline clamping (`[-10m, +10m]`).<br>3. *Fallback Track Point Preservation (`WorkoutData.toMapTrack()`)*: When `WorkoutData.toMapTrack()` converts a `WorkoutData` record to a `MapTrack`, it SHALL preserve available `encodedAltitudes` and `encodedDistances` by decoding them into corresponding `PathPoint(altitude, latLng, distance)` elements rather than hardcoding altitude and distance to `0.0`.<br>4. *Sensor Extrema & Technical Track Layers*: When inspecting a workout from the cluster view, the system SHALL initialize `TrackOnMapAftermathViewModel` with the inspected workout (`loadAftermathData(workout)`), ensuring that all sensor extrema pins (Altitude Min/Max, Speed Max, Heart Rate Max, Power Max), technical track layers (Fused, GPS, Network), segments, and route overlays are fully populated, selectable, and rendered on `TrackOnMapScreen`.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete viewing the workouts belonging to a Favorite Track (Lieblingsstrecke) or unclustered workouts,<br>• *When* the user taps a workout card to inspect it in the full-screen track map view (`TrackOnMapScreen`),<br>• *Then* the elevation profile at the bottom of the map SHALL display the workout's true altitude curve, with accurate elevation gain, minimum altitude, and maximum altitude matching the workout summary.<br>• *Then* the map SHALL display the authoritative extrema pins (Min Altitude, Max Altitude, Max Speed, and Max Heart Rate where recorded).<br>• *Then* the technical track layer controls (GPS, Fused, Network) SHALL reflect the recorded provider data and be toggleable.<br>• *Given* any `WorkoutData` instance containing valid `encodedAltitudes` and `encodedDistances`,<br>• *When* `workout.toMapTrack()` is invoked,<br>• *Then* the resulting `MapTrack.path` points SHALL contain the decoded altitude and distance values.<br><br>**Invariants**: Cluster list display, cluster grouping metrics, cluster sorting (`REQ-UI-136`), cluster filtering (`REQ-UI-135`), standalone workout summary detail screen (`WorkoutTabsScreen`), and live tracking map rendering MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Guarantee analytical consistency and eliminate visual regressions when inspecting recorded workouts from the Favorite Tracks interface, providing the identical depth of elevation and telemetry analysis as the primary workout history. | `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt`, `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt`, `MapModels.kt`, `TrackOnMapScreen.kt` | `TST-UI-091` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-139** | **Right-Aligned Edge FastScrollbar, Isolated Thumb Touch Target & Continuous Drag Accumulation.** | The system SHALL position the fast scrollbar (`FastScrollbar.kt`) flush against the right edge of the viewport, isolate drag gesture detection strictly to the draggable thumb, and maintain continuous drag displacement accumulation:<br>1. *Flush Edge Alignment*: In `WorkoutList.kt`, `FastScrollbar` SHALL be aligned flush to `Alignment.CenterEnd` with zero end padding (`padding(end = 0.dp)`), eliminating floating inward offsets.<br>2. *Right-Aligned Track & Ergonomic Thumb*: In `FastScrollbar.kt`, the track (`width = 4.dp`) SHALL be aligned to `Alignment.CenterEnd` flush against the viewport edge. The draggable thumb hit box SHALL be aligned to `Alignment.TopEnd` with an ergonomic touch target (`width = 28.dp`, `height = 48.dp`) containing an 8dp visual pill aligned to `Alignment.CenterEnd`, ensuring effortless grabbing while physically isolating the track from list cards (which end at `screenWidth - 8.dp`).<br>3. *Isolated Thumb Touch Target & Gesture Scoping*: Drag gesture detection (`detectDragGestures`) SHALL be scoped strictly to the draggable thumb composable, and MUST NOT be declared on the full-height outer container Box. The track area above and below the thumb SHALL NOT capture or consume pointer input, ensuring underlying list item action buttons (e.g. 3-dots export menu, edit button) receive click events without interference.<br>4. *Continuous Drag Accumulation & Zero-Latency Visual Tracking*: During drag gestures, the scrollbar SHALL maintain an active drag state (`isDragging: Boolean`, `dragProgress: Float`), continuously accumulating fractional displacement deltas without per-frame integer truncation loss ($\text{dragProgress} = (\text{dragProgress} + \text{dragAmount.y} / \text{maxOffsetPx}).\text{coerceIn}(0f, 1f)$). The visual thumb position during dragging SHALL be driven directly by `dragProgress` for zero-lag responsiveness, while dispatching `state.scrollToItem(targetIndex)` across all items ($0 \dots \text{totalItems} - 1$).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a scrollable list of workouts in `WorkoutList`,<br>• *When* the list is displayed,<br>• *Then* the fast scrollbar track and thumb SHALL sit flush against the right display edge.<br>• *Given* any list item card with right-aligned action buttons (such as the 3-dots menu or edit button in `WorkoutHeader`),<br>• *When* the user taps the button while the scrollbar thumb is at a different vertical position,<br>• *Then* the button click SHALL be triggered immediately and reliably without being intercepted by `FastScrollbar`.<br>• *Given* the fast scrollbar thumb,<br>• *When* the user touches and drags the thumb vertically at normal finger speed (small per-frame deltas),<br>• *Then* the thumb SHALL follow the finger with zero lag and the `LazyColumn` SHALL scroll smoothly across all items from first to last.<br><br>**Invariants**: Dynamic alpha fading (0.4f idle to 1.0f active scroll with 500ms tween), idle progress calculation from `firstVisibleItemIndex` / `firstVisibleItemScrollOffset`, and smooth scrolling via `scrollToItem` MUST NOT be regressed; list item layouts and action button callbacks MUST NOT be altered. | Eliminate click interception on right-aligned list actions while providing a responsive, edge-aligned fast scrollbar experience. | `FastScrollbar.kt`, `WorkoutList.kt` | `TST-UI-092` | Verified |

scrollable collection screens in the application:<br>1. *Periods (Zeiträume)*: In `PeriodList.kt`, `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to `scrollState`, respecting system navigation bar insets.<br>2. *Routes (Routen)*: In `RouteList.kt`, `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to `scrollState`, respecting collapsing app bar offset and system navigation insets.<br>3. *Favorite Tracks (Lieblingsstrecken)*: In `WorkoutClustersList.kt`, both `WorkoutClustersClusteredList` and `UnclusteredWorkoutsList` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to `scrollState`, respecting header offset and FAB bottom margin (`80.dp`).<br>4. *Segments (Segmente)*: In `SegmentList.kt`, `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to `scrollState`, respecting collapsing app bar offset and system navigation insets.<br>5. *Equipment (Ausrüstung)*: In `EquipmentTabsScreen.kt` (`EquipmentList`), `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to its `LazyListState`, respecting collapsing app bar offset and system navigation insets.<br>6. *Sport Types (Sportarten)*: In `SportTypesTabsScreen.kt`, `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to its `LazyListState`, respecting collapsing app bar offset and system navigation insets.<br>7. *Connected Devices (Geräte)*: In `DeviceListScreen.kt`, `LazyColumn` SHALL be accompanied by a flush right-aligned `FastScrollbar` mapped to `scrollState`, respecting search header offset and navigation insets.<br>8. *Universal Invariants & Behavior*:<br>• In all views, `FastScrollbar` MUST automatically hide when `totalItems <= visibleItems || totalItems == 0`.<br>• In all views, touch detection MUST remain strictly confined to the draggable thumb, ensuring all item click actions, checkboxes, switches, menus, and delete handlers remain 100% accessible with zero click interception.<br>• Fractional drag displacement accumulation and zero-latency visual thumb tracking MUST be preserved across all screens.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* any of the 7 collection screens (Periods, Routes, Favorite Tracks, Segments, Equipment, Sport Types, Devices),<br>• *When* the number of items exceeds the screen viewport,<br>• *Then* the edge-aligned `FastScrollbar` SHALL be visible on the right display edge.<br>• *When* the user touches and drags the scrollbar thumb vertically,<br>• *Then* the list SHALL scroll smoothly across all items from top to bottom.<br>• *Given* any collection screen where total items fit within the screen viewport,<br>• *Then* `FastScrollbar` SHALL remain completely hidden without altering list content bounds.<br>• *Given* any list item card in any collection screen with interactive action buttons (e.g. Route item, Cluster card, Equipment config/stats, Device pair switch),<br>• *When* the user taps the action button while the scrollbar thumb is at a different vertical position,<br>• *Then* the action click SHALL be triggered immediately and reliably without being intercepted by `FastScrollbar`.<br><br>**Invariants**: Existing list layouts, card layouts, collapsing toolbar connections, FAB placement, filtering, sorting, and database operations across all views MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Provide consistent, rapid, and effortless tactile navigation through large archives and catalogs across the entire application. | `PeriodList.kt`, `RouteList.kt`, `WorkoutClustersList.kt`, `SegmentList.kt`, `EquipmentTabsScreen.kt`, `SportTypesTabsScreen.kt`, `DeviceListScreen.kt` | `TST-UI-093` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-141** | **Workout Summary Lap Overview & Performance Highlights.** | The system SHALL display a structured overview of all recorded laps within workout summary cards (`WorkoutSummary.kt`) in the aftermath screen (`WorkoutTabsScreen` / `WorkoutList.kt`), featuring lap names, metrics, and split performance badges:<br>1. *Laps Section Placement & Visibility*: In `WorkoutSummary.kt`, a dedicated `WorkoutLaps` section SHALL be rendered directly after `WorkoutExtrema` and before `StravaActivitySection` / `WorkoutMediaSection`. When a workout contains 0 recorded laps, `WorkoutLaps` SHALL be completely omitted with zero vertical layout overhead.<br>2. *Split Table Layout & Formatting*: For workouts with $\ge 1$ laps, the section SHALL render a structured table displaying each lap's sequential order and metrics:<br>• *Lap Name*: Renders the lap's custom `name` if present and non-blank; otherwise falls back to localized default `"Lap X"` (where $X$ is the 1-based sequential lap index). If a custom description is present, a secondary note line SHALL be rendered beneath the lap name.<br>• *Duration & Distance*: Renders formatted split active duration (`formatters.time.format`) and split distance (`formatters.distance.format_with_units`).<br>• *Pace / Speed*: Renders sport-dependent pace for running and walking activities (`formatters.pace.format_with_units`), and speed for cycling and other sports (`formatters.speed.format_with_units`).<br>3. *Performance Highlight Badges (Rabbit 🐇 & Hedgehog 🦔)*:<br>For workouts containing $\ge 2$ valid laps (`speedAverageMps > 0`):<br>• *Fastest Lap (Rabbit)*: The lap possessing the highest average speed (`speedAverageMps`) SHALL be highlighted with a Rabbit badge (`🐇`).<br>• *Slowest Lap (Hedgehog)*: The lap possessing the lowest average speed (`speedAverageMps`) SHALL be highlighted with a Hedgehog badge (`🦔`).<br>• Workouts with a single lap or identical lap speeds SHALL NOT display fastest/slowest badges.<br>4. *Expandable List Ergonomics*: When a workout contains $> 3$ laps, `WorkoutLaps` SHALL default to showing the first 3 laps accompanied by an expandable toggle button (`Show all X laps` / `Show fewer`) to conserve vertical space in `WorkoutList`. When $\le 3$ laps, all laps SHALL be displayed directly.<br>5. *Database Schema & Migration Safety*: In `LapsDatabaseManager.java`, SQLite database `Laps.db` (`LapsDbHelper`) SHALL be upgraded to `DB_VERSION = 2`. The migration in `onUpgrade` MUST execute non-destructive `ALTER TABLE` operations adding nullable columns `name TEXT` and `description TEXT`, and MUST NOT drop or clear existing tables. Batch retrieval method `getLapsForWorkouts(chunkIds)` SHALL be provided to eliminate N+1 queries during list loading.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout session with $\ge 1$ laps displayed in `WorkoutSummary`,<br>• *When* the workout card is rendered,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutLaps` SHALL display each lap with its name, duration, distance, and sport-appropriate pace/speed.<br>• *Given* a workout session with $\ge 2$ laps of varying speeds,<br>• *Then* the fastest lap SHALL be badged with `🐇` (Rabbit) and the slowest lap badged with `🦔` (Hedgehog).<br>• *Given* a workout session with $> 3$ laps,<br>• *Then* the table SHALL initially show 3 laps and tapping the expand button SHALL reveal all laps.<br>• *Given* an existing database at `DB_VERSION = 1` with historical laps,<br>• *When* the app upgrades to `DB_VERSION = 2`,<br>• *Then* all existing lap records SHALL be preserved intact with null `name` and `description`.<br><br>**Invariants**: Card touch navigation to map (`REQ-SET-071`), single-workout deletion (`REQ-DAT-010`), TCX/GPX export queries, and metric unit localization (`REQ-UI-016`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Provide athletes with intuitive segment analysis directly in the workout summary view while establishing the persistent data model for custom lap names and descriptions. | `WorkoutSummary.kt`, `WorkoutLaps.kt`, `LapData.kt`, `WorkoutData.kt`, `WorkoutDataMapper.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-UI-094` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-142** | **Interactive Lap Detail Editing, Quick-Tag Presets, Sequential Navigation, and Segment Map Highlighting.** | The system SHALL provide an interactive editing bottom sheet (`LapEditBottomSheet.kt`) invoked by tapping any lap row in `WorkoutLaps.kt`, allowing athletes to customize lap names and descriptions, rapidly apply preset tags, navigate sequentially across laps, and visually inspect the lap track on an expandable map:<br>1. *Edit Sheet Invocation & Split Summary Header*: In `WorkoutLaps.kt`, tapping any lap row SHALL invoke `LapEditBottomSheet` for the selected lap. The sheet header SHALL render the lap index and formatted metrics: active duration (`formatters.time.format`), distance (`formatters.distance.format_with_units`), and sport-appropriate pace or speed (`formatters.pace.format_with_units` / `formatters.speed.format_with_units`).<br>2. *Quick-Tag Presets*: The sheet SHALL display a horizontally scrollable row of quick-tag suggestion chips: `Warm-up`, `Interval`, `Recovery`, `Hill Climb`, `Tempo`, `Sprint`, `Cool-down` (fully localized across all 9 supported languages). Tapping any chip SHALL immediately populate the lap name text field with the selected preset tag.<br>3. *Custom Name & Description Editing*: Single-line `OutlinedTextField` pre-filled with the lap's existing custom `name` if present (or placeholder defaulting to localized `"Lap X"`), and multi-line `OutlinedTextField` pre-filled with existing `description` notes. Tapping `Save` (or navigating sequentially / dismissing after changes) SHALL invoke `WorkoutRepository.updateLapDetails(workoutId, lapNr, name, description)` to persist changes to `Laps.db` and reactively update the in-memory `WorkoutData.laps` cache without requiring a full workout list reload.<br>4. *Sequential Lap Navigation*: The sheet SHALL provide `< Previous` (disabled when on the first lap) and `Next >` (disabled when on the last lap) navigation buttons. Tapping either navigation control SHALL persist pending modifications for the active lap and transition the sheet state to the adjacent sequential lap.<br>5. *Expandable Map Visualization & Intelligent Zoom Strategy*:<br>• *Two-State Sheet Ergonomics*: In the default half-expanded state, the bottom sheet SHALL prioritize text editing controls. Sliding the sheet upward (fully expanded) SHALL reveal the interactive segment map view.<br>• *Dual Track Overlay*: The entire workout polyline SHALL be rendered in the background with subtle muted neutral styling (`width = 4.dp`, semi-transparent outline color, `zIndex = 1f`), and the selected lap segment SHALL be rendered prominently in the foreground with vibrant primary styling (`width = 8-10.dp`, `zIndex = 2f`), along with distinct start (green) and stop (red) pins (`zIndex = 3f`).<br>• *Camera Zoom & Bounding Box*: The map camera SHALL automatically calculate and smoothly animate to the `LatLngBounds` enclosing all trackpoints of the complete workout session (`workoutBounds` derived from `allPoints`) with standard padding (`60-80px`), ensuring the entire course is visible for spatial context. The camera framing SHALL remain stable across sequential lap navigation (`< Previous` / `Next >`), while the highlighted foreground segment and start/stop markers update reactively to reflect the active lap. For stationary/indoor workouts or workouts lacking GPS trackpoints, the map view SHALL safely display an informative empty state without runtime exceptions.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout session with recorded laps rendered in `WorkoutSummary`,<br>• *When* the athlete taps any lap row in `WorkoutLaps`,<br>• *Then* `LapEditBottomSheet` SHALL open displaying the lap's metrics and existing details.<br>• *When* the athlete taps the "Interval" quick-tag chip,<br>• *Then* the lap name field SHALL immediately update to "Interval".<br>• *When* the athlete edits the name and description and taps "Save",<br>• *Then* `Laps.db` SHALL be updated and `WorkoutLaps` SHALL immediately reflect the new name and description.<br>• *When* the athlete taps "Next >",<br>• *Then* pending edits SHALL be saved and the sheet SHALL update to display Lap 2.<br>• *When* the athlete expands the bottom sheet upward,<br>• *Then* the segment map SHALL become visible, displaying the entire workout polyline in the background with the selected lap highlighted in the foreground and camera zoomed to enclose the complete workout route.<br>• *When* the athlete navigates between laps via `< Previous` or `Next >`,<br>• *Then* the map camera framing SHALL remain stable over the entire workout route, while the highlighted segment moves along the track to reflect the newly selected lap.<br>• *Given* an indoor workout with 0 GPS trackpoints,<br>• *When* the edit sheet is opened and expanded,<br>• *Then* the edit controls SHALL remain 100% functional and the map section SHALL gracefully indicate that no GPS track is available.<br><br>**Invariants**: Lap quantitative metrics (`timeTotalS`, `distanceTotalM`, `speedAverageMps`, `timeStart`) MUST remain immutable; card touch navigation to `TrackOnMapScreen` (`REQ-SET-071`) on non-lap elements MUST NOT be intercepted; overview table layout, collapsible threshold (`> 3` laps), and performance badges (Rabbit/Hedgehog) (`REQ-UI-141`) MUST NOT be regressed. | Provide athletes with effortless post-workout lap annotation and intuitive geographical visualization of workout intervals in the spatial context of the full workout. | `LapEditBottomSheet.kt`, `WorkoutLaps.kt`, `WorkoutSummary.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `LapSegmentUtils.kt`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-UI-095` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-143** | **Workout Summary Laps Table Headers, Single-Line Formatting & Proportional Layout.** | The system SHALL enhance the workout summary laps table (`WorkoutLaps.kt`) with dedicated table headers, guaranteed single-line metric formatting, rebalanced column proportions, and vertically centered performance badges:<br>1. *Dedicated Table Header Row (`LapTableHeader`)*: In `WorkoutLaps.kt`, when laps are displayed, a dedicated header row SHALL be rendered directly above the lap rows. The header row SHALL render 4 column labels styled consistently with `WorkoutExtrema` (`style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall`, `color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant`):<br>• *Column 1 (Lap Name)*: Localized label `@string/lap_table_header_lap` ("Runde" / "Lap").<br>• *Column 2 (Time)*: Localized label `@string/lap_table_header_time` ("Zeit" / "Time").<br>• *Column 3 (Distance)*: Localized label `@string/lap_table_header_distance` ("Distanz" / "Distance").<br>• *Column 4 (Pace or Speed with Unit)*: For running/walking activities, localized pace header including bracketed unit: `@string/pace_short` + `" ["` + unit + `"]"` (e.g., `"Tempo [min/km]"` or `"Pace [min/mi]"`). For cycling and other activities, localized speed header including bracketed unit: `@string/speed_short` + `" ["` + unit + `"]"` (e.g., `"Geschw. [km/h]"` or `"Speed [mph]"`).<br>• *Column 5 (Spacer / Badge column)*: Fixed width (`26.dp`) matching badge alignment in data rows.<br>2. *Guaranteed Single-Line Metric Cells*: Data cells for Pace/Speed SHALL render the pure formatted numerical value (`formatters.pace.format` or `formatters.speed.format`) without repeating the unit string. Time, Distance, and Pace/Speed cells SHALL enforce `maxLines = 1`, `softWrap = false`, preventing multi-line wrapping artifacts (such as `"46:27 min/"` wrapping `"km"` onto a second line).<br>3. *Proportional Column Width Layout*: Column layout in both `LapTableHeader` and `LapRow` SHALL adhere to proportional weights:<br>• Lap Name column: `Modifier.weight(1.6f)` (providing generous space for custom lap names like "1. Pause am Bach" without premature truncation).<br>• Time column: `Modifier.weight(0.85f)`.<br>• Distance column: `Modifier.weight(0.85f)`.<br>• Pace/Speed column: `Modifier.weight(1.1f)`.<br>• Badge column: `Modifier.width(26.dp)`, right-aligned.<br>4. *Badge Alignment*: Performance highlight badges (Rabbit 🐇 and Hedgehog 🦔) SHALL be vertically centered (`Alignment.CenterVertically` or `Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center)`) within single-line lap rows.<br>5. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*: All lap table header string resources SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a workout session with $\ge 1$ laps displayed in `WorkoutSummary`,<br>• *When* `WorkoutLaps` is rendered,<br>• *Then* a table header row SHALL appear above the lap rows with localized labels: "Lap", "Time", "Distance", and "Pace [unit]" (or "Speed [unit]").<br>• *Given* a lap with a long custom name (e.g. "1. Pause am Bach"),<br>• *When* displayed on a standard 360-400dp mobile screen,<br>• *Then* the lap name SHALL occupy the widened 1.6f column weight without premature truncation.<br>• *Given* any lap pace value,<br>• *When* rendered in the pace column,<br>• *Then* the numerical value SHALL render cleanly on a single line without wrapping unit text.<br>• *Given* the fastest or slowest lap with a Rabbit (🐇) or Hedgehog (🦔) badge,<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* the badge SHALL be vertically centered relative to the row's metric values.<br><br>**Invariants**: Interactive lap row tap opening `LapEditBottomSheet` (`REQ-UI-142`), Rabbit/Hedgehog detection logic (`REQ-UI-141`), expandable threshold (`> 3` laps), and underlying lap database schema (`Laps.db`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Eliminate awkward text wrapping, premature lap name truncation, and visual misalignment in the workout summary laps table, providing clean, professional split metrics. | `WorkoutLaps.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-096` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-144** | **Streamlined & Collapsible Strava Best Efforts Presentation in Workout Summary.** | Within the Strava activity results section of the workout summary card (`StravaActivitySection.kt`), the system SHALL provide a streamlined, unit-aware, and collapsible presentation for Strava best efforts (`best_efforts`) to prevent summary card bloat while prioritizing personal achievements and the activity's primary benchmark time:<br>1. *Effort Classification & Metric Attributes*:<br>• A best effort is classified as *Highlight* if it represents a Personal Record (`prRank != null`, where `prRank` $\in \{1, 2, 3\}$).<br>• An effort is classified as *Mile Effort* (`isMileEffort == true`) if its benchmark name contains `"mile"` or `" mi"` (e.g., `"1/2 mile"`, `"1 mile"`, `"2 miles"`, `"10 miles"`).<br>• Each effort resolves an effective distance in meters (`effectiveDistanceMeters`) parsed directly from JSON `distance` or inferred from standard benchmark names (400m $\rightarrow$ 400m, 1/2 mile $\rightarrow$ 804.67m, 1k $\rightarrow$ 1000m, 1 mile $\rightarrow$ 1609.34m, 2 miles $\rightarrow$ 3218.69m, 5k $\rightarrow$ 5000m, 10k $\rightarrow$ 10000m, 15k $\rightarrow$ 15000m, 10 miles $\rightarrow$ 16093.44m, 20k $\rightarrow$ 20000m, Half-Marathon $\rightarrow$ 21097.5m, Marathon $\rightarrow$ 42195m).<br>2. *Unit System Filtering in Collapsed View*:<br>• When the active unit system is Metric (`TrainingApplication.getUnit() == MyUnits.METRIC`), the system SHALL exclude all imperial mile efforts (`isMileEffort == true`) from the collapsed (reduced) view.<br>• When Imperial units are active (`MyUnits.IMPERIAL`), mile intervals SHALL remain eligible for the collapsed view.<br>3. *Mandatory Inclusion of the Longest Distance in Reduced View*:<br>• When the best efforts are reduced in the collapsed view, the system SHALL guarantee the presentation of the time for the **longest** eligible distance (e.g. 10k in a 10k run, or longest metric distance when metric units are active).<br>• If the activity achieved PRs on eligible distances, the collapsed view SHALL display all eligible PRs plus the longest distance.<br>• If the longest distance is itself a PR, it SHALL be included without duplication.<br>• Displayed efforts in the collapsed view SHALL maintain natural ascending distance order.<br>4. *Collapse Eligibility & Accordion Interaction*:<br>• The section SHALL be eligible for collapse if and only if `allEfforts.size > reducedEfforts.size`.<br>• If `allEfforts.size == reducedEfforts.size`, all efforts SHALL be displayed directly without expansion controls.<br>• *Collapsed View (`isExpanded == false`, Default)*: Renders `reducedEfforts` and an expand action button: `@string/strava_show_all_best_efforts_format` (`"▼ Show all %1$d best efforts (+%2$d more)"` / in DE: `"▼ Alle %1$d Bestzeiten anzeigen (+%2$d weitere)"`).<br>• *Expanded View (`isExpanded == true`)*: Renders all `activity.bestEfforts` in full (including mile intervals) and a collapse action button: `@string/strava_show_less_best_efforts_format` (`"▲ Show less (%1$d)"` / in DE: `"▲ Weniger anzeigen (%1$d)"`).<br>• *State Preservation*: Expansion state SHALL be preserved during lazy list scrolling and configuration changes via `rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }`.<br>5. *Localization Parity (9 Locales)*:<br>All user-facing action labels and formatting strings SHALL be localized across all 9 supported application locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete with Metric units viewing a 10k run with 7 best efforts (400m, 1/2 mi, 1k, 1 mi, 2 mi, 5k, 10k) where 1k is a PR #2,<br>• *When* the workout summary is rendered in collapsed view,<br>• *Then* exactly 2 best effort rows SHALL be displayed (1k and 10k), zero mile rows SHALL be visible, followed by an expand button `"▼ Alle 7 Bestzeiten anzeigen (+5 weitere)"`.<br>• *When* the athlete taps the expand button,<br>• *Then* all 7 best efforts SHALL be displayed, followed by `"▲ Weniger anzeigen (2)"`.<br>• *Given* an athlete with Metric units viewing a 10k run with 7 best efforts and 0 PRs,<br>• *When* rendered in collapsed view,<br>• *Then* exactly 1 row SHALL be displayed (10k), followed by `"▼ Alle 7 Bestzeiten anzeigen (+6 weitere)"`.<br>• *Given* an athlete with Metric units viewing a 5k run where 5k is PR #1,<br>• *When* rendered in collapsed view,<br>• *Then* exactly 1 row SHALL be displayed (5k), with zero duplicate 5k rows, followed by `"▼ Alle 6 Bestzeiten anzeigen (+5 weitere)"`.<br>• *Given* an activity with 1 best effort (or where all efforts equal the reduced list),<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* all efforts SHALL be displayed directly with zero expand/collapse buttons.<br><br>**Invariants**: Segment efforts rendering (`REQ-UI-131`), Strava branding requirements (`PoweredByStrava`), external Strava activity click launch (`StravaHelper.openActivity`), and PR badge styling (`RankBadge`) MUST remain fully intact. | Prevent summary card bloat while ensuring primary benchmark distances and personal records remain immediately visible without unit clutter. | `StravaActivitySection.kt`, `StravaActivityData.kt`, `strings.xml` (all 9 locales) | `TST-UI-097` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-145** | **UI Deletion of Old Unfinished Workouts & Active Tracking Deletion Protection.** | The system SHALL allow the user to delete old unfinished or crashed workouts (`finished == false` / SQLite column `FINISHED == 0`) via the user interface in both expanded (`WorkoutSummary.kt`) and compact (`WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`) aftermath list views, while strictly preserving unfinished workouts in SQLite until explicitly deleted, maintaining their visual alpha (`0.5f`), and preventing deletion of any workout currently being actively tracked:<br>1. *Database Retention Invariant*: Unfinished and crashed workouts SHALL remain preserved in the SQLite database (`WorkoutSummaries.TABLE`) across application restarts and new tracking launches. The system SHALL NOT automatically delete or purge unfinished workouts in the background or upon starting a new session.<br>2. *Visual Differentiation*: In `WorkoutSummary.kt` and `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`, unfinished workouts (`workoutData.headerData.finished == false`) SHALL continue to be rendered with semi-transparent alpha (`contentAlpha = 0.5f`), clearly indicating their incomplete or interrupted status.<br>3. *Decoupled Header Deletion Ergonomics*: In `WorkoutHeader.kt`, the long-press context menu for workout deletion (`showContextMenu = true`) SHALL be decoupled from export menu enablement (`menuEnabled`). Whenever deletion is permitted (`canDelete == true`), the header surface SHALL attach `combinedClickable` with `onLongClick = { showContextMenu = true }` displaying the "Delete" (`R.string.delete`) context menu action. The 3-dots export menu SHALL remain governed by `menuEnabled = workoutData.headerData.finished` (preventing incomplete workouts from being exported to external formats or Strava).<br>4. *Active Tracking Safety Guard*: If an unfinished workout is the one currently being tracked by `TrackerService` (`TrainingApplication.isActivelyTracked(workoutId) == true`, where `isTracking() == true && workoutId == activeWorkoutId`), the system SHALL strictly prohibit deletion via the UI in both expanded (`WorkoutSummary`) and compact (`WorkoutSummaryCompact`) views. For non-actively tracked workouts (finished workouts and old unfinished workouts), deletion SHALL remain fully accessible.<br>5. *Cascading Deletion Execution*: When the user confirms deletion in the confirmation dialog (`WorkoutDeleteDialog`), the deletion SHALL execute via `WorkoutDeletionHelper.deleteWorkout(workoutId)` to cleanly purge the summary row, laps, extrema values, export status, and drop dynamic sample tables (`workout_samples_<baseFileName>`) with zero orphaned tables (`REQ-DAT-010`).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an old unfinished workout in SQLite (`finished == false`) and tracking is idle (`isTracking() == false`),<br>• *When* displayed in `WorkoutSummary` (expanded view),<br>• *Then* it SHALL render with `alpha = 0.5f`, and long-pressing the header SHALL open the context menu with "Delete".<br>• *When* the user taps "Delete" and confirms in `WorkoutDeleteDialog`,<br>• *Then* the workout and all associated relational records and sample tables SHALL be completely purged from SQLite, and the workout list SHALL refresh.<br>• *Given* an old unfinished workout displayed in `WorkoutSummaryCompact` (compact view),<br>• *When* the user long-presses the item,<br>• *Then* the context menu SHALL display the "Delete" option.<br>• *Given* an active live tracking session (`isTracking() == true` with active workout ID $W$),<br>• *When* viewing workout $W$ in `WorkoutSummary` or `WorkoutSummaryCompact`,<br>• *Then* the UI SHALL NOT allow deletion of workout $W$ while tracking is active.<br>• *Given* a completed workout (`finished == true`),<br>• *Then* both the 3-dots export menu and the long-press deletion context menu SHALL remain fully functional.<br><br>**Invariants**: Automatic workout data retention (`finished == false` remains in SQLite), cascaded table drop sequencing (`ATT-296`), and single-workout deletion dialog flow MUST remain strictly intact. | Ensure users have full data sovereignty to remove obsolete crashed or abandoned workouts from their history while safeguarding active tracking sessions from accidental destruction. | `WorkoutHeader.kt`, `WorkoutSummary.kt`, `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java` | `TST-UI-098` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-146** | **Option to Mark Old Unfinished Workouts as Finished & Period Integration.** | The system SHALL allow the user to mark an old unfinished or crashed workout (`finished == false` / SQLite column `FINISHED == 0`) as finished via the user interface in both expanded (`WorkoutSummary.kt` via `WorkoutHeader.kt`) and compact (`WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`) aftermath list views, updating persistence, visual opacity, export capability, and periods aggregation while strictly preventing actively tracked sessions from being marked finished via the aftermath UI:<br>1. *Context Menu Action Availability*: For any unfinished workout (`finished == false`) that is not actively being recorded (`!TrainingApplication.isActivelyTracked(workoutId)`), the long-press context menu in `WorkoutHeader.kt` and `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt` SHALL display the action "Als abgeschlossen markieren" / "Mark as finished" (`@string/mark_as_finished`).<br>2. *Database & Memory State Transition*: Selecting "Mark as finished" SHALL invoke `WorkoutRepository.markWorkoutFinished(workoutId)` which SHALL atomically update `WorkoutSummaries.FINISHED = 1` in SQLite (`WorkoutSummaries.TABLE`) for the target workout ID and update the in-memory cache (`updateWorkoutInMemory`), emitting the updated `WorkoutData` with `finished = true`.<br>3. *Reactive Visual & Menu Ergonomics*: Upon marking as finished, the workout card SHALL reactively re-render with full opacity (`alpha = TTAlpha.High` / `1.0f`), the 3-dots export menu SHALL become enabled (`menuEnabled = true`), granting immediate access to TCX/GPX export and Strava upload, and the "Mark as finished" action SHALL be omitted from subsequent context menus.<br>4. *Historical Period Aggregation*: `WorkoutRepository.markWorkoutFinished` SHALL directly invoke `PeriodsRepository.getInstance(app).onWorkoutFinished(updatedWorkout)`, immediately aggregating the newly finalized workout into its Day period and rolling up metrics (workout count, duration, distance, ascent) to Week, Month, and Year periods.<br>5. *Active Tracking Safety Guard*: If an unfinished workout is currently being recorded by `TrackerService` (`TrainingApplication.isActivelyTracked(workoutId) == true`), the "Mark as finished" option MUST NOT be visible or accessible in the aftermath context menu.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an old unfinished workout in SQLite (`finished == false`) and tracking is idle (`isTracking() == false`),<br>• *When* the athlete long-presses the workout in expanded view (`WorkoutSummary`) or compact view (`WorkoutSummaryCompact`),<br>• *Then* the context menu SHALL display the "Mark as finished" option.<br>• *When* the athlete selects "Mark as finished",<br>• *Then* `WorkoutSummaries.FINISHED` SHALL update to 1 in SQLite, the workout card SHALL render with normal opacity (`alpha = 1.0f`), the 3-dots export menu SHALL be enabled, and the workout metrics SHALL appear in the corresponding Period Summary (Day, Week, Month, Year).<br>• *Given* an active live tracking session (`isTracking() == true` with active workout ID $W$),<br>• *When* viewing workout $W$ in aftermath list views,<br>• *Then* the "Mark as finished" option SHALL NOT be accessible.<br>• *Given* an already finished workout (`finished == true`),<br>• *Then* the "Mark as finished" option SHALL NOT be present in the context menu.<br><br>**Invariants**: Recorded raw samples, trackpoints, laps, sensor extrema, and base file names MUST NOT be modified or corrupted; cascading deletion (`REQ-UI-145`) MUST remain available for all non-actively tracked workouts; single-workout deletion flow MUST remain intact. | Enable athletes to recover and finalize valuable crashed or interrupted workouts into first-class completed activities eligible for export and temporal analytics. | `WorkoutHeader.kt`, `WorkoutSummary.kt`, `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`, `WorkoutList.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`, `PeriodsRepository.kt`, `strings.xml` | `TST-UI-099` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-147** | **Compact Workout Summary Context Menu Streamlining.** | The system SHALL streamline the long-press context menu of the compact workout summary (`WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`) by eliminating the "Edit workout" action and scoping the compact context menu strictly to lifecycle management actions:<br>1. *Removal of Edit Action*: `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt` SHALL NOT render an "Edit workout" (`@string/edit_workout`) item in its dropdown context menu and SHALL NOT require an `onEditWorkout` callback parameter.<br>2. *Permitted Compact Context Menu Actions*: The compact context menu SHALL contain only:<br>• "Als abgeschlossen markieren" / "Mark as finished" (`@string/mark_as_finished`) when the workout is unfinished and not actively tracked (`canMarkFinished == true`).<br>• "Löschen" / "Delete" (`@string/delete`) when the workout is not actively tracked (`canDelete == true`).<br>3. *Context Menu Trigger Condition*: The long-press trigger for `WorkoutSummaryCompact` SHALL be governed strictly by `hasContextMenu = canDelete || canMarkFinished`. If an item is actively tracked (`TrainingApplication.isActivelyTracked(workoutId) == true`), both `canDelete` and `canMarkFinished` evaluate to `false`, rendering `hasContextMenu == false` and disabling long-press interaction.<br>4. *Preservation of Full Summary Editing*: The expanded workout summary card (`WorkoutSummary.kt` via `WorkoutHeader.kt`) SHALL retain its full workout editing capabilities (`onEditWorkout`) intact.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete viewing workouts in compact view (`WorkoutSummaryCompact`),<br>• *When* the athlete long-presses an idle finished workout,<br>• *Then* the context menu SHALL display ONLY the "Delete" option and SHALL NOT display "Edit workout".<br>• *Given* an idle unfinished workout in compact view,<br>• *When* long-pressed,<br>• *Then* the context menu SHALL display "Mark as finished" and "Delete", and SHALL NOT display "Edit workout".<br>• *Given* an actively tracked workout in compact view,<br>• *When* long-pressed,<br>• *Then* NO context menu SHALL be shown (`hasContextMenu == false`).<br>• *Given* the expanded view (`WorkoutSummary`),<br>• *When* viewed or edited,<br>• *Then* workout editing functionality SHALL remain fully functional.<br><br>**Invariants**: Workout deletion (`REQ-UI-145`), mark as finished (`REQ-UI-146`), and expanded workout editing MUST NOT be altered. | Ensure the compact view is optimized for rapid scannability and high-density map exploration without cluttering the context menu with detailed metadata editing actions. | `WorkoutSummaryCompact.kt`, `WorkoutList.kt` | `TST-UI-100` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-148** | **Standardized Edge-to-Edge Bottom Sheet Insets & Core UI Design Components.** | The system SHALL provide a centralized design system package (`com.atrainingtracker.trainingtracker.ui.components.core`) and harmonize edge-to-edge system bar window insets across all modal bottom sheets and tabular headers:<br>1. *Universal Modal Bottom Sheet (`AppModalBottomSheet`)*:<br>• The system SHALL provide a reusable Material 3 `ModalBottomSheet` container (`AppModalBottomSheet.kt`) wrapping standard bottom sheet mechanics.<br>• The sheet container SHALL automatically apply `navigationBarsPadding()` and `imePadding()` to guarantee that content and bottom action bars do not overlap the Android navigation bar or keyboard on gesture and 3-button navigation devices.<br>• The sheet SHALL provide a standard drag handle (`MinimumDragHandle`), a structured header row displaying an optional leading icon, localized title (`MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge`), and optional close/dismiss button (`Icons.Default.Close`).<br>• The sheet SHALL provide an adaptive scrollable body column and an optional bottom action bar slot separated by a subtle divider.<br>2. *Edge-to-Edge Bottom Sheet Insets Harmonization*:<br>• In `RichStatsSheet.kt`, hardcoded bottom padding (`padding(bottom = 32.dp)`) SHALL be replaced with adaptive `navigationBarsPadding()`, preventing clipping on devices with tall 3-button navigation bars and eliminating dead whitespace on gesture navigation devices.<br>• All modal bottom sheets (`FilterBottomSheetScaffold`, `LapEditBottomSheet`, `RichStatsSheet`, `AppModalBottomSheet`) SHALL uniformly anchor to the bottom navigation bar without bottom gap or clipping artifacts.<br>3. *Shared Tabular Header Component & Typography (`AppTableHeader`, `AppTableTypography`)*:<br>• The system SHALL extract a standardized tabular header composable (`AppTableHeader.kt`) and reusable typography style (`AppTableTypography.kt`) unifying `labelSmall` with `PlatformTextStyle(includeFontPadding = false)` and `LineHeightStyle(Alignment.Bottom, Trim.Both)` to ensure consistent bottom baseline alignment across data tables.<br>• `WorkoutLaps.kt` (`LapTableHeader`) SHALL be refactored to use `AppTableHeader` and `AppTableTypography`, preserving exact column weights (1.6f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.1f, 26.dp) and localized strings.<br>4. *Component Consolidation in `ui.components.core`*:<br>• Universal UI primitives (`AppModalBottomSheet`, `AppTableHeader`, `AppTableTypography`, `MinimumDragHandle`, `FastScrollbar`, `BadgeBox`) SHALL be housed in `com.atrainingtracker.trainingtracker.ui.components.core` as the foundation for modernizing dialogs (ATT-900).<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* any device running gesture navigation or 3-button navigation,<br>• *When* `AppModalBottomSheet` or `RichStatsSheet` is displayed,<br>• *Then* content and bottom controls SHALL render above the system navigation bar with zero clipping and zero hardcoded bottom gaps.<br>• *Given* `LapTableHeader` in `WorkoutLaps.kt`,<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* column labels SHALL use `AppTableHeader` / `AppTableTypography` with bottom baseline alignment matching `WorkoutExtrema`.<br>• *Given* the `ui.components.core` package,<br>• *Then* it SHALL provide `AppModalBottomSheet`, `AppTableHeader`, `AppTableTypography`, `MinimumDragHandle`, `FastScrollbar`, and `BadgeBox` with clean API contracts.<br><br>**Invariants**: Filter bottom sheet scaffold behavior (`REQ-UI-133`, `REQ-UI-134`), status bar boundary limits on map bottom sheets (`REQ-SET-069`), live tracking lap summary HUD dialog (`LapSummaryDialog`), and lap table formatting (`REQ-UI-143`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Standardize edge-to-edge window insets handling, eliminate hardcoded padding, unify tabular typography, and establish reusable core UI primitives to support modernizing legacy dialogs to bottom sheets. | `AppModalBottomSheet.kt`, `AppTableHeader.kt`, `AppTableTypography.kt`, `MinimumDragHandle.kt`, `FastScrollbar.kt`, `BadgeBox.kt`, `RichStatsSheet.kt`, `WorkoutLaps.kt`, `WorkoutExtrema.kt` | `TST-UI-101` | Verified |

(`DisplaySettingsDialogFragment`) SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying display behavior toggles (force portrait, keep screen on, keep screen unlocked) with leading display icon (`Icons.Default.DisplaySettings`), title (`@string/Display`), close button, and confirm action button (`@string/Done`).<br>• `ActivityTypeSelectionDialog.kt` (`ActivityTypeSelectionDialogFragment` / confirm button.<br>3. *Tracking Grid & Sensor Field Modernization*:<br>• `EditSensorFieldDialog.kt` SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying sensor type, source device, and text size selection with save and cancel actions.<br>• `ConfigureFilterDialog.kt` SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying smoothing filter parameters (filter type, smoothing factor alpha) with save and cancel actions.<br>4. *Sport Types & Equipment Modernization*:<br>• `EditSportTypeDialog.kt` SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying sport name, speed thresholds, base sport selector, and sensor compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying device technical status LED, name, protocol, calibration, wheel circumference, and unpairing action.<br>5. *Workout Clusters & Export Status Modernization*:<br>• `WorkoutClusterSelectionDialog` and `EditWorkoutClusterDialog` / `EditWorkoutClusterIdentityDialog` SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` for cluster candidate selection and cluster compose `AppModalBottomSheet` displaying per-service upload status and details.<br>6. *AppModalBottomSheet Architectural Enhancement*:<br>• `AppModalBottomSheet.kt` SHALL support both vector icons (`icon: ImageVector? = null`) and drawable painter icons (`iconPainter: Painter? = null`) to cleanly accommodate Android resource drawables across all bottom sheet headers.<br>7. *Preserved Modal Dialog Invariants*:<br>• `LapSummaryDialog` MUST remain an auto-dismissing (3-second) centered HUD `Dialog` during active tracking.<br>• Destructive confirmation dialogs (`DeleteConfirmationDialog`, `WorkoutDeleteDialog`, `DeleteOldWorkoutsDialog`), deletion progress dialogs (`WorkoutDeletionProgressDialog`), and system redirects (`GPSDisabledDialog`, `StartOrResumeDialog`) MUST remain centered `AlertDialog`s.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* the navigation drawer in `MainActivityWithNavigation`,<br>• *When* the athlete taps Export, Units, Display Settings, or `SensorStatus` in the tracking screen,<br>• *When* the athlete taps a sensor status tile,<br>• *Then* `SensorSourceDialog` SHALL present as an `AppModalBottomSheet` with the sensor's icon and `AppModalBottomSheet`.<br>• *Given* tracking grid or equipment/sport/device editing,<br>• *When* invoked,<br>• *Then* the corresponding configuration sheet SHALL present as an | **REQ-UI-150** | **Standardized Bottom Sheet Action Layouts, Semantic Action Verbs & Route Dialog Parity.** | The system SHALL standardize bottom action bars, button arrangements, and semantic action verbs across all application bottom popups (`AppModalBottomSheet`), and SHALL eliminate full-screen editing dialog outliers by migrating `EditRouteScreen` to an `AppModalBottomSheet`:<br>1. *Core Action Bar Architecture (`AppDialogActions`)*:<br>• The system SHALL provide reusable action composables in `com.atrainingtracker.trainingtracker.ui.components.core.AppDialogActions`:<br>  - `SaveCancel`: Two-button horizontal action bar comprising a secondary `TextButton` ("Abbrechen" / `@string/Cancel`), flexible space separation, and a primary filled `Button` ("Speichern" / `@string/save`) with configurable `enabled` state.<br>  - `Confirm`: Single primary filled `Button` ("OK" / `@android:string/ok`) aligned to the right by default.<br>  - `CancelOnly`: Single secondary `TextButton` ("Abbrechen" / `@string/Cancel`) filling horizontal width (`fillMaxWidth()`).<br>2. *Semantic Action Verb Enforcement & Transactional Staging*:<br>• Whenever a dialog persists, updates, or commits data changes to ViewModel, database, or SharedPreferences upon clicking the primary action button, the primary button SHALL display the verb "Speichern" (`@string/save`), and the dialog SHALL stage modifications locally until "Speichern" is tapped. The system MUST NOT display "OK" or "Done" on data-mutating buttons.<br>  Specifically:<br>  - `DisplaySettingsDialog.kt`: SHALL stage display preferences locally and use `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel` with `@string/save` ("Speichern"). Reverting changes when dismissed or "Abbrechen" tapped.<br>  - `ExportSettingsDialog.kt`: SHALL stage export preferences locally and use `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel` with `@string/save` ("Speichern"). Reverting changes when dismissed or "Abbrechen" tapped.<br>  - `EditDeviceDialog.kt`: Primary button SHALL display `@string/save`.<br>  - `EditSensorFieldDialog.kt`: Primary button SHALL display `@string/save`.<br>  - `ConfigureFilterDialog.kt`: Primary button SHALL display `@string/save`.<br>  - `UnitsSettingsDialog.kt`: Primary button SHALL display `@string/save` instead of `@string/Done`.<br>  - `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` (Cluster-Parameter): Primary button SHALL display `@string/save`.<br>  - `EditEquipmentDialog.kt`, `EditSportTypeDialog.kt`, and `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt`: SHALL continue to display `@string/save`.<br>• Whenever a dialog provides informational acknowledgment without deferred data staging, the action button SHALL display "OK" (`@android:string/ok` or `@string/OK`) aligned to the right.<br>  Specifically:<br>  - `SensorSourceDialog.kt`, `ExportDetailsDialog`, and `ClusterInfoDialog.kt`: SHALL display right-aligned `@android:string/ok` or `@string/OK`.<br>3. *Full-Screen Outlier Elimination (`EditRouteDialog`)*:<br>• `EditRouteScreen.kt` (full-screen `Scaffold` with `TopAppBar`) SHALL be refactored into `EditRouteDialog.kt` presenting as an `AppModalBottomSheet` with route icon, localized title `@string/route_edit`, close dismiss icon, scrollable form body, and standard `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel` bottom action bar.<br>• Callers in `RoutesFragment.kt` and `GpxImportActivity.kt` SHALL invoke the dialog as a modal bottom sheet, preserving existing `onSave` and `onCancel` flows.<br>4. *Standard Bottom Sheet Adoption in Import Tuning*:<br>• In `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt`, the Cluster-Parameter tuning sheet SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` with `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel`, replacing the raw `ModalBottomSheet` and removing the `OutlinedButton`.<br>5. *Localization Parity*:<br>• All action buttons across all dialogs SHALL be fully localized across all 9 supported locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>6. *System Invariants & Preserved Behavior*:<br>• Data persistence logic, ViewModel state holding, database transactions, SharedPreferences keys, route summary updating, filter type conversions, and device pairing/calibration MUST NOT be altered or regressed.<br>• Bottom sheet drag handles (`MinimumDragHandle`), system insets (`navigationBarsPadding()`, `imePadding()`), and scrim dismiss gestures MUST be preserved across all sheets.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* any data-storing bottom sheet (Edit Device, Edit Sensor Field, Configure Filter, Units Settings, Edit Equipment, Edit Sport Type, Edit Route, Cluster Parameter, Edit Workout Name, Display Settings, Export Settings),<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* the bottom action bar SHALL display a borderless text button "Abbrechen" on the left and a filled primary button "Speichern" on the right.<br>• *Given* an informational bottom sheet (Sensor Source, Export Details, Cluster Info),<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* the bottom action bar SHALL display a right-aligned button with text "OK" and SHALL NOT display "Done".<br>• *Given* a user editing a route in `RoutesFragment` or `GpxImportActivity`,<br>• *When* edit route is invoked,<br>• *Then* the route editor SHALL present as a modal bottom sheet (`AppModalBottomSheet`) anchored above the navigation bar instead of a full-screen scaffold.<br>• *Given* `ImportBackupTabsScreen` when cluster parameter tuning is opened,<br>• *When* rendered,<br>• *Then* it SHALL use `AppModalBottomSheet` and standard text button "Abbrechen" + filled button "Speichern" without `OutlinedButton`.<br><br>**Invariants**: Database schemas, ViewModel save implementations, route coordinate data, SharedPreferences preferences, authentic un-tinted sport icons, and system window insets MUST NOT be altered. | Establish uniform presentation, layout spacing, and clear semantic action verb distinction across all application dialogs and bottom sheets, eliminating UX confusion and design divergence. | `AppModalBottomSheet.kt`, `AppDialogActions.kt`, `EditRouteDialog.kt`, `RoutesFragment.kt`, `GpxImportActivity.kt`, `EditDeviceDialog.kt`, `EditSensorFieldDialog.kt`, `ConfigureFilterDialog.kt`, `UnitsSettingsDialog.kt`, `ExportSettingsDialog.kt`, `DisplaySettingsDialog.kt`, `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-UI-103` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-151** | **Workout Editing Modal Bottom Sheet & Fragment Viewport Layering.** | The system SHALL eliminate the full-screen `Scaffold` outlier in workout editing by refactoring `EditWorkoutScreen` into a standardized modal bottom sheet (`EditWorkoutDialog` / `AppModalBottomSheet`), and SHALL optimize fragment caller viewport composition so background lists and maps remain visible behind the modal scrim:<br>1. *Modal Bottom Sheet Architecture (`EditWorkoutDialog`)*:<br>• The workout editor SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` with leading edit icon (`Icons.Default.Edit` or `ic_table_edit`), localized title (`@string/edit_workout`), dismiss handling via scrim tap / drag handle / close icon, and window insets (`navigationBarsPadding()`, `imePadding()`).<br>• The editor SHALL retain all 8 workout input sections within a vertically scrollable body (`verticalScroll`): Workout Name, Route / Cluster assignment, Sport & Equipment spinners (`DropdownSelector`), Commute and Trainer flags, Strava individual upload toggle (when Strava is configured), Description (`minLines = 3`), Goal, and Method.<br>• The Route/Cluster field SHALL continue to support launching `EditWorkoutClusterDialog` as a nested modal bottom sheet and unassigning routes.<br>2. *Standard Action Bar Integration (`AppDialogActions`)*:<br>• The bottom sheet SHALL integrate `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel`:<br>  - Secondary action: borderless `TextButton` ("Abbrechen" / `@string/Cancel`) invoking dismiss without persisting.<br>  - Primary action: filled `Button` ("Speichern" / `@string/save`) invoking `viewModel.saveChanges()` followed by dismiss.<br>3. *Caller Viewport Layering Optimization*:<br>• In `WorkoutSummariesListFragment`, `WorkoutSummariesTabbedFragment`, `PeriodsFragment`, and `WorkoutClustersFragment`, the active underlying screen content (`WorkoutList`, `TrackOnMapScreen`, `WorkoutTabsScreen`, `PeriodMapScreen`, `PeriodList`, `WorkoutClustersScreen`) SHALL remain composed and active in the view hierarchy when workout editing is invoked (`selectedWorkoutIdForEdit != null` / `editedWorkoutId != null`), presenting `EditWorkoutDialog` as an overlay to ensure fluid presentation without empty/blank background artifacts.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping "Edit workout" in any workout summary or list,<br>• *When* the workout editor is displayed,<br>• *Then* it SHALL present as an `AppModalBottomSheet` anchored above the navigation bar with "Abbrechen" on the left and "Speichern" on the right, and the underlying list or map view SHALL remain visible beneath the translucent scrim.<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping the Route/Cluster field in the editor,<br>• *When* tapped,<br>• *Then* `EditWorkoutClusterDialog` SHALL open as a nested modal bottom sheet without dismissing the workout editor.<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping "Abbrechen", swiping down, or tapping outside,<br>• *When* dismissed,<br>• *Then* changes SHALL be discarded and `viewModel.saveChanges()` SHALL NOT be called.<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping "Speichern",<br>• *When* saved,<br>• *Then* `viewModel.saveChanges()` SHALL be called to commit changes to the database and the sheet SHALL dismiss.<br><br>**Invariants**: Database persistence logic in `WorkoutRepository`, `EditWorkoutViewModel` state holding, sport and equipment selection rules (`REQ-UI-130`), cluster creation and matching heuristics, and IME keyboard padding MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Eliminate the full-screen Scaffold outlier for workout editing, unify action bar ergonomics with `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel`, and preserve underlying view context across all fragment callers. | `EditWorkoutScreen.kt`, `EditWorkoutDialog.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesListFragment.kt`, `WorkoutSummariesTabbedFragment.kt`, `PeriodsFragment.kt`, `WorkoutClustersFragment.kt` | `TST-UI-104` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-152** | **Dropbox Settings Modal Bottom Sheet & Navigation Integration.** | The system SHALL eliminate the full-screen fragment outlier for Dropbox settings by replacing `CloudUploadFragment` with a standardized modal bottom sheet (`DropboxSettingsDialog` / `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment` backed by `AppModalBottomSheet`), integrating with the navigation drawer and preserving all backup and authentication mechanics:<br>1. *Modal Bottom Sheet Architecture (`DropboxSettingsDialog`)*:<br>• The dialog SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` with authentic Dropbox logo (`R.drawable.dropbox_logo_blue` via `iconPainter`, `iconTint = Color.Unspecified`), localized title (`@string/Dropbox`), dismiss handling (drag handle, scrim tap, close icon), and window insets (`navigationBarsPadding()`, `imePadding()`).<br>• *Connection Section*: The dialog SHALL display the current connection state (`@string/dropbox_connected_status` in green vs. `@string/dropbox_disconnected_status` in error color). When disconnected, it SHALL provide a Dropbox-blue button (`@string/dropbox_connect`) initiating OAuth PKCE via `Auth.startOAuth2PKCE(...)`. When connected, it SHALL provide a button (`@string/dropbox_disconnect`) revoking credentials via `TrainingApplication.deleteDropboxCredential()` and `TrainingApplication.setUploadToDropbox(false)`.<br>• *Automated Backups Section*: The dialog SHALL display an interactive switch for automated backups (`@string/automated_backups` with summary `@string/automated_backups_summary`, defaulting to `true`), and a backup interval selector (`@string/backup_interval`, options: Daily, Every 3 days, Weekly, Monthly from `@array/backup_interval_entries`, defaulting to `"1"`), enabled only when automated backups are enabled.<br>2. *Standard Action Bar Integration (`AppDialogActions`)*:<br>• The bottom sheet SHALL integrate `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel`:<br>  - Secondary action: borderless `TextButton` ("Abbrechen" / `@string/Cancel`) dismissing the dialog and discarding uncommitted backup preference changes.<br>  - Primary action: filled `Button` ("Speichern" / `@string/save`) committing `automated_backups` and `backup_interval_days` to `SharedPreferences`, immediately invoking `BackupWorker.schedule(context)` to reschedule WorkManager periodic backup tasks, and dismissing the dialog.<br>3. *DialogFragment Host (`DropboxSettingsDialogFragment`)*:<br>• The dialog SHALL be hosted by `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment` extending `DialogFragment` with `STYLE_NORMAL, android.R.style.Theme_Translucent_NoTitleBar`. In `onResume()`, it SHALL inspect `Auth.getDbxCredential()` to capture and store any new credential resulting from the OAuth browser redirect.<br>4. *Navigation Drawer & Preference Routing*:<br>• In `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt`, selecting `R.id.drawer_dropbox` SHALL close the navigation drawer and present `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment` without replacing the main screen fragment. Preference screen routing for `"cloudUpload"` in `onPreferenceStartScreen` SHALL launch `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment`.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete opening the navigation drawer,<br>• *When* "Dropbox" is selected,<br>• *Then* `DropboxSettingsDialog` SHALL present as an `AppModalBottomSheet` anchored to the screen bottom with "Abbrechen" and "Speichern", leaving the background screen visible beneath the scrim.<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping "Mit Dropbox verknüpfen" in the dialog,<br>• *When* OAuth authentication is initiated and completes in the browser,<br>• *Then* upon returning to the app, the dialog SHALL capture the credential in `onResume()` and display the connected status.<br>• *Given* an athlete modifying the backup interval or toggle,<br>• *When* "Abbrechen" is tapped,<br>• *Then* changes SHALL be discarded without mutating `SharedPreferences`.<br>• *Given* an athlete modifying backup preferences and tapping "Speichern",<br>• *When* saved,<br>• *Then* preferences SHALL be committed to `SharedPreferences`, `BackupWorker.schedule()` SHALL be called, and the sheet SHALL dismiss.<br><br>**Invariants**: Dropbox OAuth PKCE redirect scheme (`db-iknmdmr31sf64r0`), `SP_DROPBOX_CREDENTIAL` and `SP_UPLOAD_TO_DROPBOX` keys, `automated_backups` and `backup_interval_days` keys in `SharedPreferences`, and un-tinted Dropbox logo colors MUST NOT be altered. | Modernize Dropbox settings into a modal bottom sheet matching Export, Units, and Display settings, eliminating full-screen fragment replacement and preserving all backup and authentication mechanics. | `DropboxSettingsDialog.kt`, `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment.kt`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt` | `TST-UI-105` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-153** | **Strava Settings Modal Bottom Sheet & Navigation Integration.** | The system SHALL eliminate the full-screen fragment outlier for Strava settings by replacing `StravaUploadFragment` with a standardized modal bottom sheet (`StravaSettingsDialog` / `StravaSettingsDialogFragment` backed by `AppModalBottomSheet`), integrating with the navigation drawer and preserving all connection, synchronization, and selective upload mechanics:<br>1. *Modal Bottom Sheet Architecture (`StravaSettingsDialog`)*:<br>• The dialog SHALL compose `AppModalBottomSheet` with authentic Strava logo (`R.drawable.logo_square_strava` via `iconPainter`, `iconTint = Color.Unspecified`), localized title (`@string/Strava`), dismiss handling (drag handle, scrim tap, close icon), and window insets (`navigationBarsPadding()`, `imePadding()`).<br>• *Connection Section*: The dialog SHALL display the current connection state (`@string/strava_connected_status` in green vs. `@string/strava_disconnected_status` in error color). When connecting, it SHALL display a progress indicator with `@string/please_wait`. When disconnected, it SHALL provide a button initiating OAuth via `StravaHelper.requestAccessToken(context)`. When connected, it SHALL provide a disconnect button (`@string/strava_disconnect`) styled with `errorContainer` / `onErrorContainer`, revoking tokens via `TrainingApplication.deleteStravaToken()`, executing `StravaDeauthorizationThread`, and updating UI state. Upon successful token exchange (`StravaAuthState.Success`), it SHALL trigger initial sync of equipment (`StravaEquipmentSynchronizeThread`), segments (`SegmentsRepository.syncSegmentsAsync`), and routes (`RoutesRepository.syncRoutesFromStravaAsync`), resetting auth state.<br>• *Manual Synchronization Section (visible when connected)*: The dialog SHALL provide actions to synchronize Strava equipment (`@string/updateStravaEquipment`) and Strava routes (`@string/updateStravaRoutes`), displaying their respective last updated timestamps (`TrainingApplication.getLastUpdateTimeOfStravaEquipment()`, `TrainingApplication.getLastUpdateTimeOfStravaRoutes()`). Tapping triggers background synchronization threads/coroutines.<br>• *Selective Upload Section (visible when connected)*: The dialog SHALL display interactive toggles for selective telemetry upload (`@string/selectiveUpload`) including GPS (`uploadStravaGPS`), Altitude (`uploadStravaAltitude`), Heart Rate (`uploadStravaHR`), Power (`uploadStravaPower`), and Cadence (`uploadStravaCadence`), all defaulting to `true`.<br>2. *Standard Action Bar Integration (`AppDialogActions`)*:<br>• The bottom sheet SHALL integrate `AppDialogActions.SaveCancel`:<br>  - Secondary action: borderless `TextButton` ("Abbrechen" / `@string/Cancel`) dismissing the dialog and discarding uncommitted selective upload preference modifications.<br>  - Primary action: filled `Button` ("Speichern" / `@string/save`) committing staged selective upload preferences to `SharedPreferences` and dismissing the dialog.<br>3. *DialogFragment Host (`StravaSettingsDialogFragment`)*:<br>• The dialog SHALL be hosted by `StravaSettingsDialogFragment` extending `DialogFragment` with `STYLE_NORMAL, android.R.style.Theme_Translucent_NoTitleBar`.<br>4. *Navigation Drawer & Preference Routing*:<br>• In `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt`, selecting `R.id.drawer_strava` SHALL close the navigation drawer and present `StravaSettingsDialogFragment` without replacing the main screen fragment. Preference screen routing for `TrainingApplication.PREFERENCE_SCREEN_STRAVA` in `onPreferenceStartScreen` SHALL launch `StravaSettingsDialogFragment`.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete opening the navigation drawer,<br>• *When* "Strava" is selected,<br>• *Then* `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL present as an `AppModalBottomSheet` anchored to the screen bottom with "Abbrechen" and "Speichern", leaving the background screen visible beneath the scrim.<br>• *Given* an athlete tapping "Mit Strava verknüpfen" in the dialog,<br>• *When* OAuth authorization completes via browser and `StravaOAuthCallbackActivity`,<br>• *Then* `StravaSettingsDialog` SHALL reflect connected status and trigger background synchronizations.<br>• *Given* an athlete modifying selective upload toggles,<br>• *When* "Abbrechen" is tapped,<br>• *Then* changes SHALL be discarded without mutating `SharedPreferences`.<br>• *Given* an athlete modifying selective upload toggles and tapping "Speichern",<br>• *When* saved,<br>• *Then* preferences SHALL be committed to `SharedPreferences` and the sheet SHALL dismiss.<br><br>**Invariants**: Strava OAuth callback URL prefix `strava://rainerblind.github.io`, `SP_STRAVA_TOKEN` and token expiration handling, `uploadStravaGPS`, `uploadStravaAltitude`, `uploadStravaHR`, `uploadStravaPower`, and `uploadStravaCadence` preference keys in `SharedPreferences`, and un-tinted Strava logo colors MUST NOT be altered. | Modernize Strava settings into a modal bottom sheet matching Export, Units, Display, and Dropbox settings, eliminating full-screen fragment replacement and preserving all synchronization and authentication mechanics. | `StravaSettingsDialog.kt`, `StravaSettingsDialogFragment.kt`, `MainActivityWithNavigation.kt` | `TST-UI-106` | Verified |

`startSearchWhenAppStarts` (default: `true`, title: `@string/prefsStartSearchWhenAppStartsTitle`)<br>  - `startSearchWhenResumeFromPaused` (default: `true`, title: `@string/prefsStartSearchWhenResumeFromPausedTitle`)<br>  -
