architecture by extending Google Material's `BottomSheetDialogFragment`, eliminating double-window nesting, pitch-black status bars, and navigation bar dismiss flickering:<br>1. *Dedicated Theme Overlay (`ThemeOverlay.aTrainingTracker.BottomSheetDialogFragment`)*:<br>• In `themes.xml`, the system SHALL define `ThemeOverlay.aTrainingTracker.BottomSheetDialogFragment` inheriting from `ThemeOverlay.MaterialComponents.BottomSheetDialog`.<br>• The theme overlay SHALL set `android:windowDrawsSystemBarBackgrounds` to `true`.<br>• The theme overlay SHALL explicitly set `android:navigationBarColor` and `android:statusBarColor` to `@android:color/transparent`.<br>• The theme overlay SHALL set `android:enforceNavigationBarContrast` and `android:enforceStatusBarContrast` to `false` (targetApi 29).<br>• The theme overlay SHALL set `android:windowLightNavigationBar` (targetApi 27) and `android:windowLightStatusBar` to `true`.<br>• The theme overlay SHALL set `android:windowBackground` to `@android:color/transparent` and enable a single window dim scrim.<br>2. *Base Class (`AppBottomSheetDialogFragment`)*:<br>• In `com.atrainingtracker.trainingtracker.ui.components.core`, the system SHALL provide an abstract base class `AppBottomSheetDialogFragment` inheriting directly from `com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialogFragment`.<br>• In `onCreate()`, `AppBottomSheetDialogFragment` SHALL apply `setStyle(STYLE_NORMAL, R.style.ThemeOverlay_aTrainingTracker_BottomSheetDialogFragment)`.<br>• In `onStart()`, `AppBottomSheetDialogFragment` SHALL configure the single `BottomSheetDialog` window: setting layout to `MATCH_PARENT`, setting background drawable to transparent, ensuring `WindowCompat.setDecorFitsSystemWindows(window, false)`, configuring transparent system bars with `FLAG_DRAWS_SYSTEM_BAR_BACKGROUNDS`, disabling contrast enforcement (`isNavigationBarContrastEnforced = false`, `isStatusBarContrastEnforced = false`), and synchronizing light system bars.<br>3. *Direct Compose Content (No Nested ModalBottomSheet)*:<br>• In `onCreateView()`, the 7 dialog fragments SHALL return a `ComposeView` hosting their respective settings composable wrapped in `ATrainingTrackerTheme`.<br>• The dialog contents SHALL render the bottom sheet layout (drag handle, header, scrollable body, actions) directly within the single window *without* invoking Compose's `ModalBottomSheet`, eliminating the redundant second `ComponentDialog` window.<br>4. *Consolidated DialogFragment Hosts*:<br>• All 7 bottom popup dialog fragments SHALL extend `AppBottomSheetDialogFragment`:<br>  - `SearchSettingsDialogFragment`<br>  - `ExportSettingsDialogFragment`<br>  - `UnitsSettingsDialogFragment`<br>  - `StravaSettingsDialogFragment`<br>  - `DropboxSettingsDialogFragment`<br>  - `ActivityTypeSelectionDialogFragment`<br>  - `DisplaySettingsDialogFragment`<br>• Companion object contracts (`TAG`, `newInstance()`, and listener interfaces) SHALL remain strictly identical.<br>5. *Dismiss Transition & System Bar Parity*:<br>• When a popup is displayed, the top status bar SHALL remain transparent with a single dim scrim over the underlying Activity screen (never solid black).<br>• When dismissed via back button, back gesture, scrim tap, or action button, `BottomSheetDialogFragment` SHALL slide down smoothly with zero black navigation bar flicker.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* any bottom popup dialog,<br>• *When* the popup is displayed on Android 17 (API 37),<br>• *Then* the status bar SHALL show the underlying activity dimmed by a single scrim without any solid black block.<br>• *When* the popup is dismissed via system back button, gesture, scrim tap, or close button,<br>• *Then* the navigation bar area SHALL remain transparent throughout the transition with zero black flash.<br>• *Given* the 7 DialogFragment classes,<br>• *When* inspected via reflection in `com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialogFragment`.<br><br>**Invariants**: Fragment companion `newInstance()` and `TAG` signatures, `MainActivityWithNavigation` drawer routing, MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Eliminate double-window dialog nesting, eliminate pitch-black status bars, unify bottom sheet lifecycle on Material's BottomSheetDialogFragment, and enforce controls, tab-aware sport type selection, and sport-aware equipment selection across the workout aftermath views:<br>1. *Tab-Aware Sport Sub-Type Selection*:<br>• When viewing the "Bike" tab (`BSportType.BIKE`), the sport filter multi-choice selector in `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet` SHALL present only cycling-related sport types (`it.bSportType == BSportType.BIKE`).<br>• When viewing the "Run" tab (`BSportType.RUN`), the sport filter selector SHALL present only running-related sport types (`it.bSportType == BSportType.RUN`).<br>• When viewing the "Other" tab (`BSportType.UNKNOWN`), the sport filter selector SHALL present only non-bike, non-run sport types (`it.bSportType != BSportType.BIKE && it.bSportType != BSportType.RUN`).<br>• When viewing the "All" tab (`activeBSportType == null`), the sport filter selector SHALL present all recorded sport types.<br>2. *Sport-Aware Equipment Selection*:<br>• The equipment selector in `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet` SHALL present only equipment associated with workouts matching the active sport context:<br>  - If a specific sport is selected (`localSportId != null`), only equipment associated with that sport ID SHALL be displayed.<br>  - If no specific sport is selected but an active tab sport type is present (`activeBSportType != null`), only equipment associated with that base sport type SHALL be displayed.<br>  - If no sport or tab filter is active (`activeBSportType == null && localSportId == null`), all recorded equipment SHALL be displayed.<br>  - If the currently selected equipment (`localEquipId`) is not part of the newly filtered available equipment list (e.g. when the athlete switches sport or tab), `localEquipId` SHALL automatically be cleared (`null`).<br>3. *Date Interval Filtering*:<br>• The filter bottom sheet SHALL provide custom date interval controls with start and end date pickers (`startDateS` and `endDateS`), allowing the athlete to define arbitrary date ranges $[T_{\text{start}}, T_{\text{end}}]$ with day precision.<br>4. *Distance Interval Filtering*:<br>• The filter criteria SHALL support both lower bound (`minDistanceMeters: Double?`) and upper bound (`maxDistanceMeters: Double?`), enabling filtering within $[D_{\text{min}}, D_{\text{max}}]$.<br>• The filter bottom sheet SHALL allow setting lower and upper distance boundaries.<br>5. *Duration/Time Interval Filtering*:<br>• The filter criteria SHALL support both lower bound (`minDurationSec: Long?`) and upper bound (`maxDurationSec: Long?`), enabling filtering within $[T_{\text{min}}, T_{\text{max}}]$.<br>• The filter bottom sheet SHALL allow setting lower and upper duration boundaries.<br>6. *Serialization & Backward Compatibility*:<br>• `WorkoutFilterCriteria` JSON serialization (`toJson` / `fromJson`) SHALL preserve all existing keys and serialize `maxDistanceMeters` and `maxDurationSec` with null-safe fallbacks.<br>7. *Active Filter Count*:<br>• `activeFilterCount` in `WorkoutFilterCriteria` SHALL correctly reflect date range, distance range, and duration range selections.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* the athlete is on the "Bike" tab and opens `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet`,<br>• *When* inspecting the sport sub-type multi-choice list,<br>• *Then* only cycling sport types SHALL be listed (zero running or other sports).<br>• *Given* the athlete selects a sport (or is on the "Bike" tab),<br>• *When* inspecting the equipment options in `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet`,<br>• *Then* only equipment used in that sport (or base sport type) SHALL be displayed.<br>• *Given* an equipment filter is active and the athlete selects a sport type that does not use this equipment,<br>• *When* the available equipment list updates,<br>• *Then* the active equipment selection SHALL automatically clear (`null`).<br>• *Given* the athlete selects a distance interval (e.g. 20 km to 50 km),<br>• *When* applied,<br>• *Then* only workouts with $20000 \le \text{distance} \le 50000$ SHALL be displayed.<br>• *Given* the athlete selects a duration interval (e.g. 30 min to 90 min),<br>• *When* applied,<br>• *Then* only workouts with $1800 \le \text{duration} \le 5400$ SHALL be displayed.<br>• *Given* the athlete selects a date interval,<br>• *When* applied,<br>• *Then* only workouts recorded between the start date and end date inclusive SHALL be displayed.<br><br>**Invariants**: Sorting orders, compact view toggle, deletion workflows, and tab-level `BSportType` partitioning MUST NOT be altered. | Provide granular, tab-contextualized | **REQ-UI-158** | **Retired Equipment Isolation in Pickers, Visual Identification & Equipment Lifecycle Management.** | The system SHALL isolate retired equipment from active selection interfaces while maintaining historical workout attribution and providing clear equipment lifecycle controls:<br>1. *Active Workout Equipment Picker Isolation (`EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`)*:<br>• When presenting equipment options in `EditWorkoutBottomSheet` (`updateEquipmentNames`), the system SHALL filter out all equipment marked as retired (`isRetired == true`).<br>• *Current Assignment Invariant*: If the workout currently being edited is already assigned to a retired equipment (`equipmentId == it.id` or `equipmentName == it.name`), the system SHALL retain that specific retired equipment in the options list, ensuring existing historical assignments remain visible, selectable, and not inadvertently cleared or reset.<br>• Secondary expansion tokens (`showAllBikes()`, `showAllShoes()`) SHALL likewise filter out retired equipment, retaining only active items and any currently assigned retired item.<br>2. *Tracking & Hardware Sensor Identity Resolution (`EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.kt`)*:<br>• When inferring workout identity or linked equipment from active hardware sensors (`resolveIdentity`, `getLinkedEquipmentIds`), the system SHALL exclude any equipment marked as retired (`Retired == 1`). A retired equipment MUST NOT be automatically linked or assigned to newly recorded workouts.<br>3. *Sport Type Equipment Association (`EditSportTypeDialog.kt`, `SportTypeViewModel.kt`)*:<br>• When associating equipment with sport types in `availableEquipment(bSportType)`, the system SHALL exclude retired equipment so newly created or edited sport types are not linked to obsolete gear.<br>4. *Equipment Management & Visual Distinction (`EquipmentTabsScreen.kt`, `EquipmentViewModel.kt`)*:<br>• The equipment list in `EquipmentTabsScreen` SHALL visually distinguish active equipment from retired equipment:<br>  - Retired items SHALL display an explicit localized "Retired" badge/indicator.<br>  - The equipment screen SHALL organize items into distinct "Active" and "Retired" sections (or provide a toggle to show/hide retired items), keeping the active fleet clear and prominent.<br>5. *Manual Equipment Retirement Lifecycle (`EditEquipmentDialog.kt`, `EquipmentDbHelper.java`)*:<br>• `EditEquipmentDialog` SHALL provide a toggle/switch for retirement status ("Retired" / "In Ruhestand"), allowing athletes to manually retire active equipment or restore retired equipment.<br>• `EquipmentDbHelper` SHALL persist retirement changes via `updateEquipment` / `setEquipmentRetired` to SQLite `Equipment.db`.<br>6. *Historical Workout Display Fidelity (`WorkoutDataMapper.kt`, `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet.kt`)*:<br>• Display of historical workouts in compact summaries, expanded headers, and extrema tables SHALL continue to resolve equipment name and frame/sport icons by equipment ID regardless of retirement status.<br>• Workout list filtering in `WorkoutFilterBottomSheet` SHALL continue to dynamically derive available equipment from historical workouts, ensuring past activities on retired gear remain filterable.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an active equipment "Road Bike" and a retired equipment "Old Mountain Bike" in `Equipment.db`,<br>• *When* editing a workout with no equipment assigned (or an active equipment assigned),<br>• *Then* the equipment dropdown in `EditWorkoutBottomSheet` SHALL display "Road Bike" and SHALL NOT display "Old Mountain Bike".<br>• *Given* a historical workout already assigned to "Old Mountain Bike" (which is retired),<br>• *When* opening `EditWorkoutBottomSheet` for this workout,<br>• *Then* the equipment selector SHALL display "Old Mountain Bike" as the selected option, and "Old Mountain Bike" SHALL be present in the available options.<br>• *Given* a sensor linked to a retired bike,<br>• *When* `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.resolveIdentity` executes for a new tracking session,<br>• *Then* the retired bike SHALL NOT be inferred as the workout's `equipmentId`.<br>• *Given* the Equipment screen (`EquipmentTabsScreen`),<br>• *When* viewing the list of bikes or shoes,<br>• *Then* retired items SHALL display a localized "Retired" badge and be grouped in the retired section.<br>• *Given* the user edits an active bike in `EditEquipmentDialog` and toggles "Retired" to true,<br>• *When* saving changes,<br>• *Then* `Equipment.db` SHALL update `Retired = 1`, and the bike SHALL move to the retired section.<br><br>**Invariants**: Existing SQLite schema (v2) and data in `Equipment.db` MUST NOT be dropped or corrupted; historical workout summaries and TCX/GPX exports MUST NOT lose equipment names or frame types. | Prevent retired equipment from cluttering active workout pickers and sensor inference, while providing visual distinction and retirement controls in the equipment manager and preserving 100% historical workout attribution. | `EditWorkoutViewModel.kt`, `EquipmentTabsScreen.kt`, `EditEquipmentDialog.kt`, `EquipmentDbHelper.java`, `EquipmentViewModel.kt`, `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.kt` | `TST-UI-111` | Verified |

| **REQ-PRO-006** | **Thread-Safe Sensor Management.** | The sensor management system SHALL be thread-safe to prevent race conditions during registration and unregistration of hardware sensors across multiple threads. | `MySensorManager.java`, `MyDevice.java` | `TST-STR-011` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-007** | **Traceable Test Execution.** | The system SHALL integrate test verification into the Jira workflow by automatically creating sub-tasks for each test case defined in `docs/tests.md` that is relevant to the current ticket. | `jira_util.py`, `project_protocol.md` | `TST-STR-012` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-010** | **Traceable Implementation Planning.** | The development workflow SHALL include the creation of a dedicated Jira sub-task for every Implementation Plan to ensure high-visibility traceability and progress tracking. | `project_protocol.md` | `TST-PRO-003` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-008** | **Automated Attachment Management.** | The development workflow SHALL support automated downloading of all attachments from a Jira ticket to a local documentation folder to facilitate analysis. | `jira_util.py` | `TST-PRO-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-009** | **Type-Aware Task Management.** | The development workflow SHALL provide visibility into Jira issue types (Bug, Task, Story) during listing and inspection to ensure appropriate processing logic. | `jira_util.py` | `TST-PRO-002` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-011** | **Mandatory Code Documentation.** | To ensure long-term maintainability and architectural transparency, all core system components SHALL utilize comprehensive internal documentation. Every class MUST feature a purpose-driven header, and every method MUST include a functional description detailing its logic and synchronization patterns. | `project_protocol.md`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-STR-015` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-003** | **Pause Distance Isolation.** | The system SHALL prevent distance accumulation while paused to ensure workout statistics only reflect active movement. | `MyAccumulatorSensor.java`, `ANTBikeSpeedDevice.java`, `BTLEBikeDevice.java` | `TST-UNT-008`, `TST-REG-004` | Verified |
| **REQ-PRO-004** | **Pause Track Isolation.** | The system SHALL prevent track and profile accumulation while paused, ensuring they strictly reflect active periods. | `BANALServiceRepository.kt` | `TST-UI-039` | Verified |

## 13. High-Fidelity Navigation (ATT-131)

| ID | Description | Rationale | Implementation File(s) | Verification ID | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-FIL-007** | EKF Sensor Fusion. | The system SHALL implement an Extended Kalman Filter to fuse GPS, IMU (Accel/Mag), and Hardware Speed Sensors for high-precision state estimation. | TBD | `TST-FUSION-001` | Backlog |
| **REQ-FIL-008** | Body-to-World Frame Transformation. | The system SHALL rotate raw phone MEMS data into the geographic East-North-Up (ENU) frame using a 3x3 Rotation Matrix or Quaternions. | TBD | `TST-FUSION-001` | Backlog |
| **REQ-FIL-009** | Dead Reckoning Navigation. | In GPS-denied environments, the system SHALL maintain location and speed predictions using integrated IMU and wheel speed data. | TBD | `TST-FUSION-002` | Backlog |
| **REQ-FIL-010** | Speed Sensor Auto-Calibration. | The system SHALL dynamically solve for the "Wheel Scale Factor" within the EKF state vector to automate circumference calibration. | TBD | `TST-FUSION-003` | Backlog |
| **REQ-FIL-011** | **Null-Safe Sensor Filtering.** | All sensor filters SHALL gracefully handle null values passed from the sensor manager. The filters SHALL NOT attempt to process null data and SHALL NOT crash when a sensor provides a null value (e.g., during initialization or signal loss). | `MyFilter.java`, `AverageFilter.java`, `ExponentialSmoothingFilter.java`, `NumberedMovingAverageFilter.java` | `TST-FIL-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-NFR-001** | Deterministic EKF Timing. | The estimation loop SHALL use hardware event timestamps and dynamic Delta-T calculation to ensure mathematical robustness against Android CPU jitter. | TBD | `TST-STR-010` | Backlog |
| **REQ-NFR-002** | Stationary Pinning. | The system SHALL detect "Not Moving" events via IMU variance and coordinate clustering to prevent stationary "Drunkard's Walk" artifacts. | TBD | `TST-FUSION-004` | Backlog |

| **REQ-STP-001** | **Multilingual Store Presence.** | The system SHALL provide localized App Titles, Short Descriptions, and Full Descriptions for the Google Play Store in all supported languages. These descriptions SHALL emphasize core automation, sensor intelligence, and privacy features. | `docs/store_presence/` | `TST-STP-001` | Verified |
| **REQ-STP-002** | **Modernized Product Messaging.** | The Play Store description SHALL reflect the latest architectural improvements (Route Clusters, Jetpack Compose UI) and emphasize zero-configuration tracking for serious athletes. | `docs/store_presence/` | `TST-STP-002` | Verified |

|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **REQ-MIG-001** | **State Bundling.** | The system SHALL be able to bundle all internal SQLite databases (including high-fidelity samples) and shared preferences into a single portable archive (`.attbackup`). | `BackupManager` | `TST-MIG-001` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-002** | **Multi-Destination Backup.** | The system SHALL support both manual backup (via Android SAF) and direct upload to a linked Dropbox account. | `BackupManager`, `DropboxIntegration` | `TST-MIG-001` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-003** | **Regular Cloud Backups.** | The system SHALL optionally perform automated background backups to Dropbox to ensure disaster recovery (e.g., lost/stolen device). | `BackupWorkManager` | `TST-MIG-003` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-004** | **Full Migration Mode.** | The system SHALL support a "Full Restore" mode that replaces the entire local state with a backup, followed by a forced app restart to ensure consistency. | `MigrationEngine` | `TST-MIG-001` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-005** | **Incremental Import.** | The system SHALL support merging backups into an existing database. It MUST perform deduplication using the `FILE_BASE_NAME` as the unique identifier. Legacy recovery SHALL also skip files that already exist in the database. | `ImportEngine.kt`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt` | `TST-MIG-002` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-013** | **Idempotent Recovery.** | The legacy recovery process SHALL NOT trigger clustering prompts for workouts that are already present in the database or already have a cluster assigned. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt` | `TST-MIG-010` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-014** | **Queued User Interaction.** | The system SHALL decouple user interaction requests (e.g., cluster naming) from background file importing. While an interaction is pending user resolution, the TCX import engine SHALL continue downloading and processing subsequent files concurrently in the background, queuing up to 3 pending user interactions without blocking progress. **Invariants**: Progress indicators and status updates MUST remain visible during background importing. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-017` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-006** | **State Lockdown.** | Backup and Restore operations SHALL ONLY be allowed when the system is in a non-tracking state to prevent data corruption. | `MigrationUI` | `TST-MIG-001` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-007** | **Full Restore Confirmation.** | The system SHALL require explicit user confirmation before initiating a "Full Restore" from Dropbox. | `BackupRestoreScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-004` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-008** | **Informative Dashboard Labels.** | The Backup \u0026 Restore dashboard SHALL provide clear descriptive text for all operations. The Dropbox backup button SHALL be labeled \"Create \u0026 Upload Backup to Dropbox\". Destructive and merging sections SHALL feature high-level descriptions explaining their impact on local data. | `BackupRestoreScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-005` | Proposed |
| **REQ-MIG-011** | **Tabbed Import \u0026 Backup UI.** | The system's data management interface SHALL be organized into a three-tabbed layout: Import, Backup, and Restore. This layout SHALL use the project's standard `HorizontalPager` pattern for consistent navigation. The screen SHALL be prominently labeled \"Import \u0026 Backup\". | `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-008` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-012** | **Responsive Pre-Import Workout Cluster Tuning Bottom Sheet.** | The system SHALL provide access to workout clustering parameters (spatial tolerances, sport type firewall, and altitude position extrema) within an interactive Material 3 `ModalBottomSheet` prior to executing a bulk or single-file TCX workout import. The bottom sheet SHALL span the available screen width and dynamically adapt vertically, displaying the title (`cluster_tuning_title`), an integrated educational Info button (`Icons.Outlined.Info`) triggering the `ClusterInfoDialog`, explanatory guidance, and a scrollable `ClusterTuningContent` layout. The bottom sheet SHALL provide explicit confirmation and dismissal actions.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>1. *Given* a single legacy TCX import or bulk TCX archive recovery is triggered from the Import tab,<br>2. *When* the pre-import tuning interface is displayed,<br>3. *Then* it SHALL render as a Material 3 `ModalBottomSheet` with a drag handle, header title, Info icon button, and scrollable controls.<br>4. *When* the user taps the Info button in the header,<br>5. *Then* the system SHALL present the `ClusterInfoDialog` on top without dismissing the tuning sheet.<br>6. *When* the user adjusts sensitivity controls and confirms via OK,<br>7. *Then* the system MUST persist the selected tolerances to `SharedPreferences` and commence file importing.<br><br>**Invariants**: User-selected tolerances MUST be persisted to shared preferences upon confirmation. The underlying mathematical clustering engine, Room database entities, and file import workflows MUST remain unaltered. | `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt`, `ClusterTuningScreen.kt`, `ClusterInfoDialog.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-009` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-009** | **Default Backup Interval.** | The system SHALL default the automated backup interval to 1 day (Daily) to maximize data safety for new users. | `prefs_dropbox.xml`, `BackupWorker.kt` | `TST-MIG-006` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-010** | **Enable Backups by Default.** | The system SHALL enable the automated backup feature by default for new installations. | `prefs_dropbox.xml`, `BackupWorker.kt` | `TST-MIG-007` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-015** | **Efficient Legacy Recovery.** | The legacy recovery process SHALL skip already imported workouts BEFORE downloading them from remote storage to ensure optimal performance and bandwidth usage. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt` | `TST-MIG-011` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-016** | **Paginated & Recursive Cloud Recovery.** | The system SHALL robustly scan and paginate cloud storage paths (e.g., `/TCX`, `/apps/Workouts/TCX` and their subdirectories) using `.withRecursive(true)` and `listFolderContinue()` with error recovery to discover all TCX workout files regardless of folder size. **Invariants**: Deduplication (`isWorkoutExisting`) MUST continue using `FILE_BASE_NAME` as the unique identifier. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-018` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-021** | **Informative Import Summary.** | Upon completing a bulk import, the system SHALL display a detailed summary stating the number of newly imported workouts, the number of skipped/already existing workouts, and the total number of files scanned. **Invariants**: Success and error messages MUST be accurately formatted. | `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `strings.xml` | `TST-MIG-018` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-022** | **Preserve Original Filename During Import.** | The TCX import engine SHALL preserve the original source filename when storing `FILE_BASE_NAME` and `WORKOUT_NAME` in the database. | **REQ-MIG-023** | **Unlinked Cloud Feature Deactivation.** | When the user is not authenticated or connected to Dropbox, all cloud-dependent features (including scanning/importing legacy TCX files, cloud backups, and cloud restores) SHALL be visually deactivated in the UI (rendered in a muted/grey state). Clicking a deactivated cloud action SHALL present an informational dialog explaining that Dropbox is disconnected. **Invariants**: Credential reading and background jobs MUST NOT throw null pointer exceptions when unlinked. | `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `DropboxUploader.java` | `TST-MIG-020` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-017** | **Limited Interaction Queue & Backpressure.** | The system SHALL limit the number of concurrent pending UI interactions to a maximum of 3 using a semaphore (`interactionSemaphore(3)`). The background import engine MUST ONLY suspend file downloading and parsing when 3 candidate interactions are active in `_interactionQueue`, automatically resuming as the user resolves queued items. **Invariants**: Queue size MUST NOT exceed 3 items. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-017` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-018** | **Sparse Data Import.** | The TCX import engine SHALL dynamically detect available sensors and only create database columns and summary metrics for telemetry actually present in the source file, preventing the population of zero-filled streams for missing data. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-MIG-014` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-019** | **Dropbox Wait Feedback.** | The TCX import process SHALL provide explicit visual feedback (e.g., status message) when the system is waiting for a response from the Dropbox API (e.g., during folder scanning or file downloading) to ensure the user is aware of the external dependency. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-015` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-020** | **Positive Import Branding.** | The workout import feature (formerly Legacy Recovery) SHALL be presented as a first-class feature for migrating data from previous devices. Terminology SHALL avoid negative or technical qualifiers like \"Legacy\" or \"Recovery\" and instead use active, feature-oriented language. Redundant introduction text SHALL be removed from the tabbed interface. | `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt`, `strings.xml` | `TST-MIG-016` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-024** | **High-Fidelity TCX Telemetry & Multi-Lap Structure Import.** | The TCX import engine (`LegacyImportEngine`) SHALL parse and persist high-fidelity telemetry and multi-lap structures from TCX activity files:<br>1. *Trackpoint Extensions (TPX)*: The engine SHALL parse Garmin TrackpointExtension v2 elements, extracting instantaneous speed (`<Speed>` in m/s) and running cadence (`<RunCadence>` in RPM/spm) when present, populating `SensorType.SPEED` and `SensorType.CADENCE` respectively without falling back to lossy delta approximations.<br>2. *Multi-Lap Preservation*: The engine SHALL preserve all native `<Lap>` elements across the activity. For each `<Lap>`, it SHALL persist a distinct lap record in `LapsDatabaseManager` recording the lap's `lap_nr`, `start_time`, `duration` (`<TotalTimeSeconds>`), `distance` (`<DistanceMeters>`), `avg_speed`, `max_speed` (`<MaximumSpeed>`), and `avg_hr` (`<AverageHeartRateBpm>`). It SHALL assign each buffered trackpoint sample the corresponding `SensorType.LAP_NR` value matching its parent lap.<br>3. *Active Duration & Pause Derivation*: The engine SHALL derive the total active workout duration by summing `<TotalTimeSeconds>` across all laps, ensuring paused or discontinuous intervals do not artificially inflate active workout time in `WorkoutSummaries`.<br>4. *Supplementary Metrics & Metadata*: The engine SHALL parse `<Calories>` per lap and record total aggregated calories burned in `WorkoutSummaries`. It SHALL extract activity `<Notes>` or `<Name>` and populate the workout's notes/comments field in the database.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a multi-lap TCX file containing TPX extension speed and running cadence, calories, and pause gaps between laps,<br>• *When* the workout is imported via `LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx()`,<br>• *Then* `LapsDatabaseManager` SHALL contain separate rows for each lap with valid metrics, `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager` SHALL contain `SPEED`, `CADENCE`, and accurate `LAP_NR` columns, and `WorkoutSummaries` SHALL record the true active duration, total calories, and notes.<br>**Invariants**: Sparse stream detection (`REQ-MIG-018`), automated Strava upload triggering (`REQ-EXT-008`), and original file base name preservation (`REQ-MIG-022`) MUST NOT be altered or regressed. | Ensure imported historical workouts match the analytical fidelity and segment granularity of native live recordings, avoiding loss of sensor extensions, split intervals, and activity metadata. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `LapsDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-MIG-021` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-025** | **TCX Import Cluster Decision Integrity & Unclustered Workout Preservation.** | The system SHALL strictly respect user cluster decisions when importing workouts from TCX or external files:<br>1. *User Decision Sovereignty*: When a user dismisses or chooses "Leave Unclustered" (`@string/cluster_naming__leave_unclustered`) in `ClusterNamingDialog`, the workout SHALL remain unclustered in the database with `WorkoutSummaries.CLUSTER_ID = -1L`.<br>2. *Prohibition of Auto-Clustering*: The system MUST NOT automatically create a new `WorkoutCluster` or assign any cluster to a workout that has been explicitly left unclustered or has `clusterId == -1L`. Specifically, `WorkoutClusterEngine.onWorkoutFinished()` MUST NOT generate fallback clusters (e.g. `@string/cluster_default_name_format`, "Workout at %1$s") or assign clusters to workouts where `w.clusterId == -1L`.<br>3. *Import vs. Live Tracking Lifecycle Disambiguation*: When `WorkoutRepository.reloadWorkoutData()` processes a newly loaded or imported workout, it MUST NOT invoke live cluster creation or discovery logic on workouts whose clustering was already finalized or deliberately rejected during import.<br>4. *Name & State Preservation*: The imported workout SHALL preserve its original name and file base name, and SHALL appear in the "Unclustered" tab of the Favorite Tracks / Workout Clusters view (`WorkoutClustersTabsScreen`) and in the workout summaries list without an auto-generated cluster name prefix or suffix.<br>**Acceptance Criteria**:<br>• *Given* a TCX workout imported via `LegacyImportEngine` that does not match an existing cluster,<br>• *When* the user selects "Leave Unclustered" in `ClusterNamingDialog`,<br>• *Then* the workout SHALL be saved with `clusterId = -1L`, NO new cluster SHALL be created in `WorkoutClusterDatabaseManager`, the workout name SHALL NOT be altered to a cluster default name, and the workout SHALL appear under the Unclustered tab.<br>**Invariants**: User-confirmed cluster assignments during TCX import (selecting an existing cluster or providing a custom route name) MUST continue to link the cluster and increment its hit count; live tracking sessions completed via `TrackerService` MUST continue to update cluster metrics and spatial bounds when a cluster match is found; periods aggregation in `PeriodsRepository` MUST continue to process all newly imported and finished workouts; existing SQLite database schema for `WorkoutSummaries` and `WorkoutCluster` MUST NOT be altered. | Prevent unwanted cluster creation and preserve user sovereignty over workout categorization during TCX file imports. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt` | `TST-MIG-022` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-026** | **TCX Import Reactive Period Aggregation, Direct Pipeline Notification & Self-Healing Period Sync.** | The system SHALL ensure that all workouts imported from TCX (single file or cloud bulk recovery) immediately and deterministically appear in the Workout Periods analytics views (Days, Weeks, Months, Years):<br>1. *Direct Repository Notification Pipeline*: Upon completing statistical recalculation for an imported workout (`recalculateStats`), `LegacyImportEngine` SHALL NOT rely exclusively on unbuffered `LocalBroadcastManager` broadcasts. It SHALL directly invoke `WorkoutRepository.getInstance(app).reloadWorkoutData(workoutId)` and ensure `PeriodsRepository.getInstance(app).onWorkoutFinished(freshWorkoutData)` executes to aggregate the session into its Day period and propagate metrics upward (Day -> Week/Month -> Year).<br>2. *Self-Healing Period Integrity Check*: `PeriodsRepository` SHALL implement an $O(1)$ fast integrity check comparing the total count of finished workouts in `WorkoutSummaries` against the sum of `total_workouts` across all `DAY` periods in `PeriodSummaries.db`. If a discrepancy is detected (e.g., following background imports, process death, or database restoration), `PeriodsRepository` SHALL automatically trigger asynchronous hierarchical re-synchronization (`performHierarchicalMigration()`), guaranteeing that no imported sessions remain omitted from historical period cards, charts, and heatmaps.<br>3. *Post-Import Batch Reconciliation*: Upon completion of single-file import or bulk recovery in `BackupRestoreViewModel`, the system SHALL explicitly trigger `periodsRepo.loadPeriods()`, `workoutRepo.loadAllWorkouts()`, and `clusterRepo.refreshClusters()`.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an app state where `WorkoutRepository` is not pre-instantiated in memory,<br>• *When* a historical TCX workout is imported via `LegacyImportEngine`,<br>• *Then* `PeriodsRepository.onWorkoutFinished()` SHALL be invoked, creating or updating the Day, Week, Month, and Year periods corresponding to the workout's timestamp.<br>• *Given* an out-of-sync state where `WorkoutSummaries` contains finished workouts lacking entries in `PeriodSummaries.db`,<br>• *When* `PeriodsRepository` initializes or loads from database,<br>• *Then* the self-healing count check SHALL detect the discrepancy and trigger `performHierarchicalMigration()`, reconciling all missing periods.<br><br>**Invariants**: Existing date grouping logic (`getPeriodSortKey`), spatial boundary aggregation, and O(1) live tracking completion rollups MUST NOT be altered. | Guarantee that imported workouts are fully integrated into temporal analytics and period summaries regardless of repository instantiation timing or background import mode. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `WorkoutRepository.kt`, `PeriodsRepository.kt`, `PeriodSummariesDatabaseManager.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-023` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-027** | **TCX Import Cluster Assignment, Multi-Sport Heuristics & In-Memory Cache Refresh.** | The system SHALL ensure that workouts imported from TCX are accurately evaluated against route clusters without false rejections due to unmapped sports, and that cluster state is reactively reflected in the UI:<br>1. *Multi-Sport & Unknown Sport Inference*: When evaluating cluster candidates in `LegacyImportEngine` (`suggestCluster`), if `bSportType == BSportType.UNKNOWN`, the engine MUST NOT pass `setOf(BSportType.UNKNOWN)` (which incurs a punitive `+5.0` penalty in `calculateSimilarity`). Instead, it SHALL evaluate candidate sports using `EquipmentAndSportTypeDiscoveryManager.getCandidateBSportTypes(bSportType, avgSpeed)` or pass `emptySet()` to enable multi-sport candidate evaluation.<br>2. *Authoritative Workout Name Heuristic*: `LegacyImportEngine` SHALL pass the extracted `workoutName` (from TCX notes or activity extensions) into `suggestCluster` to enable the 50% similarity bonus (`totalScore *= 0.5`) when the workout name matches an existing cluster name.<br>3. *Sport Column Persistence*: `LegacyImportEngine` SHALL persist `WorkoutSummaries.B_SPORT` (`bSportType.name()`) alongside `SPORT_ID` in `WorkoutSummaries`.<br>4. *Reactive In-Memory Cache Refresh*: Whenever a workout is assigned to an existing cluster or seeded into a new cluster during import, `LegacyImportEngine` / `BackupRestoreViewModel` SHALL invoke `WorkoutClusterRepository.getInstance(app).refreshClusters()`, ensuring `allClusters`, `clusterStats`, and `unclusteredWorkouts` in `WorkoutClustersViewModel` update immediately without requiring an application restart.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a TCX workout whose sport attribute is missing or maps to `UNKNOWN`, but whose geometry and speed match an existing `BIKE` or `RUN` cluster,<br>• *When* imported via `LegacyImportEngine`,<br>• *Then* the cluster engine SHALL suggest the matching cluster without the `+5.0` penalty, and assign it to the workout.<br>• *Given* a TCX workout with a name matching an existing cluster (e.g. \"Hausrunde\"),<br>• *When* evaluated for clustering,<br>• *Then* the 50% name-match similarity discount SHALL be applied.<br>• *Given* a cluster assignment during TCX import,<br>• *When* import completes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutClusterRepository.refreshClusters()` SHALL be called, updating the cluster's hit count and previews in memory.<br><br>**Invariants**: User decision sovereignty (`REQ-MIG-025`, \"Leave Unclustered\" remains `clusterId = -1`) MUST be preserved; existing spatial tolerances (endpoints, apex, distance, altitude) MUST NOT be altered. | Ensure TCX imports reliably associate with existing favorite routes and keep cluster presentations up-to-date across all screens. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `WorkoutClusterRepository.kt`, `WorkoutClustersViewModel.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt` | `TST-MIG-024` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-028** | **Optional Strava Upload on TCX Import.** | The system SHALL allow athletes to choose whether workouts imported from TCX (via single file import or cloud bulk recovery) are automatically uploaded to Strava:<br>1. *Persistent Configuration*: The system SHALL provide a persistent configuration setting (`SP_IMPORT_TCX_UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA`) in `TrainingApplication` / `SharedPreferences` indicating whether imported workouts should be uploaded to Strava. To prevent unintended historical feed flooding and Strava API quota exhaustion, this setting SHALL default to `false`.<br>2. *Pre-Import User Control*: Within the pre-import configuration sheet (`PreImportTuningBottomSheet`), when Strava is connected (`TrainingApplication.uploadToStrava() == true`), the system SHALL render an interactive toggle switch allowing the athlete to enable or disable Strava upload for the impending import. Toggling the switch SHALL immediately update the persistent setting. When Strava is not connected, the toggle switch SHALL NOT be displayed.<br>3. *Engine Parameterization*: The TCX import engine (`LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx()` and `LegacyImportEngine.bulkRecoverFromDropbox()`) SHALL accept an explicit `uploadToStrava` parameter defaulting to `TrainingApplication.uploadImportedWorkoutsToStrava()`.<br>4. *Database Flag & Upload Dispatch*: When `uploadToStrava` is `false` (or Strava is not connected), the import engine SHALL insert the workout with `WorkoutSummaries.UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA = 0`, and `LegacyImportEngine.schedulePostImportCommunityUpload()` SHALL bypass Strava upload. When `uploadToStrava` is `true` and Strava is connected, the engine SHALL insert `WorkoutSummaries.UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA = 1` and schedule post-import upload via `ExportManager`.<br>5. *Manual Upload Availability*: Workouts imported with Strava upload disabled SHALL remain eligible for subsequent manual upload via the Workout Summary or Edit Workout screens.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an athlete with Strava connected who imports a TCX workout with Strava upload toggled off (default),<br>• *When* `LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx()` executes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutSummaries.UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA` SHALL be 0, and NO upload task SHALL be enqueued to `ExportManager` or Strava WorkManager.<br>• *Given* an athlete with Strava connected who toggles Strava upload ON in the pre-import bottom sheet,<br>• *When* the TCX import executes,<br>• *Then* `WorkoutSummaries.UPLOAD_TO_STRAVA` SHALL be 1, and an upload task SHALL be enqueued to `ExportManager` for Strava.<br>• *Given* an athlete whose Strava account is disconnected,<br>• *When* the pre-import bottom sheet is presented,<br>• *Then* the Strava upload toggle switch SHALL NOT be visible.<br><br>**Invariants**: Live tracking sessions (`TrackerService`) upload lifecycle MUST NOT be affected; manual upload capability in `WorkoutSummary` and `EditWorkoutScreen` MUST remain fully functional; existing TCX parsing, metric calculations, and cluster heuristics MUST NOT be altered. | Prevent unwanted historical Strava activity flooding, duplicate activities, and API quota exhaustion during single-file or bulk TCX import while giving athletes explicit opt-in agency. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `TrainingApplication.java`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-025` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-029** | **TCX Import & Spatial Route Cluster Auto-Matching Parity & Lossless Candidate Discovery.** | The system SHALL guarantee lossless candidate evaluation and scoring parity between automated TCX import cluster matching and interactive route cluster selection:<br>1. *Lossless Candidate Discovery in `suggestCluster`*: `WorkoutClusterEngine.suggestCluster()` SHALL NOT prune candidate clusters using restrictive bounding box filters that prematurely discard valid matches whose composite similarity score is $< 1.0$. Specifically, `suggestCluster()` SHALL evaluate candidate similarity across all available route clusters (`dbManager.getAllClusters()`), guaranteeing that single-dimension deviations (such as start point displacement $> 1.0 \times \text{endpointTol}$, e.g. 250m–400m) are not pruned before their multi-component composite score is evaluated.<br>2. *Full Parameter Alignment between Auto-Matching and Interactive Dialog*: The TCX import pipeline (`LegacyImportEngine.kt`), interaction queue (`BackupRestoreViewModel.ClusterInteraction`), and cluster selection interface (`ClusterNamingDialog` / `WorkoutClusterSelectionDialog`) SHALL maintain complete parameter symmetry when invoking `calculateSimilarity()` / `scoreClusters()`. Specifically, `onNewClusterCandidate()` and `ClusterInteraction` SHALL preserve `workoutName`, `candidateSportTypes`, `minAltPos`, and `maxAltPos`. When `ClusterNamingDialog` renders the candidate route picker (`showSelectionDialog = true`), it SHALL pass `state.workoutName`, `state.candidateSportTypes`, `state.minAltPos`, and `state.maxAltPos` into `clusterEngine.scoreClusters()`.<br>3. *Deterministic Auto-Matching Invariant*: If an imported workout matches an existing route cluster with a composite similarity score $< 1.0$, `suggestCluster()` SHALL deterministically identify and auto-assign the best matching cluster (`minByOrNull { it.second }`) without presenting `ClusterNamingDialog` to the user. Conversely, `ClusterNamingDialog` SHALL only be presented when no existing cluster achieves a similarity score $< 1.0$.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* an existing route cluster in the database with start point $S_{cluster}$ and an imported TCX workout whose start point $S_{workout}$ has a displacement of 350m ($> \text{endpointTol}$ default 200m), but whose overall route geometry, apex, and distance yield a composite similarity score $< 1.0$ (e.g. 0.55),<br>• *When* the TCX workout is imported via `LegacyImportEngine.importFromTcx()`,<br>• *Then* `suggestCluster()` SHALL evaluate the cluster without premature SQLite pruning, identify it as a match, and auto-assign it without prompting the user in `ClusterNamingDialog`.<br>• *Given* an imported workout whose spatial signature fails to match any existing cluster (all similarity scores $\ge 1.0$),<br>• *When* `ClusterNamingDialog` is presented and the user opens the existing route selection dialog,<br>• *Then* every cluster score displayed in the picker SHALL be $\ge 1.0$, matching the exact parameters (`workoutName`, `candidateSportTypes`, `minAltPos`, `maxAltPos`) evaluated during import.<br><br>**Invariants**: User decision sovereignty (`REQ-MIG-025`, "Leave Unclustered" remaining `clusterId = -1L`) MUST be preserved; existing 2D and 3D altitude similarity scoring formulas (`REQ-SET-065`) MUST NOT be altered; live workout tracking completion matching in `TrackerService` MUST NOT be degraded. | Eliminate repetitive, false manual cluster selection prompts during historical TCX file import when existing route clusters match the activity within the configured similarity threshold. | `WorkoutClusterEngine.kt`, `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-026` | Verified |
| **REQ-MIG-030** | **High-Fidelity GPX Workout Import & Full Telemetry Ingestion Parity.** | The system SHALL support importing historical workouts from GPX files (single file via file picker and cloud bulk recovery from Dropbox), matching the analytical fidelity, cluster routing, and period aggregation of the TCX import pipeline:<br>1. *Spatial & Temporal Parsing*: Parse `<trk>` / `<trkseg>` / `<trkpt>` (and fallback `<rte>` / `<rtept>`) elements, extracting latitude (`lat`), longitude (`lon`), elevation (`<ele>`), and UTC timestamps (`<time>`). Compute cumulative geodesic distance (`SensorType.DISTANCE_m`) between consecutive GPS coordinates.<br>2. *Extended Telemetry (Garmin TPX & Extensions)*: Parse extension telemetry when present, including heart rate (`<gpxtpx:hr>`, `<hr>`, `<heartrate>`), cadence (`<gpxtpx:cad>`, `<cad>`, `<cadence>`), speed (`<gpxtpx:speed>`, `<speed>` in m/s), temperature (`<gpxtpx:atemp>`, `<atemp>`), and power (`<power>`, `<watts>`), populating the corresponding `SensorType` columns without creating zero-filled streams for absent sensors (`REQ-MIG-018`).<br>3. *Multi-Segment / Multi-Lap Structure*: When a GPX file contains multiple `<trkseg>` elements, the engine SHALL preserve each segment as a distinct lap in `LapsDatabaseManager` and tag buffered samples with `SensorType.LAP_NR`.<br>4. *Sport Type Detection & Heuristics*: Determine sport type from `<trk><type>`, `<name>`, or `<desc>` (e.g. "Running" -> `BSportType.RUN`, "Cycling"/"Biking"/"Ride" -> `BSportType.BIKE`, etc.), falling back to `BSportType.UNKNOWN` and multi-sport / speed-based heuristics (`REQ-MIG-027`).<br>5. *Activity Metadata & Naming*: Extract workout name from `<trk><name>` or `<metadata><name>` (or file base name if absent) and activity description/notes from `<trk><desc>` or `<metadata><desc>`.<br>6. *Pipeline Parity with TCX*: Deduplicate via `FILE_BASE_NAME` (`isWorkoutExisting`), evaluate route clustering (`suggestCluster`), sync periods (`PeriodsRepository`), and respect optional Strava upload configuration (`REQ-MIG-028`).<br>7. *UI Integration*: Single file import in `ImportBackupTabsScreen` SHALL accept `.gpx` files alongside `.tcx` files seamlessly, and Dropbox recovery SHALL support scanning and importing GPX archives. | Enable athletes to import their historical activities from GPX files with full telemetry, lap fidelity, and automatic route clustering. | `LegacyImportEngine.kt`, `BackupRestoreViewModel.kt`, `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt` | `TST-MIG-027` | Verified |
| **REQ-PER-007** | **Reactive Period-Workout Association.** | The system SHALL ensure that all workouts belonging to a specific period are correctly and reactively associated when viewing period details (Map or List). | **REQ-PER-012** | **Period Map Scalability, Memory Budget & Adaptive Layering Invariant.** | The system SHALL ensure high-performance, non-crashing spatial visualization across periods of arbitrary size (days, weeks, months, years with hundreds of workouts):<br>1. *Adaptive Layering Threshold*: When a period contains more than a configured safety threshold of member workouts (`MAX_VECTOR_WORKOUTS = 30`), the system SHALL NOT instantiate or render individual vector `Polyline`s (`memberTracks`) and individual member markers (`memberMarkers`) for all member workouts simultaneously. Instead, the spatial analytics SHALL render the high-performance raster `Heatmap` layer representing aggregate route density plus the prominent `anchorTracks` (the spatial boundary/longest routes) and period extrema markers (`extremaMarkers`).<br>2. *Interactive Single-Track Inspection*: When the athlete taps or peeks a specific workout within a large period, that single workout's path SHALL be rendered as a crisp, interactive vector polyline via `peekedTracks` without rendering all other background sessions as vector paths.<br>3. *Threshold-Filtered Detail*: When the number of displayed workouts is within the safety threshold ($\le 30$, such as in small periods or when filtered by sport type), individual member tracks and technical markers SHALL be rendered as detailed vector overlays.<br>4. *Memory & Point Budgeting*: In `PeriodsViewModel`, polyline decoding and `MapTrack` instantiation SHALL be budget-aware, avoiding redundant duplicate representations in memory.<br>5. *Process Heap Headroom*: The application manifest SHALL declare `android:largeHeap="true"` to grant the runtime sufficient memory bandwidth for GIS map overlays and raster heatmaps on high-resolution devices. | Ensure smooth, non-crashing (OOM-free) spatial rendering of long-term training periods with hundreds of sessions. | `PeriodsViewModel.kt`, `PeriodMapScreen.kt`, `InteractivePeriodMap.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml` | `TST-PER-018` | Verified |
| **REQ-PER-013** | **Multi-Region Period Map Viewport Detection & Adaptive Bounding.** | When a period contains workouts separated across geographically distinct regions ($> \text{REGION\_CLUSTER\_THRESHOLD\_METERS} = 75\text{ km}$ geodesic span), the system SHALL NOT center the initial map camera or overview thumbnail over empty oceans or continents between regions (e.g. centering over Greenland / the North Atlantic for athletes training in Europe and North America). Instead, the system SHALL partition workouts into geographic activity regions, prioritize the primary activity region, and provide interactive region switching:<br>1. *Spatial Region Partitioning & Fast-Path*: In `SpatialRegionEngine.detectRegions(workouts)`, workouts with valid GPS coordinates SHALL be partitioned into connected component clusters using Great-Circle Haversine distance with configurable parameter `REGION_CLUSTER_THRESHOLD_METERS` ($75\text{ km}$). If the global bounding box diagonal is $< \text{SINGLE\_REGION\_ENVELOPE\_METERS}$ ($60\text{ km}$) or total GPS workouts $\le 1$, the engine SHALL return a single region immediately ($O(1)$ fast-path).<br>2. *Dominant Region Identification*: When multiple regions are detected ($k > 1$), the engine SHALL rank regions deterministically: (a) Primary: Workout frequency/count (descending); (b) Secondary tie-breaker: Total active distance in meters (descending); (c) Tertiary tie-breaker: Timestamp of most recent workout (descending). The highest-ranked region SHALL be marked as primary (`isPrimary = true`, index 0).<br>3. *Initial Viewport & Thumbnail Framing*: In `PeriodSummaryCard.kt` (overview thumbnail) and `InteractivePeriodMap.kt` (detail map), the initial camera bounds SHALL focus tightly on the primary region's bounding box (`primaryRegion.bounds`), eliminating empty ocean tiles and rendering recognizable route polylines.<br>4. *Antimeridian & Polar Normalization*: When workouts within a cluster cross the $180^\circ$ meridian, `SpatialRegionEngine` SHALL construct wrapped `LatLngBounds(southwest, northeast)` with `southwest.longitude > northeast.longitude`, ensuring Google Maps fits the local $< 180^\circ$ span rather than a $350^\circ+$ world-spanning box. Polar coordinates SHALL be clamped to $[-85^\circ, 85^\circ]$.<br>5. *Interactive Region Navigation & Overflow Protection*: When `regions.size > 1`, `PeriodMapScreen.kt` SHALL render an interactive horizontally scrollable chip row (`Modifier.horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState())`) featuring `[ Primary: Region 1 (N) ]`, `[ Region 2 (N) ]`, ..., and `[ All Regions (Total) ]`. Tapping a chip SHALL update `selectedRegionId` and animate the camera to that region's bounds. Tapping \"All Regions\" SHALL fit the aggregate global bounds across all regions. When `regions.size <= 1`, the region switcher bar SHALL be completely hidden (`Visibility.GONE`).<br>6. *State Persistence & Threading*: `PeriodsViewModel` SHALL execute region detection exclusively on background worker threads (`Dispatchers.Default`) and SHALL persist `selectedRegionId` via `SavedStateHandle` across configuration changes and process recreation.<br><br>**Acceptance Criteria (Given-When-Then)**:<br>• *Given* a period containing 100 workouts in Germany (~9°E) and 10 workouts in California (~-122°W),<br>• *When* the period overview card or detail map is displayed,<br>• *Then* the initial map camera SHALL focus tightly on the German training region, with zero centering over Greenland or the North Atlantic.<br>• *When* viewing `PeriodMapScreen` for this multi-region period,<br>• *Then* an interactive horizontal chip bar SHALL appear with chips for Region 1 (100), Region 2 (10), and All Regions (110).<br>• *When* the athlete taps the Region 2 chip,<br>• *Then* the map camera SHALL animate to the California workouts.<br>• *When* the athlete taps \"All Regions\",<br>• *Then* the camera SHALL zoom out to encompass both continents.<br>• *Given* a period where all workouts are within a 50 km perimeter,<br>• *When* the period map is displayed,<br>• *Then* the region chip bar SHALL be hidden and the camera bounds SHALL match legacy bounding behavior 100%.<br><br>**Invariants**: SQLite schema for `PeriodSummaries.db` MUST NOT be altered; single-region periods MUST maintain 100% camera and visual parity; vector track limit ($\le 30$) and heatmap rendering per `REQ-PER-012` MUST NOT be regressed; all user-facing chip strings MUST maintain 9-language localization parity. | Eliminate unusable viewports centered on empty oceans (Greenland anomaly) during multi-region training periods while providing seamless 1-tap region switching and preserving single-region performance. | `SpatialRegionEngine.kt`, `PeriodsViewModel.kt`, `InteractivePeriodMap.kt`, `PeriodMapScreen.kt`, `PeriodSummaryCard.kt` | `TST-PER-019` | Verified |
| **REQ-MAP-018** | **Detailed Map View Heatmap Visibility.** | The system SHALL render density heatmaps across all map views (including detailed Workout Cluster maps and Period Detail maps) at all zoom levels (zoom 1 to 18). Heatmap point weighting and intensity scaling SHALL ensure point density remains clearly visible without vanishing at high zoom levels. **Invariants**: The heatmap overlay MUST NOT obscure map terrain or road labels and MUST NOT exceed the `maxPoints` memory budget. | `MapContentScope.kt`, `MapUtils.kt`, `PeriodMapUtils.kt`, `WorkoutClusterHeatmapScreen.kt` | `TST-MAP-020` | Verified |
| **REQ-MAP-019** | **Authoritative Sensor Extrema Spatial Positioning.** | The system SHALL bind sensor extrema (including minimum altitude `ExtremaType.MIN`, maximum altitude `ExtremaType.MAX`, and maximum speed) strictly to authoritative sensor telemetry (`getBestSensorData`), preventing secondary or noisy fallback sensors from polluting session peak values and spatial coordinates. If tracking commences before spatial fix (`currentPos == null`), the system SHALL anchor initial extrema to the first acquired valid GPS coordinate when the initial reading matches the baseline. Furthermore, legacy database queries for extrema positions SHALL filter out records lacking valid coordinates (`LATITUDE IS NOT NULL AND LONGITUDE IS NOT NULL`). | Ensure extrema markers (e.g. min/max altitude pins) accurately reflect true spatial locations on the route map. | `TrackerService.java`, `LiveWorkoutSession.java`, `WorkoutSamplesDatabaseManager.java` | `TST-MAP-021` | Verified |
| **REQ-MAP-020** | **Authoritative Workout Max Line Distance (Apex) Calculation & Self-Anchored Displacement Integrity.** | The system SHALL ensure that a recorded workout's Maximum Line Distance (Apex) value and coordinate (`maxDisplacementLatLng`) accurately and authoritatively reflect the geographical point along the workout's recorded track that has the maximum geodesic displacement from the workout's start point (`startLatLng`). `SpeedAndLocationDevice` SHALL instantiate `mLineDistanceSensor` as an instantaneous scalar gauge `MySensor<Double>` rather than an accumulator (`MyDoubleAccumulatorSensor`), preventing accumulator reset operations from corrupting displacement with negative offsets. During live workout recording, `LiveWorkoutSession` / `TrackerService` SHALL anchor line distance calculations directly to the workout's authoritative start coordinate (`startLatLng`), computing displacement dynamically rather than relying on unanchored or pre-tracking hardware sensor state. Upon workout completion, `TrackerService.finalizeLiveSession()` SHALL deterministically compute the authoritative maximum line distance and apex coordinate by evaluating the geodesic displacement of all recorded track points relative to `startPos`, persisting the result in `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager`. The system SHALL provide self-healing recalculation for existing workouts with missing, zero, or geometrically invalid apex coordinates by deriving the apex from the stored `mapPolyline` and `startLatLng`. **Invariants**: Existing raw samples database schema and non-target sensor extrema (Altitude, Speed, HR, Power) MUST NOT be altered. | Ensure the Max Line Distance marker (`ic_distance`) accurately and reliably indicates the true furthest point from the start location on the route map across all recorded workouts. | `SpeedAndLocationDevice.java`, `LiveWorkoutSession.java`, `TrackerService.java`, `WorkoutSummariesDatabaseManager.java`, `WorkoutDataMapper.kt` | `TST-MAP-022` | Verified |
| **REQ-UI-123** | **Localized Cluster Naming Interface.** | The cluster candidate naming dialog (`ClusterNamingDialog`) SHALL render all user-facing text labels (titles, route assignment prompts, task counters, field labels, and button actions) using localized string resources. Zero hardcoded string literals SHALL remain in the composable layout. **Invariants**: Formatted placeholders (date strings, task numbers) MUST follow valid Java Formatter syntax across all 9 supported locales. | `ImportBackupTabsScreen.kt`, `strings.xml` | `TST-UI-078` | Verified |

| **REQ-PRI-001** | **Permission Transparency.** | The system SHALL provide transparent information about location and background permissions to comply with Android standards and build user trust. | `MainActivityWithNavigation.java`| `TST-MAN-001` | Verified |
